Across several few-shot medical imaging benchmarks, C2I-guided generation improves downstream accuracy and robustness over diffusion-based augmentation baselines, showing that synthetic augmentation is most effective when guided by task usefulness rather than image quality alone.

</details>


### [104] [Self in Space: Benchmarking Self-Awareness and Spatial Cognition in UAV Embodied Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12477)
*Zhishan Zou,Guoyan Sun,Zhiwei Wei,Jiancheng Pan,Yujie Li,Mugen Peng,Wenjia Xu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出SIS-Bench基准，用于评估无人机场景下以"自我在空间中"统一形式化表述的具身空间智能，揭示了当前多模态大语言模型在空间认知与自我意识之间的不平衡，并探索了基于运动感知的表征来提升自我意识能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有无人机相关方法和基准主要关注环境中心的空间理解任务，智能体的自我意识仍然隐式存在。为填补这一空白，本文提出SIS-Bench，在统一的"自我在空间中"（self-in-space）框架下评估具身空间智能。

Method: SIS-Bench沿"空间"与"自我"两个互补维度，以及感知、记忆、推理三个认知层次组织评估，包含从1,646个真实世界无人机视频经任务条件化构建管道和专家验证得到的4,856个问答对，涵盖13个任务。进一步探索基于光流和视觉特征融合的运动感知表征。

Result: 当前MLLM在建模动态和以智能体为中心的过程中存在根本性局限，空间认知与自我意识之间存在明显不平衡，且随认知层次提升性能逐步下降。运动感知表征一致提升了感知和记忆性能，并泛化到下游无人机决策任务。

Conclusion: 自我意识对于推进具身空间智能至关重要，本文提供了新基准和运动感知自我在空间建模的实证证据。

Abstract: Autonomous UAV systems increasingly rely on multimodal large language models (MLLMs) to operate in complex real-world environments. Such embodied scenarios require not only understanding the surrounding space but also maintaining a coherent representation of the agent itself. However, existing UAV-oriented approaches and benchmarks remain largely environment-centric, primarily focusing on spatial understanding tasks, with the agent's self-awareness remaining implicit. To address this gap, we introduce SIS-Bench, a benchmark for evaluating embodied spatial intelligence in UAV scenarios under a unified self-in-space formulation. SIS-Bench organizes evaluation along two complementary dimensions, space and self, and a three-level hierarchy of perception, memory, and reasoning. It contains 4,856 question--answer pairs across 13 tasks derived from 1,646 real-world UAV videos through a task-conditioned construction pipeline with expert verification. Extensive evaluations reveal that current MLLMs exhibit fundamental limitations in modeling dynamic and agent-centered processes. In particular, we observe a clear imbalance between spatial cognition and self-awareness, as well as a progressive performance degradation across cognitive levels. Motivated by these findings, we further explore a motion-aware representation that incorporates self-related dynamics through optical flow and visual feature fusion. Experimental results show that modeling agent motion consistently improves perception and memory performance, not only in spatial cognition but also in self-awareness, and generalizes to downstream UAV decision-making tasks. Our results highlight the importance of self-awareness for advancing embodied spatial intelligence, and provide both a new benchmark and empirical evidence for motion-aware self-in-space modeling.

</details>


### [105] [RealSkin: Spatio-Spectral Partial Neural Adjoint Maps for Image-to-3D Attribute Transfer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12495)
*Jing Li,Yawei Luo,Xiangze Meng,Ying Li,Tieru Wu,Rui Ma*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: RealSkin是一个自监督框架，通过学习谱域中的对应优化，结合空间引导的配准和谱感知神经伴随网络，解决了从真实图像到合成3D表面的视觉属性迁移中存在的分辨率不匹配、点对应离散性、近等距假设和拓扑不一致等挑战，在真实到合成场景中达到了最先进性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 创建逼真3D资产需要弥合真实观测与合成模型之间的外观差距，现有方法受限于分辨率不匹配、点对应的离散性以及近等距假设和拓扑一致性要求，无法有效处理真实到合成场景中的严重拓扑差异和非等距变形。

Method: 提出RealSkin框架，包含两个核心模块：(1) 空间引导的配准算法，在严重拓扑差异下建立粗略对应；(2) 谱感知神经伴随网络，将部分对应融入神经函数空间并建模非等距残差，实现对应精化。

Result: 在具有挑战性的真实到合成场景中，该方法达到了最先进的性能。

Conclusion: 通过自监督学习策略在谱域中优化对应关系，同时摆脱了严格的等距假设和拓扑一致性约束，有效实现了真实到合成的视觉属性迁移。

Abstract: Creating photorealistic 3D assets requires bridging the appearance gap between real-world observations and synthetic models. A promising approach is to transfer visual attributes from real images onto synthetic 3D surfaces. Traditional methods struggle with resolution mismatch and the inherent discreteness of point correspondences. In contrast, resolution-robust functional maps enable smooth attribute propagation but rely on near-isometry assumptions and topological consistency. To address these limitations, we propose RealSkin, a self-supervised framework that performs correspondence optimization in a learned spectral domain, guided by spatial correspondences. We first introduce a spatial-guided registration algorithm to establish coarse correspondences under severe topological discrepancies. To relax strict isometric assumptions and handle partial correspondences, we further design a spectral-aware neural adjoint network that incorporates partial correspondences into a neural function space and models non-isometric residuals for correspondence refinement. Experimental results demonstrate that our method achieves state-of-the-art performance on challenging real-to-synthetic scenarios. The code will be publicly released.

</details>


### [106] [TerraLogic: A Benchmark for Hierarchical Geospatial Reasoning in Earth Observation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12497)
*Yuhang Yan,Linchao Mou,Bokang Yang,Qingyu Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 TerraLogic，一个包含545个层次化地理空间推理任务的基准数据集，以及 HieraPlan，一种具备工具层级组织和容错推理能力的工具增强型智能体。实验表明现有方法在层次化地理空间推理上表现不足，而 HieraPlan 在推理能力、跨模态泛化和错误处理方面均建立了强基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有遥感研究主要聚焦于感知导向任务，而认知层面的地理空间推理（如灾害评估、城市热岛分析、森林破碎化动态）仍未被充分探索。

Method: 构建 TerraLogic 基准（545个场景驱动、层次感知的任务，涵盖光学、SAR、红外影像），并提出 HieraPlan 智能体：将工具包组织为功能层次结构，并实现容错推理机制，支持结构化抽象、工具故障恢复和稳定长程规划。

Result: 当前方法在层次化地理空间推理上表现不佳；HieraPlan 在推理准确性、跨模态泛化能力和错误鲁棒性上均优于现有基线。

Conclusion: TerraLogic 填补了遥感认知推理评估的空白，HieraPlan 为层次化地理空间推理提供了有效解决方案，数据集和代码已开源。

Abstract: Beyond perception, reasoning is essential in remote sensing for advanced interpretation, inference, and decision-making. Recent advances in large language models (LLMs) have enabled tool-augmented agents that leverage external tools to perform complex analytical tasks. However, existing studies in remote sensing primarily focus on perception-oriented tasks, leaving cognitive geospatial reasoning largely underexplored. To address this gap, we introduce TerraLogic, a benchmark for geospatial reasoning. TerraLogic comprises 545 scenario-driven, hierarchy-aware tasks, such as hazard vulnerability assessment, urban heat island analysis, and forest fragmentation dynamics, spanning optical, Synthetic Aperture Radar (SAR), and infrared (IR) imagery. It advances evaluation beyond recognition and monitoring toward cognitive-level geospatial analysis. To facilitate evaluation on TerraLogic, we further propose HieraPlan, a tool-augmented agent that organizes toolkits into functional hierarchies and performs fault-tolerant reasoning. HieraPlan enables structured abstraction, robust recovery from tool failures, and stable long-horizon planning. Extensive experiments demonstrate that current approaches struggle with hierarchical geospatial reasoning, while HieraPlan provides a strong baseline with improved reasoning, cross-modal generalization, and error handling. The dataset and agent code are publicly available at https://github.com/Ireliya/TerraLogic.

</details>


### [107] [DynTrace: Tracking Dynamic Object Evidence for 4D Spatio-Temporal Reasoning in MLLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12503)
*Rongxin Gao,Yuzhi Huang,Dongxuan Liu,Chu Li,Zhenye Wang,Jie Wu,Shuzhao Xie,Jingyan Jiang,Xinghao Ding,Xiaotong Tu,Yue Huang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: DynTrace是一个无需训练的4D时空推理框架，通过动态轨迹可视化（DTV）和动态轨迹令牌（DT-Token）两个互补组件，为多模态大模型提供连续的动态目标证据，在多个基准上达到最优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MLLMs在连续动态场景感知方面存在显著局限，尤其是在跟踪动态目标证据以实现连贯的4D时空推理方面表现不佳。这主要是因为模型依赖稀疏的帧级观测，导致连续动态线索被碎片化，且无法区分目标的真实动态与相机运动引起的表观运动。

Method: DynTrace包含两个互补组件：(1) 动态轨迹可视化（DTV）将世界坐标轨迹重投影到图像平面，提供几何信息引导的视觉先验，以区分真实动态与相机引起的表观运动；(2) 动态轨迹令牌（DT-Token）组织成动态轨迹图（DTG），跟踪目标级动态线索、轨迹演化和关键时刻，维持连续的动态目标证据。

Result: DynTrace在Dyn-Bench、VLM4D和DSI-Bench三个基准上均取得最优结果，显著提升了开源MLLMs的性能。

Conclusion: 持续跟踪动态目标证据对于稳健的4D时空推理至关重要，DynTrace通过几何信息引导的视觉先验和结构化的时空轨迹有效解决了当前模型的局限。

Abstract: 4D spatio-temporal reasoning, jointly modeling 3D spatial structure and temporal evolution, is essential for understanding dynamic worlds and enabling embodied interaction. While current Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) show strong capabilities in static scene understanding and coarse-grained 4D tasks, they still have notable limitations in continuous dynamic scene perception, especially in tracking dynamic object evidence for coherent 4D spatio-temporal reasoning. This shortcoming stems mainly from relying on sparse frame-level observations, fragmenting continuous dynamic cues and leaving models unable to disentangle genuine object dynamics from camera-induced apparent motion. Inspired by humans tracking dynamic cues while compensating for viewpoint changes, we propose DynTrace, a training-free framework for 4D spatio-temporal reasoning with two complementary components. Dynamic Trajectory Visualization (DTV) reprojects world-coordinate trajectories onto the image plane, providing geometry-informed visual priors that disentangle genuine object dynamics from camera-induced apparent motion. Meanwhile, the Dynamic Trace Token (DT-Token), organized into a Dynamic Trace Graph (DTG), tracks object-level dynamic cues, trace evolution, and key moments, maintaining continuous dynamic object evidence for coherent 4D reasoning. Together, these two components equip MLLMs with continuously tracked dynamic object evidence, grounded in geometry-informed visual priors and structured spatio-temporal traces. DynTrace consistently improves open-source MLLMs, achieving state-of-the-art results on Dyn-Bench, VLM4D, and DSI-Bench, validating the importance of tracking dynamic object evidence for robust 4D spatio-temporal reasoning.

</details>


### [108] [DiTailed: Ensuring Visual Object Consistency in Text-Image-to-Image Flow Matching Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12539)
*Francesco Taioli,Daniel Coelho,Iaroslav Melekhov,Roberto Alcover-Couso,Jose Miguel Grande Saiz,Virginia Fernandez Arguedas,Artur Bekasov*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文针对文本引导图像编辑中的视觉对象一致性问题，提出了ABO-Edit数据集、发现编辑流程中条件嵌入空间的可预测特性，并基于此设计了无需参数修改的FlowMirror辅助损失函数，有效提升了编辑质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文本引导图像编辑模型在编辑过程中难以保持被编辑对象的关键属性不变（即视觉对象一致性问题），该问题尚未得到充分研究和解决。

Method: 1）构建了ABO-Edit数据集（12,000+三元组，含多视角和人工验证）；2）发现整流流模型中条件嵌入空间虽未受直接监督，但在高噪声水平下已编码了最终生成图像的预测信息；3）提出FlowMirror，一种无需参数修改的辅助损失函数，对该条件嵌入空间进行监督。

Result: FlowMirror在多个评估指标上优于基线方法，且无需改变模型架构。

Conclusion: 利用条件嵌入空间的隐式预测能力设计辅助损失，是一种轻量级且有效的提升图像编辑一致性的途径。

Abstract: Despite remarkable progress in text-guided image editing, generative models frequently fail to preserve visual object consistency, defined as the preservation of a subject's key attributes throughout the editing process. We address this limitation through three contributions. First, we introduce ABO-Edit, a dataset specifically designed to study object consistency, comprising over 12,000 triplets of source images, editing prompts, and high-quality target images rendered from artist-designed 3D assets, with multi-view coverage and human-verified quality control. Second, we uncover an overlooked property of image-editing rectified flow models: the conditioning embedding space, not directly supervised during training, encodes a prediction of the final generated image even at high noise levels. Third, exploiting this finding, we propose FlowMirror, a parameter-free auxiliary loss that supervises this conditioning embedding space. Without architectural changes, our method improves generation quality across several metrics over baselines.

</details>


### [109] [Edge-Aware Thermal Infrared UAV Swarm Tracking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12544)
*Yu-Hsi Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 针对热红外环境下无人机集群跟踪中的小目标、遮挡和机动问题，本文提出基于自适应运动学卡尔曼滤波（AKKF）的边缘感知在线跟踪流水线，在保持实时效率的同时改进轨迹连续性，并在BSB基准上联合评估跟踪性能与计算效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有TIR无人机跟踪方法优先考虑精度，忽视了边缘设备实时部署的计算约束；标准KF虽高效但恒速假设在动态运动与传感器抖动下失效，而非线性估计器则计算成本过高。

Method: 提出自适应运动学卡尔曼滤波（AKKF），在线性KF中引入状态相关运动学建模，结合瞬态假阳性抑制和运动学驱动预测滑行（predictive coasting），构建边缘感知在线跟踪流水线。

Result: 在Beyond Strong Baseline（BSB）基准上评估，联合考量跟踪性能与计算效率，验证了AKKF在挑战性TIR条件下改善轨迹连续性的效果。

Conclusion: AKKF在保持实时边缘部署效率的同时，有效提升了动态TIR场景下的跟踪鲁棒性，为未来真实环境下的实时部署提供了方向。

Abstract: Thermal infrared (TIR) imaging is essential for UAV swarm operations in visually degraded environments. However, tracking tiny UAVs remains challenging due to limited appearance cues, frequent occlusions, and rapid maneuvers. Despite significant progress driven by benchmarks such as the Anti-UAV challenge, existing methods primarily prioritize accuracy while overlooking the computational constraints of real-time edge deployment. The standard Kalman Filter (KF) offers the efficiency required for edge devices, yet its constant-velocity assumption often breaks down under highly dynamic UAV motion and thermal sensor jitter. More sophisticated nonlinear estimators can improve robustness but often introduce additional computational costs. To address this gap, we propose an edge-aware online tracking pipeline centered on the Adaptive Kinematic Kalman Filter (AKKF), which augments the linear KF with state-dependent kinematic modeling while preserving real-time efficiency. Combined with transient false-positive suppression and kinematics-driven predictive coasting, the presented pipeline improves trajectory continuity under challenging TIR conditions. Experiments on the Beyond Strong Baseline (BSB) benchmark provide a starting point for edge-aware UAV tracking by jointly evaluating tracking performance and computational efficiency, offering insights toward future real-time deployment.

</details>


### [110] [CGRL: Concept-Guided Pruning and Representation Learning for Whole-Slide Image Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12556)
*Thuc Huynh,Tuan Le,Doanh C. Bui*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出概念引导的剪枝与表示学习（CGRL）框架，通过引入类别级概念原型，在弱监督全切片图像分类中实现基于概念相关性的补丁剪枝和对比表示学习，提升多种MIL方法的性能并降低计算成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MIL方法主要依赖视觉线索聚合大量补丁嵌入，导致大量非信息性补丁被保留，且实例特征与类别级疾病语义之间的对齐较弱。引入类别级概念原型可提供有效的先验知识，改善补丁选择与表示学习。

Method: 1) 概念相关性剪枝：计算补丁实例与类别概念原型的相似度，保留Top-K个概念相关补丁用于后续MIL聚合；2) 概念引导的对比表示学习：基于相同相似度矩阵构建类别正负补丁集，优化目标类、对称辅助类和跨类分离三个对比目标，规范化投影概念空间。

Result: 在TCGA-BRCA和TCGA-NSCLC数据集上使用多种MIL方法进行评估，CGRL在多个模型-数据集组合上均有提升，在准确率和macro-F1上表现尤为明显，同时通过概念相关性剪枝降低了计算成本。

Conclusion: 类别级语义概念为弱监督计算病理学中的补丁选择和表示学习提供了有效且实用的先验知识。

Abstract: Weakly supervised whole-slide image (WSI) classification is widely used in computational pathology because slide-level labels are easier to obtain than dense region annotations. Existing multiple instance learning (MIL) methods often aggregate large bags of patch embeddings using mainly visual cues, which can retain many non-informative patches and provide weak alignment between instance features and class-level disease semantics. We propose Concept-Guided Pruning and Representation Learning (CGRL), a simple framework that introduces class-level concept prototypes derived from disease prompts into the MIL pipeline. First, concept-relevance pruning ranks patch instances by their similarity to class concepts and retains the top-K concept-relevant patches for downstream MIL aggregation. Second, concept-guided contrastive representation learning constructs class-wise positive and negative patch sets from the same similarity matrix and optimizes target-class, symmetric auxiliary, and cross-class separation objectives, thereby regularizing the projected concept space. We evaluate CGRL on TCGA-BRCA and TCGA-NSCLC using multiple representative MIL methods. Experimental results show that CGRL improves several model-dataset combinations, with gains depending on the downstream MIL model and dataset. It achieves particularly clear improvements in accuracy and macro-F1 while reducing computational cost through concept-relevance pruning. These findings demonstrate that class-level semantic concepts provide an effective and practical prior for patch selection and representation learning in weakly supervised computational pathology.

</details>


### [111] [Gaussian Mixture Modeling for Event-Aware Visual Allocation in Long Video Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12557)
*Yifan Lu,Ziqi Zhang,Chunfeng Yuan,Jun Gao,Bing Li,Weiming Hu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: GMM-EVA利用高斯混合模型对视频进行事件级结构建模，通过差异化分配视觉预算（每个事件保留一个高分辨率关键帧和多个低分辨率辅助帧），在无需训练的条件下实现高效长视频理解，仅用约一半的视觉token预算即可达到与基线方法相当的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LVLMs在长视频理解中面临均匀采样的计算开销大和信息丢失问题，现有关键帧选择方法将视频帧视为原子实体并平均分配视觉预算，忽略了高层语义结构，导致大量冗余。

Method: 利用高斯混合模型从离散帧观测中建模事件级结构，采用差异化分配策略：每个事件保留一张高分辨率主关键帧以保留细节，同时使用低分辨率辅助帧维持时序上下文，优化token预算。该方法为无需训练、即插即用框架。

Result: 在多个长视频基准测试中显著优于均匀采样，在仅使用约一半视觉token预算的情况下达到与基线选择方法相当的性能。

Conclusion: GMM-EVA通过事件感知的差异化视觉分配策略，在保持性能的同时大幅提升效率，且具有良好的跨相关性度量和下游LVLM的泛化性。

Abstract: Large Vision-Language Models (LVLMs) face significant challenges in long video understanding due to the excessive computational cost and information loss associated with uniform sampling. Existing keyframe selection methods often treat video frames as atomic entities and allocate visual budgets equally, thereby overlooking high-level semantic structures and introducing substantial redundancy. To address these limitations, we propose GMM-EVA (Gaussian Mixture Modeling for Event-Aware Visual Allocation), which leverages Gaussian Mixture Models to model event-level structure from discrete frame-wise observations. A differentiated allocation strategy is then applied to preserve one primary high-resolution keyframe per event for high-fidelity detail, while utilizing lower-resolution secondary keyframes to maintain temporal context and optimize token budgets. GMM-EVA is a training-free, plug-and-play framework that generalizes robustly across various relevance measures and downstream LVLMs. Extensive experiments on multiple long video benchmarks demonstrate that our method significantly outperforms uniform sampling. Notably, GMM-EVA achieves comparable performance to baseline selection methods while utilizing only approximately half of the visual token budget, highlighting its superior efficiency and effectiveness.

</details>


### [112] [Traceback Translators Against Forgetting in Continual Fake Speech Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12569)
*Enrico Gottardis,Mattia Tamiazzo,Simone Milani*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种基于域翻译器的抗遗忘伪造语音检测方法，通过回溯翻译网络将新特征空间映射回原始特征空间，在冻结检测器上实现持续学习，既保持对旧数据的高检测精度，又降低计算开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着生成模型不断进步，伪造语音检测器面临新的数据分布挑战。传统的持续学习虽然能更新模型，但会导致灾难性遗忘，即在新数据上表现提升的同时对旧样本检测能力下降。因此需要一种既能适应新数据又不遗忘旧知识的检测方案。

Method: 在冻结的伪造语音检测器上引入回溯翻译网络（Traceback Translator Network），该网络充当域翻译器，将新特征空间映射回原始特征空间，从而使原始检测器无需重新训练即可处理新数据分布。

Result: 实验表明，该方法相比传统重训练策略，在实现高检测率的同时，显著降低了计算开销，并保持了在旧数据上的检测精度。

Conclusion: 基于域翻译器的抗遗忘策略是一种有效的伪造语音检测持续学习方案，能够在适应新数据的同时有效缓解灾难性遗忘问题。

Abstract: Fake speech detectors are increasingly challenged by the development of new and more accurate generative models. To cope with this problem, continual learning techniques are nowadays widely considered feasible strategies for updating models to new datasets, but they also lead to decreased performance on previously seen samples (catastrophic forgetting). In this work, we propose a forgetting-resilient solution based on the adoption of domain translators within a frozen detector, which remaps the new feature spaces into the original ones by means of a traceback translator network. Experimental results show that this strategy enables the achievement of high detection rates with respect to traditional retraining, while minimizing the computational effort and preserving the detection accuracy on previous data.

</details>


### [113] [WanToFight: Real-Time Generative Game Engine for Multi-Player Combat Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12592)
*Li Hu,Guangyuan Wang,Peng Zhang,Bang Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: WanToFight 是一个基于 Wan-1.3B 视频扩散Transformer的生成式游戏引擎，实现了《拳皇'97》双人实时对战模拟。它通过流式自回归生成器、视觉锚定玩家关联模块和门控键盘注入模块，结合DMD蒸馏和剪枝VAE解码器，在RTX 5090上以30FPS运行，首次实现了多人控制、实时推理、复杂物理交互和对抗性游戏的统一。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有生成式游戏引擎仅支持单人第一人称或非实时协作场景，无法同时处理多人控制、实时推理、复杂物理交互和对抗性游戏这四方面挑战。

Method: 基于Wan-1.3B视频扩散Transformer构建三个组件：(1) 流式自回归生成器，采用块因果注意力和滚动KV缓存；(2) 视觉锚定玩家关联模块，将键盘信号绑定到角色身份；(3) 门控局部因果键盘注入模块，通过单人到完整对战的课程学习训练。使用四步DMD蒸馏学生模型和剪枝VAE解码器实现30FPS推理。

Result: 在单张NVIDIA RTX 5090上以512×384分辨率、30FPS运行整个完整对局，实现了实时双人对抗游戏模拟。

Conclusion: WanToFight是首个同时实现多人控制、实时推理、复杂物理交互和对抗性游戏的生成式游戏引擎。

Abstract: We present WanToFight, a generative game engine that simulates real-time, two-player The King of Fighters '97 (KOF~'97) gameplay from keyboard input. Prior generative game engines target either single-player first-person settings or non-real-time cooperative scenarios; multi-player control, real-time inference, complex physical interaction, and adversarial gameplay have not been jointly addressed. WanToFight closes this gap with three components built on the Wan-1.3B video diffusion transformer: a streaming autoregressive generator with block-causal attention and a rolling KV cache; a visually grounded Player Association module that binds each player's keyboard signal to a character identity; and a gated, locally causal keyboard injection module trained with a single-player-to-full-gameplay curriculum. A four-step DMD-distilled student paired with a pruned VAE decoder sustains 30FPS at 512x384 on a single NVIDIA RTX 5090 over the duration of a complete match. To our knowledge, WanToFight is the first generative game engine to combine multi-player control, real-time inference, complex physical interaction, and adversarial gameplay in one system.

</details>


### [114] [Decouple and Reason: Anatomically Guided Two-Stage Voxel-Level Grounding of Free-Text Findings in 3D Chest CT](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12602)
*Kwang-Hyun Uhm,Inhwa Son,Sung-Jea Ko*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种解耦框架DAGG，将3D胸部CT中自由文本发现的体素级定位分解为两个专门阶段：类别无关的病灶分割和文本-体积推理，通过引入解剖学引导（空间坐标和肺叶先验）解决空间歧义，在ReXGroundingCT基准上达到SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有端到端方法难以同时学习3D分割所需的局部特征表示和文本对齐所需的复杂语义理解，导致定位性能次优。

Method: 提出解耦框架，将问题分解为：(1) 类别无关的病灶分割阶段，提取候选子体积；(2) 文本-体积推理阶段，进行跨模态对齐，并引入解剖学引导（相对空间坐标和肺叶先验）解决局部空间歧义。

Result: 在ReXGroundingCT基准上达到官方排行榜最优的整体定位质量。

Conclusion: 将检测与推理解耦是处理3D医学视觉定位复杂性的有效范式。

Abstract: Automatic voxel-level grounding of free-text findings in 3D chest Computed Tomography (CT) is critical for clinical interpretability. However, this task remains highly challenging due to the intricate spatial complexity of large 3D volumes and the heterogeneity of free-text findings. Existing end-to-end approaches often struggle to simultaneously learn the localized feature representations required for accurate 3D segmentation and the complex semantic understanding needed for text alignment, leading to suboptimal grounding performance. To overcome this fundamental limitation, we propose a novel decoupled framework that disentangles the problem into two specialized stages: (1) class-agnostic lesion segmentation and (2) text-volume reasoning. This structural separation allows the model to first extract candidate sub-volumes by localizing potential abnormalities. Subsequently, intensive cross-modal reasoning is performed to align these localized sub-volumes with free-text medical findings. To resolve the spatial ambiguities inherent in local regions, the reasoning module is augmented with explicit anatomical guidance, utilizing relative spatial coordinates and lung lobe priors. Evaluated on the ReXGroundingCT benchmark, our method achieves state-of-the-art performance in overall grounding quality on the official leaderboard. These results demonstrate that decoupling detection from reasoning is a highly effective paradigm for handling the complexity of 3D medical visual grounding. Our code is publicly available at https://github.com/khuhm/DAGG.

</details>


### [115] [Towards Vision-Free CIR: Attribute-Augmented Scoring and LLM-Based Reranking for Zero-Shot Composed Image Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12621)
*Ryotaro Shimada,Yu-Chieh Lin,Yuji Nozawa,Youyang Ng,Osamu Torii,Yusuke Matsui*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种无需视觉模型的组合图像检索（CIR）框架，通过属性增强混合评分和基于LLM的重排序两大技术，在CIRR数据集上以44.04%的R@1超越现有零样本方法（+8.79%），在FashionIQ上揭示了语义推理与细粒度视觉匹配之间的权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索"无视觉"范式（将图像表示为文本）是否能有效处理更复杂的多模态任务——组合图像检索（CIR），以解决文本描述固有的信息丢失问题。

Method: 提出Vision-Free CIR框架，包含两个关键技术：(1) 属性增强混合评分（Attribute-Augmented Hybrid Scoring），通过显式属性匹配补偿丢失的视觉细节；(2) 基于LLM的重排序（LLM-Based Reranking），验证候选结果的语义一致性。

Result: 在开放域CIRR数据集上，该方法以44.04%的R@1超越现有零样本CIR方法（提升+8.79%）。在FashionIQ上揭示了语义推理与细粒度视觉匹配之间的权衡。消融实验验证了两个组件均能持续提升性能。

Conclusion: Vision-Free范式在组合图像检索任务上是可行的，但不同领域（如开放域与时尚域）在语义推理和细粒度视觉匹配之间存在性能权衡，属性增强和LLM重排序是有效的补偿策略。

Abstract: Recent work has shown that "Vision-Free'' approaches (representing images as text) can be effective for standard image retrieval tasks. However, it remains unclear whether this paradigm can effectively handle a more complex, multimodal task, Composed Image Retrieval (CIR), due to the inherent information loss in textual descriptions. In this paper, we introduce a Vision-Free CIR framework that addresses this challenge through two key techniques: (1) Attribute-Augmented Hybrid Scoring, which compensates for lost visual details via explicit attribute matching, and (2) LLM-Based Reranking, which verifies semantic consistency of top candidates. Experiments on the open-domain CIRR dataset show that our approach outperforms existing Zero-shot CIR methods (44.04% R@1, +8.79%). On FashionIQ, our results highlight the trade-off between semantic reasoning and fine-grained visual matching. Ablation studies reveal that both attribute-augmented scoring and LLM-Based Reranking consistently improve performance.

</details>


### [116] [MAGE: Color-Invariant and Spatial Knowledge Distillation for Gastric Neoplasm Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12663)
*Jiho Jun,Jeongwon Woo,Jaemin Song,Thanh Bong Nguyen,Dong-heon Yeon,Donghoon Kang,Jae-Myung Park,Sung-Jea Ko,Kwang-Hyun Uhm*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出MAGE框架，通过掩膜消色引导的局部专家分支和双目标蒸馏策略，使模型学习基于形态结构特征而非纹理偏倚进行胃腺瘤与癌的鉴别分类，显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 胃镜图像中腺瘤与癌的区分因类间相似度高、边界模糊而极具挑战；现有ROI分类方法受检测/分割误差传播和全局上下文缺失困扰，全图分类缺乏空间聚焦，且深度神经网络容易依赖于出血、光晕等纹理偏倚而非内在形态特征进行预测。

Method: 提出MAGE框架：1）训练时引入局部专家分支，输入为掩膜消色视图（抑制背景和颜色），迫使分支学习纯结构特征；2）采用双目标蒸馏策略，将分类logits和空间注意力图从专家分支转移至主分支，实现隐式空间监督；3）推理时无需标注掩膜，可实时部署。

Result: 在临床胃镜数据集上显著优于YOLO、Swin-Transformer等检测和分类方法，不仅分类性能更优，还能生成可解释的注意力图以提升临床可信度。

Conclusion: 通过消色掩膜引导与知识蒸馏，有效迫使模型基于形态特征而非纹理捷径进行预测，兼顾了颜色线索的保留与部署效率。

Abstract: Accurate differentiation between gastric adenoma and carcinoma during endoscopy is critical for clinical decision-making. Yet, this task is highly challenging due to high inter-class similarity and ambiguous boundaries between the two classes. Existing ROI-based classification methods often suffer from detection/segmentation error propagation and loss of surrounding global context. In contrast, full-image classification lacks the necessary spatial focus. Furthermore, we observe that deep neural networks gravitate towards domain-specific texture biases(e.g. bleeding, lighting artifacts), often causing models to predict based on spurious correlations instead of intrinsic morphological features. To address these limitations, we propose a novel framework, Masked Achromatic Guidance Expert (MAGE). During training, we introduce an auxiliary local expert branch trained on masked achromatic views of the neoplasm. By suppressing background context and color, this branch is forced to learn highly discriminative, purely structural features. We then employ a dual-objective distillation strategy, transferring both classification logits and spatial attention maps to provide implicit spatial supervision to the main branch that receives full WLI as input. This dual-objective distillation forces the model to ground its predictions in morphology rather than relying on shortcuts, while still retaining clinically relevant color cues. At inference time, our deployable model operates on images without annotated masks, ensuring real-time deployability . Extensive experiments on a clinical gastric endoscopy dataset show that our method significantly outperforms existing detection-based methodologies (e.g. YOLO) and classification-based methodologies (e.g. Swin-Transformer), providing not only superior classification performance but also interpretable attention maps for clinical reliability.

</details>


### [117] [Text-Aided Multi-Modal Panoptic Symbol Spotting for CAD Floor Plan Drawings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12678)
*Yan Gong,Bohao Li,Bowen Du,Junchen Ye*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: TextCAD提出了一种多模态框架，通过联合建模图形基元和文本注释，并设计类型-属性关联编码器（TACE）和语义层次对齐机制（含多级语义过滤MSF），在建筑CAD图纸的全局符号检测任务上取得了最先进的结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有CAD符号检测方法主要基于图形基元，忽视了文本注释的语义价值；即使少数文本感知方法也未能充分建模注释的复杂句法和层次化语义，导致语义丢失和检测性能欠佳。

Method: 提出TextCAD多模态框架：1) 类型-属性关联编码器(TACE)联合建模注释的类型和属性以编码组合语义；2) 语义层次对齐框架通过多级语义过滤(MSF)和基元下采样，在不同语义层级上自适应对齐注释语义与图形基元，实现跨模态语义注入与融合。

Result: 在真实建筑设计方案数据集上，TextCAD有效提升了符号检测性能，达到了最先进水平。

Conclusion: 联合建模CAD图形基元和文本注释的语义信息，尤其是通过显式编码注释的组合语义和层级化对齐，能够显著提升CAD符号检测的准确性。

Abstract: Computer-Aided Design (CAD) floor plan drawings contain both graphical primitives and textual annotations, which provide complementary geometric and semantic cues for intelligent design understanding. Among CAD analysis tasks, panoptic symbol spotting has become increasingly important with the growing demand for industrial digitalization and deep learning-based automation. However, most existing methods remain primarily primitive-centric and underexploit textual annotations, despite their critical semantic value. Even the few text-aware approaches often treat annotations only superficially, without properly modeling complex syntax and hierarchical semantics of CAD annotations, which leads to semantic loss and suboptimal spotting performance. To address these limitations, we propose TextCAD, a multimodal framework that jointly models graphical primitives and textual annotations for panoptic symbol spotting. Specifically, we design a Type-Attribute Correlation Encoder (TACE) to explicitly encode the compositional semantics within annotations by jointly modeling their types and attributes. We further introduce a Semantic Hierarchy Alignment framework with Multi-level Semantic Filtering (MSF) and primitive downsampling, which adaptively aligns annotation semantics with graphical primitives at different semantic levels and enables accurate cross-modal semantic injection and fusion. Experiments on real-world building-design datasets show that TextCAD effectively improves symbol spotting performance and achieves state-of-the-art results.

</details>


### [118] [ReflectVLN: Training Vision-Language Navigation Agents with Reflective Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12680)
*Jiahang Wang,Yirong Yang,Yanqing Zhu,Minghua Luo,Shichao Xie,Fei Liu,Mu Xu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ReflectVLN 提出了一种基于双向交互意图与执行代理的视觉语言导航框架，通过闭环比对语义进展、诊断执行失败并从错误累积中恢复，引入 Action-CoT 实现可解释的中间推理，在标准基准上提升了成功率和路径效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 VLN 方法缺乏显式的闭环比对机制来跟踪语义进展、诊断执行失败并从长程导航中的错误累积中恢复。

Method: 提出 ReflectVLN 框架，包含双向交互的意图代理（进行子任务分解与反思）和执行代理（将子任务落地为短时动作并监控进度与偏离）；引入 Action-CoT（动作链式思维），一种路径条件的双查询训练方案，用于生成时间连贯且可解释的中间推理。

Result: 在标准 VLN 基准上，ReflectVLN 在有限数据预算下提升了成功率和路径效率，训练成本低，推理时高层意图调用次数少，同时提供可解释的中间决策。

Conclusion: 双向闭环通信机制能有效提升 VLN 在长程导航中的鲁棒性和可解释性，且具有较好的训练和推理效率。

Abstract: Existing vision-language navigation methods often couple a VLM with waypoint decoders to produce multi-step action plans, but they typically lack an explicit closed-loop mechanism for tracking semantic progress, diagnosing execution failures, and recovering from error accumulation in long-horizon navigation. To address this gap, we propose ReflectVLN, an agentic VLN framework that organizes decision-making through bidirectionally interactive intention and execution agents. The intention agent performs subtask decomposition and reflection, generating executable subtask descriptions as corrective plans. Conditioned on these descriptions, the execution agent grounds them into short-horizon actions under current observations while monitoring sub-goal progress and detecting off-track behavior. Crucially, ReflectVLN enables closed-loop bidirectional communication: the execution agent emits progress and deviation signals to trigger reflection and subtask updates on demand, and the intention agent returns structured guidance that reconditions subsequent actions for recovery. To encourage temporally coherent decisions with interpretable intermediate rationales, we introduce Action Chain-of-Thought (Action-CoT), a path-conditioned dual-query training scheme for action generation. Experiments on standard VLN benchmarks show that ReflectVLN improves success rates and path efficiency under a constrained data budget, with favorable training cost and fewer high-level intention calls at inference time, while providing interpretable intermediate decisions for analysis and collaboration. Code is available at: https://github.com/AIprogrammer/ReflectVLN

</details>


### [119] [MambaPSA: A Mamba-based Replacement for C2PSA in YOLO26](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12681)
*Sheng-Wei Chan,Chia-Min Lin,Hsin-Jui Pan,Ching-Yu Tsai,Chih-Hsiang Yang,Yung-Che Wang,Jen-Shiun Chiang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出MambaPSA，将状态空间模型Mamba集成到YOLO26目标检测框架中，通过在骨干网络末端替换C2PSA模块和在颈部添加双向Vision Mamba模块，实现了更轻量化的模型。实验表明：MambaPSA参数量减少2.9%、FLOPs降低12.1%、CPU推理速度提升17.6%，精度几乎无损；P4层BiViM模块可带来最高0.9 mAP50:95的精度提升。SSM在无NMS轻量检测器中提供了良好的效率-精度权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索状态空间模型Mamba作为自注意力机制的高效替代方案，将其集成到最新的无NMS目标检测框架YOLO26中，以期在保持精度的同时实现模型轻量化和计算效率提升。

Method: 提出MambaPSA模块替换YOLO26骨干网络末端的C2PSA模块，并在颈部P3、P4、P5层级插入双向Vision Mamba模块，构建混合架构以替代原始基于注意力的模块。

Result: MambaPSA在PASCAL VOC 2007+2012上参数量降低2.9%，FLOPs降低12.1%，CPU推理吞吐量从17FPS提升至20FPS（提升17.6%），mAP50:95仅下降0.1；P4层BiViM模块带来最佳精度增益+0.9 mAP50:95。

Conclusion: SSM在无NMS轻量级检测器中能够提供比注意力模块更优的效率-精度权衡，验证了Mamba在目标检测任务中的实用价值。

Abstract: State space models (SSMs), notably Mamba, have recently emerged as efficient alternatives to self-attention with linear computational complexity. We investigate the integration of Mamba into YOLO26, the latest non-maximum suppression (NMS)-free object detection framework, by proposing MambaPSA, a lightweight Mamba-based replacement for the C2PSA block at the end of the backbone. To complement this study, we additionally insert a bidirectional Vision Mamba (BiViM) module at the P3, P4, and P5 levels of the neck. Experiments on PASCAL VOC 2007+2012 show that MambaPSA reduces parameters by 2.9%, FLOPs by 12.1%, and improves CPU inference throughput by 17.6% (from 17 to 20 FPS) with negligible accuracy change (-0.1 mAP50:95), while the P4 BiViM placement yields the best accuracy gain (+0.9 mAP50:95). These results suggest that SSMs offer a favorable efficiency-accuracy trade-off when replacing attention-based blocks in NMS-free lightweight detectors.

</details>


### [120] [Lesion Segmentation in Moderate to Severe Traumatic Brain Injury: An nnU-Net Based Approach with Adaptive Normalization in the AIMS-TBI 2025 Challenge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12684)
*Inhwa Son,Gaeun Lee,Sohyeon Sim,Kwang-Hyun Uhm*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文针对中重度创伤性脑损伤(msTBI)病灶分割难题，采用nnU-Net框架结合脑实质自适应强度归一化策略，在AIMS-TBI 2025挑战赛测试集上取得Overall Dice 0.6305，病灶Dice 0.4805，非病灶Dice 0.9324，验证了解剖约束归一化策略的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 中重度创伤性脑损伤病灶在大小、形状和位置上的高度异质性使得T1加权MRI分割极具临床挑战，亟需开发鲁棒且准确的分割算法。

Method: 采用nnU-Net框架，并在脑实质区域内进行自适应强度归一化，以减少受试者间变异性和非脑结构伪影。

Result: 在官方测试集上Overall Dice为0.6305，病灶分割Dice为0.4805，非病灶组织Dice为0.9324，在排行榜上表现极具竞争力。

Conclusion: 在nnU-Net管线中融入解剖约束的归一化策略是处理msTBI病灶分割复杂问题的有效方法。

Abstract: The segmentation of lesions in Moderate to Severe Traumatic Brain Injury (msTBI) from T1-weighted MRI presents a significant clinical challenge due to the profound heterogeneity of lesion characteristics in terms of size, shape, and location. To address this, the AIMS-TBI 2025 Challenge was organized to promote the development of robust and accurate segmentation algorithms. In this paper, we present our deep learning-based solution. Our methodology employs the nnU-Net framework with an adaptive intensity normalization strategy confined to the brain parenchyma, effectively reducing inter-subject variability and mitigating artifacts from non-brain structures. Upon final evaluation on the held-out test set, our method demonstrated highly competitive performance on the official leaderboard, achieving an Overall Dice Coefficient of 0.6305. The model obtained a Dice score of 0.4805 for lesion segmentation and 0.9324 for non-lesion tissue. While the lesion Dice reflects the difficulty of detecting highly heterogeneous lesions, the high non-lesion Dice primarily indicates the model's strong ability to correctly identify non-lesion voxels, demonstrating good specificity in differentiating lesion from non-lesion regions. These results demonstrate that incorporating anatomically constrained normalization within the nnU-Net pipeline is a powerful and effective strategy for tackling the complexities of msTBI lesion segmentation.

</details>


### [121] [Label-Decoupled Style Augmentation for Domain Generalization in Multi-Label Remote Sensing Scene Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12704)
*Alaa Almouradi,Erchan Aptoula*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 针对多标签遥感场景中的域泛化问题，提出标签解耦增强框架，通过每标签注意力获取区域级特征统计量并在同标签样本间混合，在三个数据集上平均mAP达71.5%，比全局统计基线高1.3个百分点，仅增加不到0.35%参数且不影响推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的特征统计增强方法（如MixStyle、EFDMix）对整幅图像施加全局风格扰动，在多标签遥感场景中会导致一个类别的统计特性污染其他类别的增强效果；同时，多标签遥感域泛化问题缺乏专门的方法和基准。

Method: 提出标签解耦增强框架：1) 通过可学习模块或梯度类激活映射生成每标签注意力图；2) 提取每标签特征统计量；3) 在共享相同标签的跨域样本之间，使用独立的每标签系数混合这些统计量；4) 通过注意力加权归一化重组特征。组合三种混合算子与两种注意力来源形成六个变体。

Result: 在UCM、AID、DFC15多标签数据的留一域评估基准上，最佳变体平均mAP达71.5%，超过经验风险最小化5.0个百分点，超过最强全局统计基线1.3个百分点，最难迁移任务上提升达7.7个百分点。

Conclusion: 消融实验表明空间注意力和刷新定位图影响最大；框架仅增加最多0.35%参数，推理阶段无额外开销，为多标签统计量域泛化提供了一种通用且廉价的升级方案。

Abstract: Multi-label classification assigns several co-occurring labels to each aerial scene, yet deployed models often encounter data distributions different from their training. Feature-statistics augmentation such as MixStyle, EFDMix, and correlated style uncertainty improves generalization at low cost but perturbs channel statistics globally, treating each image as a single style; one class can then contaminate the augmentation of another. Domain generalization is understudied for multi-label remote sensing; no prior method or multi-source benchmark targets it. A label-decoupled augmentation framework is therefore proposed, confining style perturbation to label-specific regions. Per-label attention, obtained from a learnable module or from gradient class-activation maps, yields per-label feature statistics; these statistics are mixed with cross-domain samples that share present labels, under independent per-label coefficients, and features are recomposed by attention-weighted normalization. Three operators combined with two attention sources produce six variants, evaluated on a leave-one-domain-out benchmark from multi-label UCM, AID, and DFC15 over six shared labels. Averaged over three splits and five seeds, the best variant attains 71.5% mean average precision, exceeding empirical risk minimization by 5.0 points and the strongest global-statistics baseline by 1.3 points, with the largest gain on the hardest transfer (up to 7.7 points). Ablations indicate that spatial attention and refreshed localization maps are most influential. The framework adds at most 0.35% parameters, leaves inference unchanged, and appears to offer a generic, inexpensive upgrade path for multi-label statistics-based domain generalization. Code is available upon acceptance at https://github.com/Alaa-Almouradi/Style-Augmentation-Upgrade.

</details>


### [122] [Weakly Supervised Spatio-Temporal Candidate Discovery of Dairy Farm Sites from Seasonal Satellite Imagery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12748)
*Usman Haider,Fatima Khalid,Karl Mason*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种弱监督流程，利用季节性Sentinel影像和OpenStreetMap先验知识，对乳牛场候选集群进行排序。通过Barlow Twins编码器学习多季节图像嵌入，结合规则评分和空间图平滑，从26,722个图块中选出535个高置信度图块，形成71个候选集群。Top 5集群在500米内精度0.60，1000米内精度0.80。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 卫星影像中的农场检测是一个时空候选排序问题，农场证据分散于牧场、田界、道路、建筑和季节性植被模式中，且直接农场标签往往不完整，导致全监督检测困难。因此需要一种弱监督方法来降低对完整标签的依赖。

Method: 1) 使用Barlow Twins编码器学习春季、夏季、秋季多季节图块的无标签嵌入；2) 利用OpenStreetMap农场先验，分为先验集和留出集；3) 基于先验特征设计规则评分，结合农场邻近性、季节性牧场证据和夏季绿度；4) 在地理邻近性和嵌入相似性构建的空间表示图上平滑规则评分；5) 将高分图块分组为排序后的候选集群。

Result: 从26,722个有效图块中选出535个高置信度图块，形成71个候选集群。Top 5集群在500米内精度0.60，1000米内精度0.80；Top 10集群在500米内精度0.40，1000米内精度0.80。

Conclusion: 季节性表征学习与弱地理先验相结合，能够将大规模卫星影像集合压缩为紧凑的候选集，便于人工审查，证明了弱监督方法在农场检测任务中的有效性。

Abstract: Farm site discovery from satellite imagery is a spatiotemporal candidate ranking problem because farm evidence is distributed across pasture, field boundaries, roads, buildings, and seasonal vegetation patterns. Direct farm labels are often incomplete, which makes fully supervised detection difficult. This paper proposes a weakly supervised pipeline for ranking dairy farm candidate clusters from seasonal Sentinel imagery and open map priors. The method uses aligned spring, summer, and autumn image tiles from County Cork, Ireland, with spectral bands, vegetation indices, built area indices, and a pasture channel. A Barlow Twins encoder learns multi-season tile embeddings without farm labels. In parallel, weak OpenStreetMap farm priors are split into a prior and a held-out set. Prior features support a rule-based tile score that combines farm proximity, seasonal pasture evidence, and summer greenness, while held-out features are reserved only for proxy evaluation. The rule score is smoothed over a spatial representation graph using geographic proximity and embedding similarity, and high-scoring tiles are grouped into ranked candidate clusters. From 26,722 valid tiles, the main run selects 535 high-confidence tiles and forms 71 candidate clusters. The top 5 clusters achieve 0.60 precision within 500 m and 0.80 precision within 1000 m of held-out OpenStreetMap farm features. The top 10 clusters achieve 0.40 precision within 500 m and 0.80 precision within 1000 m. The results show that seasonal representation learning and weak geographic priors can reduce large satellite image collections into compact candidate sets for human review.

</details>


### [123] [Color Pass-Through via Camera-Display Coupling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12746)
*Ruikang Li,Molin Li,Jiarui Wu,Zhe Wei,Pengpeng Liu,Tianfan Xue*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出Color Pass-Through，一种端到端学习框架，将相机和显示器作为耦合系统直接优化，以缩小真实场景与手机屏幕显示之间的颜色、亮度、对比度差异。相比传统分阶段校准方法，用户评分平均提升2.0分（5分制），定量指标提升2倍以上。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管相机和显示器技术不断进步，手机屏幕显示的图像在颜色、亮度和对比度上与真实场景仍存在明显差异。根本原因在于传统管线将拍摄到显示的过程分解为相机和显示器两个独立校准阶段，仅通过低维颜色变换连接，导致信息瓶颈和误差累积。

Method: 提出Color Pass-Through，一种端到端学习框架，直接对拍摄图像进行操作。核心思路是将相机和显示器视为耦合系统，而非独立校准，通过端到端优化将整个真实场景映射到显示器，并实现一步校准。

Result: 采用数字观察者和人类观察者进行验证。相比代表性基线方法，在5分制用户研究中平均提升2.0分，定量指标提升2倍以上，表明该方法能更好地再现原始场景的感知颜色。

Conclusion: 将相机和显示器作为耦合系统进行端到端优化，能够有效缩小真实场景与显示图像之间的感知差异，避免传统分阶段校准的信息瓶颈和误差累积问题。

Abstract: When a real-world scene is captured by a smartphone camera and viewed on its screen, the displayed image often differs noticeably from the original scene in color, brightness, and contrast. This gap persists despite substantial advances in both modern cameras and displays. A key reason is that most pipelines factor the high-dimensional capture-to-display process into two separately calibrated camera and display stages, and then connect them through low-dimensional color transforms, leading to information bottlenecks and inevitable error accumulation. To address this systemic challenge, we propose Color Pass-Through, an end-to-end learned framework that operates directly on captured images. Our key insight is to treat the camera and display as a coupled system rather than calibrating them in isolation. Coupling the camera and display yields two practical advantages: (1) it brings the entire real-world scenes to the display via end-to-end optimization, and (2) it allows efficient one-step calibration for each distinct observer via complete capture-to-display path. We validate Color Pass-Through using both digital and human observers. Compared with representative baselines, our method achieves an average gain of +2.0 points on a 5-point user study and more than 2x improvement on quantitative metrics, demonstrating improved reproduction of the perceived color of the original scene.

</details>


### [124] [Hallo4D: Multi-Modal Hallucination Mitigation for Consistent Spatio-Temporal Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12752)
*Hongbo Wang,Huaibo Huang,Jie Cao,Jin Liu,Haoyang Tong,Ran He*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Hallo4D提出了一种生成-检测-校正范式，利用大语言多模态模型(LMMs)识别3D/4D内容中的时空幻觉，并通过共识驱动的图像空间一致性优化和运动感知关键帧采样等机制，无需重新训练即可显著提升生成几何与时间一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有3D/4D生成方法依赖2D扩散先验但缺乏几何一致性约束，导致空间幻觉（重复结构、几何错位）和时间不一致（抖动、身份闪烁、结构漂移），亟需一个无需修改模型架构的统一一致性增强框架。

Method: 提出生成-检测-校正三阶段范式：(1) 利用LMMs从多视图/多帧渲染中识别并总结时空不一致；(2) 共识驱动的图像空间一致性优化，通过LMM选择器在多模型投票中评估候选修正；(3) 结合运动感知关键帧采样、LMM引导初始化、外观对齐、曝光感知优化和可见性剪枝等技术提升效率与鲁棒性。

Result: 在多种3D和4D生成场景下，Hallo4D显著优于强基线方法，验证了其可扩展性和泛化能力。

Conclusion: Hallo4D是首个统一且模型无关的时空幻觉缓解框架，通过LMM驱动的检测与校正机制，无需重训练或架构修改即可有效提升3D/4D生成的一致性，为一致性感知内容生成提供了可扩展的通用解决方案。

Abstract: While recent advances in 3D generation have enabled impressive visual synthesis, existing methods often rely on 2D diffusion supervision without explicit mechanisms for geometric consistency, leading to spatial hallucinations such as duplicated structures and misaligned geometry. These issues become more severe in 4D generation, where maintaining consistency across viewpoints and temporal evolution introduces additional challenges, including jitter, identity flicker, and structural drift. We present \textbf{Hallo4D}, a unified and model-agnostic framework for mitigating spatiotemporal hallucinations in 3D and 4D content generation. Hallo4D introduces a generation-detection-correction paradigm that leverages large multimodal language models (LMMs) to identify and summarize spatial and temporal inconsistencies from multi-view and multi-frame renderings. These insights guide a consensus-driven image-space consistency optimization, where an LMM-based selector evaluates candidate corrections through multi-model voting, without requiring retraining or architectural modifications. To further improve temporal consistency and optimization efficiency, Hallo4D incorporates motion-aware keyframe sampling, LMM-guided initialization, and appearance alignment. We additionally introduce exposure-aware optimization and visibility pruning to enhance robustness under challenging viewpoints. Extensive experiments demonstrate that Hallo4D consistently outperforms strong baselines across diverse 3D and 4D generation settings, providing a scalable and generalizable solution for consistency-aware content generation.

</details>


### [125] [CRC-HGD: A Histopathological Image Dataset for Grading Colorectal Cancer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12750)
*Elham Amjadi,Amin Bahreini,Sayed Mohammad Hasan Emami,Sayyed Mohammadreza Hakimian,Alireza Fahim,Hojjatollah Rahimi,Hamidreza Bolhasani*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文介绍了CRC-HGD数据集，包含来自214名结直肠腺癌患者的1,914张H&E染色组织病理学显微镜图像，涵盖三个分化等级（Grade I-III）及四种放大倍率（4x、10x、20x、40x），旨在为基于AI的结直肠癌自动分级研究提供标准化数据资源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 结直肠癌是全球第三大常见癌症，准确的组织学分级对预后和治疗规划至关重要。尽管AI技术已被广泛应用于癌症自动检测与分类，但缺乏一个涵盖多种分化等级和多放大倍率、组织良好且标注规范的标准化数据集来支持结直肠癌分级计算分析。

Method: 从214名结直肠腺癌患者（Grade I: 106例, Grade II: 75例, Grade III: 33例）的H&E染色组织切片中采集1,914张图像，依据WHO标准分为高分化（I级）、中分化（II级）和低分化（III级），并为每例标本提供4x、10x、20x和40x四种放大倍率的图像。数据集托管于Mendeley Data和databiox.com平台。

Result: 构建了一个包含1,914张图像、覆盖三个分化等级和四种放大倍率的结直肠癌组织病理学图像数据集（CRC-HGD），所有图像均来自2014-2019年间确诊并按照WHO标准分级的临床病例。

Conclusion: CRC-HGD数据集通过提供多等级、多放大倍率的标注图像，为结直肠癌分级的自动化计算分析（如机器学习和深度学习研究）提供了标准化数据基础，有望推动AI辅助病理诊断的发展。

Abstract: Colorectal cancer (CRC) is the third most common cancer worldwide and the second leading cause of cancer-related deaths globally, with approximately 1,926,425 new cases and 904,019 deaths reported in 2022. Accurate histologic grading plays a critical role in prognosis and treatment planning for colorectal adenocarcinoma. In recent years, artificial intelligence and its subcategories, including machine learning and deep learning, have been increasingly employed for automated cancer detection and classification. An appropriate and well-organized dataset is the essential first step to achieve this goal. This paper introduces CRC-HGD, a histopathological microscopy image dataset of 1,914 images obtained from 214 colorectal adenocarcinoma patients (Grade I: 106, Grade II: 75, Grade III: 33). The specimens are H&E-stained colorectal tissue sections acquired at the Poursina Hakim Research Center of Isfahan University of Medical Sciences, Iran, diagnosed between 2014 and 2019, and graded according to the World Health Organization (WHO) criteria into three grades: well-differentiated (Grade I), moderately differentiated (Grade II), and poorly differentiated (Grade III). For each specimen, four magnification levels are provided: 4x, 10x, 20x, and 40x. The dataset is accessible via Mendeley Data (https://doi.org/10.17632/yfp5sfj47m.4) and at http://databiox.com, where the latest version is also available. The distinctive feature of this dataset is the provision of labeled specimens across all three differentiation grades at multiple magnification levels, enabling comprehensive computational analysis of colorectal cancer grading.

</details>


### [126] [HSEmotion Team at the 11th ABAW Challenge: Multi-Task Learning and Ambivalence/Hesitancy Video Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12774)
*Aleksei Bakin,Andrey V. Savchenko*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文介绍了在ABAW竞赛中的两项任务成果：1）在s-Aff-Wild2数据集上使用MT-EmotiDDAMFN和MT-EmotiEffNet-B0轻量级面部特征提取器，结合时序高斯平滑、类别偏置、AffectNet混合、AU阈值调优和加权骨干融合等后处理技术，实现效价、唤醒、表情和动作单元的多任务学习；2）在BAH数据集上通过面部、HuBERT音频和RoBERTa文本分类器的晚期融合、时序聚合及全局文本门控，实现犹豫/矛盾视频识别的Macro F1提升。两个任务均在不微调重型骨干网络的情况下取得有竞争力的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在ABAW竞赛中，现有方法常依赖大规模端到端模型，计算成本高且部署灵活性差。本文旨在探索轻量级多模态融合与系统化预测校准方法，在不微调重型骨干网络的前提下，同时提升多任务情感分析和犹豫/矛盾视频识别的性能。

Method: 任务一（多任务学习）：使用冻结的轻量级面部特征提取器MT-EmotiDDAMFN和MT-EmotiEffNet-B0，配备独立预测头，结合时序高斯平滑、每类表情偏置、AffectNet数据混合、每AU阈值调优和加权骨干融合等系统化后处理。任务二（犹豫/矛盾识别）：构建面部、HuBERT音频和RoBERTa文本分类器，通过晚期融合、时序聚合和全局文本门控实现视频级Macro F1预测。

Result: 在s-Aff-Wild2验证集上，集成模型显著超越ConvNeXt基线。在BAH验证集上，帧级加权F1从ABAW-8的0.74提升至0.79；公开测试集上最佳视频级Macro F1达到0.73。

Conclusion: 系统化的预测校准和轻量级多模态融合能够在保持高效性和部署灵活性的同时，达到与更重的端到端方法相媲美的性能，验证了轻量级方案在情感计算任务中的有效性。

Abstract: This article presents our results for the 11th Affective Behavior Analysis in-the-Wild (ABAW) competition. For multi-task learning with simultaneous prediction of valence, arousal, facial expressions, and action units on s-Aff-Wild2 dataset, we use frozen lightweight facial extractors, MT-EmotiDDAMFN and MT-EmotiEffNet-B0, with separate heads and systematic post-processing: temporal Gaussian smoothing, per-class expression bias, AffectNet blending, per-AU threshold tuning, and weighted backbone fusion. On the official validation set, our ensemble significantly exceeds the performance of the ConvNeXt baseline. For ambivalence/hesitancy video recognition on the expanded BAH dataset, we extend the audiovisual pipeline to video-level Macro F1 by late fusion of face, HuBERT audio, and RoBERTa text classifiers, temporal aggregation, and a global-text gate. Frame-level Weighted F1 on validation set rises from 0.74 in ABAW-8 to 0.79, while the best public-test video-level Macro F1 reaches 0.73. In both tasks, competitive performance is achieved without fine-tuning heavy backbones. These results indicate that systematic prediction calibration and lightweight multimodal fusion can rival substantially heavier end-to-end approaches while offering improved efficiency and deployment flexibility.

</details>


### [127] [RFMSR: Residual Flow Matching for Image Super-Resolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12753)
*Shuwei Huang,Tianyao Luo,Jicheng Liu,Daizong Liu,Pan Zhou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出RFMSR，一种基于残差流匹配的图像超分辨率框架，将源分布中心化在低质量潜变量上以减少传输距离，并采用两阶段训练策略实现高质量单步生成的同时保留多步推理能力，在感知质量上达到或超越SOTA方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于流匹配的视觉超分方法采用从纯高斯先验到数据分布的标准流公式，丢弃了低质量输入中已有的丰富结构信息；同时，现有的单步加速技术往往牺牲了模型的多步推理能力。

Method: 提出残差流匹配（Residual Flow Matching）框架，将源分布中心化在LQ潜变量处，缩短传输距离并保留结构先验；采用两阶段训练：第一阶段通过条件流匹配预训练速度场，第二阶段在保留所有时间步速度损失的同时对单步预测施加端到端监督。

Result: 大量实验表明，RFMSR在感知质量上达到或超越了现有SOTA方法。

Conclusion: RFMSR通过残差流匹配和两阶段训练策略，在保持多步细化能力的同时实现了高质量的单步超分生成，代码已开源。

Abstract: Image super-resolution (ISR) has witnessed remarkable progress with diffusion models and flow matching. The dominant text-to-image (T2I) based approaches leverage large-scale foundation models as generative priors, achieving impressive perceptual quality but at the cost of massive model sizes and prohibitive training expenses. Recent flow-matching-based vision-only approaches have made significant strides; however, they adopt standard flow formulations that transport from a pure Gaussian prior to the data distribution, discarding the rich structural information already present in the low-quality (LQ) input. Furthermore, existing single-step acceleration techniques often forfeit the model's multi-step inference capability. In this paper, we propose Residual Flow Matching for Image Super-Resolution (RFMSR), a vision-only framework that centers the source distribution at the LQ latent, reducing transport distance and preserving structural priors throughout the flow trajectory. We further introduce a two-phase training strategy: Phase I pretrains the velocity field via conditional flow matching, while Phase II applies end-to-end supervision to the single-step prediction while retaining the velocity loss across all timesteps, achieving high-quality single-step generation without sacrificing multi-step refinement. Extensive experiments demonstrate that RFMSR achieves comparable or even superior perceptual quality compared to state-of-the-art (SOTA) methods. The source code is available at https://github.com/Faze-Hsw/RFMSR.

</details>


### [128] [VisCo: Leveraging Large Language Models as Intrinsic Encoders for Visual Token Compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12756)
*Yupeng Zheng,Kai Zou,Bin Liu,Nenghai Yu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: VisCo是一种训练高效的自压缩框架，通过复用预训练VLM本身作为内在压缩器，利用参数共享的自编码器结构，使用记忆令牌压缩视觉信息，在多种压缩比下超越现有方法，甚至在极端单令牌设置下仍保持稳定，且能提升基础模型性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉令牌压缩方法存在两难困境：无训练策略在高压缩比下性能严重下降；有训练方法引入外部压缩模块导致高昂重训练成本且损害VLM先验知识。VLM本身已具备强大的信息编码能力，但现有方法未能充分利用。

Method: 提出VisCo框架，复用预训练VLM自身作为内在压缩器，构建参数共享的自编码器：使用少量记忆令牌压缩视觉信息，并将编码层的层次信息迁移至解码层。

Result: 在所有评估压缩比下超越现有方法，更激进压缩下增益更大；极端单令牌设置下仍保持稳定；与原始视觉令牌结合时还能提升基础模型性能。

Conclusion: VisCo证明了视觉令牌压缩可以同时实现训练高效和性能优越，且学到的记忆令牌捕获了超越压缩本身的互补表示。

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) process large numbers of visual tokens, resulting in substantial inference latency and memory overhead. This has motivated extensive research on visual token compression. While training-free strategies rely on heuristic metrics and suffer significant performance degradation under high compression ratios, many training-based methods introduce external compression modules that force the VLM backbone to adapt, incurring substantial retraining cost and compromising VLMs' priors. Effective visual token compression hinges on strong information encoding, a capability already present in pretrained VLMs but underutilized by existing approaches. Motivated by this, we propose VisCo, a training-efficient self-compression framework that reuses the pretrained VLM itself as an intrinsic compressor. VisCo is a parameter-sharing autoencoder that compresses visual information using a small set of memory tokens and transfers hierarchical information from encoding to decoding. Experiments show that VisCo surpasses prior methods across all evaluated compression ratios, with larger gains under more aggressive compression, and remains stable even in the extreme single-token setting. Moreover, when combined with the original visual tokens, the learned memory tokens can even improve the base model, suggesting that VisCo captures complementary representations beyond compression.

</details>


### [129] [EvoGraph-R1: Self-Evolving Multimodal Knowledge Hypergraphs for Agentic Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12764)
*Jiashi Lin,Changhong Jiang,Xiangru Lin,Ruifei Zhang,Xinyi Zhu,Jiyao Liu,Cheng Tang,Ye Du,Shujian Gao,Junzhi Ning,Lihao Liu,Ziyan Huang,Tianbin Li,Jin Ye,Junjun He*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: EvoGraph-R1 提出自演化知识图谱的 GraphRAG 框架，将检索建模为马尔可夫决策过程，通过查询、扩展、编辑、终止四个动作实现动态图结构演化，在视觉问答和文本问答任务上优于现有 RAG 基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 GraphRAG 方法将知识图谱视为离线构建的单次查询静态结构，导致三个瓶颈：跨模态推理的文本碎片化、无法吸收新证据或纠正错误、缺乏自适应精炼的刚性单次检索，与知识密集型推理的交互式迭代特性不匹配。

Method: 将检索建模为马尔可夫决策过程（MDP），智能体观察图状态并执行四类动作：GraphRetrieve（查询）、WebSearch（扩展）、GraphEdit（精炼）、Answer（终止）。这些动作重塑超图结构并生成反馈信号，通过闭环机制整合新证据、纠正错误、优化结构以支持多跳推理。

Result: 在多模态 VQA 和文本 QA 基准测试上，EvoGraph-R1 在准确率、覆盖率和可追溯性方面均显著优于现有 RAG 基线方法。

Conclusion: 自演化知识图谱作为一种跨模态的基础范式，能够有效弥补静态 GraphRAG 的不足，为知识密集型推理提供更灵活、更鲁棒的检索增强方案。

Abstract: Retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) has emerged as a critical paradigm for grounding Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) in external knowledge. Recent GraphRAG methods introduce structured entity-relation graphs to improve retrieval and reasoning. However, they remain limited by treating knowledge graphs as static data structures built offline and queried in a single pass. This static paradigm misaligns with the interactive, iterative nature of knowledge-intensive reasoning, creating three bottlenecks: (i) text-centric fragmentation that impedes cross-modal reasoning, (ii) frozen structures unable to incorporate new evidence or correct errors, and (iii) rigid single-pass retrieval without adaptive refinement. To overcome these limitations, we introduce EvoGraph-R1, a self-evolving GraphRAG framework that reconceptualizes knowledge graphs as dynamic environments shaped through agent interactions. We formulate retrieval as a Markov Decision Process (MDP) where the agent observes the graph state and executes actions to query (GraphRetrieve), expand (WebSearch), refine (GraphEdit), or terminate (Answer) the reasoning. These actions reshape the hypergraph structure and generate feedback signals that guide subsequent evolution. Through this closed loop, the hypergraph evolves by integrating new evidence, correcting errors, and refining structure to support multi-hop reasoning. Experiments on multimodal VQA and text QA benchmarks demonstrate substantial improvements over existing RAG baselines in accuracy, coverage, and traceability, establishing self-evolving knowledge graphs as a fundamental paradigm across modalities.

</details>


### [130] [MBTI: A Multi-Branch Efficient Fine-Tuning Framework for Hyperspectral Image Classification with Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12782)
*Mingzhen Xu,Haonan Guo,Di Wang,Yinghua Qu,Zhiliang Zhou,Lei Zhang,Huiwen Yao,Rui Zhao,Fengxiang Wang,Gang Wan,Bo Du,Liangpei Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出MBTI框架，一种多分支高效微调方法，通过保留完整光谱信息的分支预处理、独立LoRA模块和通道注意力融合，解决高光谱基础模型因传感器光谱配置差异导致的迁移困难问题，在三个数据集上以仅2.33%-2.36%可训练参数取得竞争性性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有高光谱基础模型迁移时，不同传感器的光谱波段配置差异大，而现有适配策略（压缩、选择、重塑光谱）会丢弃有用信息并破坏局部光谱连续性，导致模型迁移效果受限。

Method: 提出MBTI框架，包含三部分：(1) 光谱连续性保持的多分支预处理策略，将原始HSI划分为连续光谱子集，采用波段复用机制避免无效填充；(2) 每个分支插入独立的低秩适配（LoRA）模块，冻结预训练参数仅微调少量参数；(3) 多分支通道注意力融合模块，自适应重新校准并整合所有分支特征。

Result: 在三个公开高光谱数据集上，MBTI相比代表性分类方法取得竞争性甚至更优的性能；在rank-8配置下，仅约2.33%-2.36%的参数是可训练的。

Conclusion: MBTI通过多分支架构保留全波段光谱信息并利用LoRA高效微调，有效解决了跨传感器高光谱图像分类中的模型迁移难题，实现了参数高效下的优异分类性能。

Abstract: Hyperspectral foundation models learn transferable spectral-spatial representations from large-scale unlabeled data. They provide an effective paradigm for adapting to downstream hyperspectral image (HSI) classification tasks with limited labeled samples. However, spectral band configurations vary substantially across sensors, which makes direct model transfer difficult. Existing adaptation strategies often compress, select, or reshape the original spectra to match model-specific input requirements. These operations may discard useful spectral information and weaken local spectral continuity. To address this problem, we propose MBTI, a Multi-Branch efficient fine-tuning framework for Hyperspectral Image classification. MBTI adapts hyperspectral foundation models to downstream classification tasks while preserving full-band spectral information. First, we introduce a spectral-continuity-preserving multi-branch preprocessing strategy. The original HSI is divided into multiple continuous spectral subsets, and a band reuse mechanism is used when the remaining bands cannot form a complete branch. This avoids invalid padding and unnecessary spectral loss. Second, independent Low-Rank Adaptation (LoRA) modules are inserted into each branch. They enable different spectral intervals to learn task-specific discriminative features while keeping most pre-trained parameters frozen. Finally, a multi-branch channel attention fusion module adaptively recalibrates and integrates features from all spectral branches. Experiments on three public hyperspectral datasets show that MBTI achieves competitive and superior performance compared with representative classification methods. Under the final rank-8 configuration, only about 2.33\%--2.36\% of the parameters are trainable. The code will be available at https://github.com/Azhenmiddleblock/MBTI/tree/main.

</details>


### [131] [ExtraGS: Enhancing Endoscopic View Extrapolation via Diffusion-Guided 3D Gaussian Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12785)
*Cheng-Tai Hsieh,Jiwei Shan,Han Fang,Jianshu Hu,Tao Ni,Lijun Han,Yutong Ban,Shing Shin Cheng,Hesheng Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ExtraGS提出一种扩散引导的3D高斯泼溅框架，通过不确定性引导的虚拟相机采样和扩散模型生成的伪观测数据，有效增强内窥镜视角外推能力，显著减少伪影并在多个公开数据集上达到SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统内窥镜视野有限，而现有神经渲染方法（NeRF、3DGS）因依赖稀疏观测，在超出训练轨迹的外推时会产生严重伪影，无法可靠重建未观察到的解剖结构。

Method: 1）不确定性引导的虚拟相机采样策略，主动探索盲区；2）利用扩散模型对虚拟视角渲染结果进行精炼，生成伪观测数据；3）置信度加权的微调策略，在引入伪观测时保护已可靠重建的区域。

Result: 在多个公开内窥镜数据集上，ExtraGS显著减少了外推伪影，在内窥镜新视角合成任务中达到SOTA性能。

Conclusion: 扩散模型引导的伪观测生成与置信度加权优化可以有效弥补内窥镜视角外推中的信息缺失，为手术导航提供更可靠的视觉感知。

Abstract: Robot-assisted minimally invasive surgery (MIS) critically depends on reliable endoscopic perception for navigation and safety. However, conventional endoscopes provide only a limited field of view, leaving large portions of surrounding anatomy unobserved. Recent neural rendering approaches, such as Neural Radiance Fields and 3D Gaussian Splatting, enable novel view synthesis from endoscopic videos, but their reliance on sparse observations often leads to severe artifacts when extrapolating beyond the training trajectory.In this work, we propose ExtraGS, a framework for enhancing endoscopic view extrapolation via diffusion-guided 3D Gaussian Splatting. Starting from an initial reconstruction, we introduce an uncertainty-guided virtual camera sampling strategy to actively explore blind spots and maximize information gain. The rendered views from these sampled locations are refined using a diffusion model to recover plausible anatomical structures, producing pseudo observations that guide further optimization. To prevent the generated content from degrading reliable regions, we adopt a confidence-weighted fine-tuning strategy when incorporating these pseudo observations.Extensive experiments on multiple public endoscopic datasets demonstrate that ExtraGS significantly reduces extrapolation artifacts and achieves state-of-the-art performance in endoscopic novel view synthesis.

</details>


### [132] [CoRe: A Comprehensive Framework for Cross-Image Comparative Reasoning in Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12786)
*Lin Peng,Cong Wan,Zeyu Guo,SongLin Dong,Yihong Gong*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出CoRe框架，包含CoRe-20K数据集、TriSR结构化奖励机制和CoRe-Bench基准，用于解决跨图像细粒度比较推理问题，在基准上相比最强基线提升28.2个百分点的部分准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉语言模型在跨图像比较推理任务上表现不佳，尤其是需要细粒度属性定位和全局一致推理的场景，缺乏专门的训练数据和评估基准。

Method: (i) CoRe-20K：利用多专家协作流水线从结构化视觉元数据自动构建大规模三元组训练集，涵盖计数、深度、距离和空间关系；(ii) TriSR：在GRPO优化下联合监督属性定位、判断对齐和三胞胎一致性的结构化奖励框架；(iii) CoRe-Bench：首个专注于细粒度跨图像比较推理的基准。

Result: CoRe在CoRe-Bench上显著优于现有视觉语言模型，部分准确率相比最强基线提升28.2个百分点，同时在标准多模态基准上保持竞争力。

Conclusion: 所提出的CoRe框架有效解决了跨图像细粒度比较推理中的属性定位和全局一致性问题，验证了结构化训练数据与奖励机制相结合的有效性。

Abstract: Cross-image comparative reasoning remains challenging for vision-language models (VLMs), especially when correct prediction requires fine-grained attribute grounding and globally consistent reasoning. We present CoRe, a unified framework for this problem. CoRe includes: (i) CoRe-20K, a large-scale triplet-based training set automatically constructed from structured visual metadata through a multi-expert collaborative pipeline, covering counting, depth, distance, and spatial relations; (ii) TriSR, a structured reward framework that jointly supervises attribute grounding, judgment alignment, and triplet consistency under GRPO optimization; and (iii) CoRe-Bench, the first benchmark dedicated to fine-grained cross-image comparative reasoning. Experiments show that CoRe substantially outperforms existing VLMs on CoRe-Bench while remaining competitive on standard multimodal benchmarks, achieving a 28.2-point gain in partial accuracy over the strongest baseline.

</details>


### [133] [UniVR: Thinking in Visual Space for Unified Visual Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12800)
*Zhongwei Ren,Yunchao Wei,Yao Zhao,Weibo Gong,Xiao Liu,Anran Wang,Xiangtai Li,Xiaojie Jin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: UniVR提出了VR-GRPO强化学习范式，通过全局和步骤级奖励机制，无需特定任务启发式方法或图像-文本对，即可从纯视觉演示中同时学习复杂推理、细粒度物理动力学和长期规划。在包含16个来源的VR-X基准上取得高达25%的提升，并在多模态理解基准上表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 直接从原始视觉数据中学习广泛的世界知识是智能的基本能力，现有方法通常依赖任务特定的启发式方法或图像-文本对，缺乏统一框架来同时学习复杂推理、物理动力学和长期规划。

Method: 提出UniVR框架，核心是VR-GRPO强化学习范式，包含全局奖励和步骤级奖励两个互补信号，确保推理过程中的逻辑一致性和物理一致性，无需任务特定启发式方法或图像-文本对。同时构建VR-X基准数据集，涵盖16个来源的长时间操作、空间谜题和物理推理任务。

Result: 在VR-X基准上实现高达25%的性能提升，同时在多种多模态理解基准上表现出卓越的视觉推理能力。

Conclusion: 研究表明在视觉空间中直接推理具有巨大潜力，所有代码、数据和模型均已开源。

Abstract: Learning broad world knowledge directly from raw visual data is a fundamental capability of intelligence. We introduce UniVR, the first investigation into simultaneously learning complex reasoning, fine-grained physical dynamics, and long-term planning from pure visual demonstrations. At its core, UniVR features VR-GRPO, a reinforcement learning paradigm with complementary global and step-level rewards. This approach enforces logical coherence and physical consistency throughout the reasoning process without requiring task-specific heuristics or image-text pairs. To train and evaluate UniVR, we construct VR-X, a large-scale benchmark curated from 16 diverse sources spanning long-horizon manipulation, spatial puzzles, and physical reasoning. It is the first comprehensive suite to assess these heterogeneous capabilities under a purely visual protocol. Remarkably, UniVR achieves up to a 25% improvement on VR-X, and its superior visual reasoning also boosts performance on various multimodal understanding benchmarks. These findings underscore the vast potential of reasoning within visual spaces, with all code, data, and models are open-sourced for further research.

</details>


### [134] [Breaking Déjà Vu: Independent Auditing of Visual Place Recognition through Vision-Language Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12818)
*Sania Waheed,Michael Milford,Sarvapali D. Ramchurn,Shoaib Ehsan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出视觉地点识别审计（VPR Auditing），一种基于视觉语言模型（VLM）的后检索验证框架，无需标注验证数据或环境先验知识，通过联合推理查询和候选图像进行实例级验证，在六个基准数据集上平均提升召回率@1达13.6%，并将误接受率降至12%，精度维持95%以上，覆盖率超75%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VPR方法依赖固定阈值进行图像匹配，该阈值需通过标注验证数据调优，在环境变化下不可靠，尤其对安全关键型机器人应用中误接受回环可能破坏轨迹与地图估计。

Method: 提出VLM审计框架，作为独立的后检索验证模块，利用VLM对查询与候选图像进行联合推理，实现实例级匹配验证，无需架构特定的置信度度量、数据集阈值或环境先验知识。

Result: 在六个基准数据集上，使用五种VPR方法和四种VLM进行评测，VLM审计平均提升召回率@1达13.6%，误接受率降至12%，精度保持在95%以上，覆盖率超过75%。

Conclusion: VLM-based审计框架能有效提升VPR匹配可靠性，消除对人工阈值调优的依赖，适用于无标注数据的环境，尤其适用于安全关键型机器人应用。

Abstract: Visual place recognition (VPR) is a key enabler of accurate localization and long-term autonomous navigation in robotics applications, such as loop closure detection for simultaneous localisation and mapping (SLAM). However, real-world VPR deployment relies on selecting an image matching threshold that balances precision and recall. These thresholds are typically tuned using labeled validation data and fixed during deployment, making them unreliable under environmental changes where ground truth is unavailable. This is particularly problematic in safety-critical robotics, where accepting a false loop closure can corrupt the estimated trajectory and map. In this work, we introduce Visual Place Recognition Auditing, an independent post-retrieval verification framework that leverages Vision-Language Models (VLMs) to assess retrieved matches by reasoning jointly over query and candidate images. Unlike conventional verification methods, our approach performs instance-level verification without requiring architecture-specific confidence measures, dataset-dependent thresholds, or prior knowledge of the deployment environment. We evaluate our method on six benchmark datasets using five state-of-the-art VPR methods and four VLMs. Results show that VLM-based auditing improves recall@1 by 13.6% on average as compared to state-of-the-art methods while reducing false acceptance rates to 12%, maintaining precision above 95% and coverage above 75%.

</details>


### [135] [AVSCap: Orchestrating Audio-Visual Synergy for Omni-modal Video Captioning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12820)
*Yanghai Wang,Jiahao Wang,Jiafu Tang,Yuanxing Zhang,Zhe Cao,Hanyan Bian,Zijie Zhang,Weiliang Luo,Zhiyu Pan,Zixuan Dong,Jiaheng Liu,Zhaoxiang Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: AVSCap提出了一种面向全模态视频描述的方法，通过解耦-融合流水线构建训练语料、两阶段训练策略（监督微调+强化学习）以及细粒度事件评估基准，显著提升了视频描述中非语音音频覆盖率和跨模态事件绑定能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大模型在处理视频描述时，往往将音频和视觉流视为松散耦合的观测，过度依赖语音识别，对非语音声音及其与视觉事件的联系描述不足，未能捕捉视觉动作、语音、音乐和音效之间的协同演化关系。

Method: 1) 构建AVSCap-130K三模态训练语料，采用解耦-融合流水线先锚定视觉和声学证据再生成接地全模态描述；2) 两阶段训练策略：监督微调建立基线能力，样本高效的强化学习使用混合奖励优化声学完整性和音视频协同；3) 提出AVSCapBench基准，将描述分解为视觉、音频和协同事件，进行细粒度事件召回评估。

Result: AVSCap-7B在AVSCapBench和外部基准上，非语音音频覆盖率和跨模态绑定能力显著提升，在开源模型中取得最佳综合性能；强化学习相比增加SFT数据带来更大收益。

Conclusion: 显式跨模态事件绑定是提升全模态视频描述质量的关键，强化学习策略在优化音视频协同方面比单纯扩大监督数据更有效。

Abstract: Omni-modal video captioning is not merely combining visual captioning with audio transcription: a useful caption must describe how visual actions, speech, music, and sound effects co-evolve. Existing large multimodal models often fail at this relational step, treating audio and visual streams as loosely coupled observations, relying on automatic speech recognition, and under-specifying non-speech sounds and their links to visual events. We present AVSCap, a framework for audio-visual captioning centered on explicit cross-modal event binding. First, we construct AVSCap-130K, a tri-modal training corpus generated by a decoupled-then-fused pipeline that anchors visual and acoustic evidence before composing grounded omni-modal captions. Second, we train AVSCap-7B, a 7B captioner with a two-stage strategy: supervised fine-tuning establishes baseline capabilities, while sample-efficient reinforcement learning uses hybrid rewards to optimize acoustic completeness and audio-visual synergy. Our scaling analysis shows that reinforcement learning brings larger gains than increasing SFT data. Third, we introduce AVSCapBench, a benchmark that decomposes captions into visual, audio, and synergy events and evaluates them with fine-grained event recall. Experiments on AVSCapBench and external benchmarks show that AVSCap-7B improves non-speech audio coverage and cross-modal binding, delivering the best overall performance among evaluated open-source models.

</details>


### [136] [LARAD: Layout-Aware Road Anomaly Detection via Spatial-Logic Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12858)
*Shiyi Mu,Xujie Chen,Shugong Xu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: LARAD提出了一种基于空间逻辑推理的道路异常检测方法，通过合成纹理一致但空间无效的训练样本，并利用轻量级OoD引导注意力分支，在保持单模型高效性的同时实现了最先进的异常分割性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有异常分割方法过度依赖纹理新颖性来识别分布外物体，忽视了上下文空间逻辑，导致大量误报；而缓解误报通常需要级联大规模视觉模型，引入不可接受的推理延迟。

Method: 1) 提出空间逻辑违规合成(SLVS)流程，生成纹理一致但空间无效的训练样本；2) 在标准闭集分割网络上增加轻量级OoD引导注意力分支。

Result: LARAD显著增强了对逻辑异常的鲁棒性，建立了新的最先进性能，同时保持单模型架构的高效性。

Conclusion: 将异常检测的范式从外观匹配转向空间逻辑推理是可行的，单模型架构可以在不牺牲效率的前提下实现更好的异常分割效果。

Abstract: Accurate open-world obstacle detection is critical for autonomous driving. Current anomaly segmentation methods suffer from a fundamental blind spot: they over-rely on texture novelty to identify out-of-distribution (OoD) objects while ignoring contextual spatial logic. Furthermore, mitigating the resulting false positives often requires cascading massive vision models, introducing unacceptable inference latency. To address these issues, we propose Layout-Aware Road Anomaly Detection (LARAD), shifting the paradigm from appearance matching to spatial-logic reasoning. First, we introduce the Spatial-Logic Violation Synthesis (SLVS) pipeline, which generates training samples that are texture-consistent yet spatially invalid, forcing the model to learn contextual violations. Second, we augment a standard closed-set segmentation network with a lightweight, OoD-guided attention branch. Extensive experiments demonstrate that LARAD significantly enhances robustness against logical anomalies and establishes a new state-of-the-art, all while retaining the high efficiency of a single-model architecture.

</details>


### [137] [Statistical Non-linear Reconstruction Loss for Image Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12866)
*Nguyen Minh Tri,Hoang Khuong Duy,Huynh Cong Viet Ngu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于sigmoid的非线性重建损失函数，并结合统计校准机制，有效抑制异常泄漏问题，在MVTec-AD和VisA数据集上实现SOTA级别的异常检测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于重建的无监督图像异常检测方法易受"异常泄漏"问题困扰，即标准MSE损失会驱动模型忠实重建异常模式，导致检测失效。现有方法缺乏对异常特征的有效抑制机制。

Method: 1) 提出非线性重建损失，采用sigmoid类挤压函数抑制高幅度特征，降低异常值对优化的主导作用；2) 引入统计校准方案，从正常特征分布的置信区间中选择缩放因子k，实现数据驱动的抑制强度控制。

Result: MVTec-AD上达到99.0% Image-AUROC和97.3% Pixel-AUROC；VisA上达到95.3% Image-AUROC和99.0% Pixel-AUROC，表现优于或持平现有SOTA方法。

Conclusion: 非线性梯度抑制是缓解异常泄漏、提升异常定位能力的有效机制，适用于统一的工业检测场景。

Abstract: Reconstruction-based methods are a cornerstone of unsupervised image anomaly detection, but they remain vulnerable to \emph{outlier leakage}, where standard mean squared error (MSE) loss drives the model to faithfully reconstruct anomalous patterns. We propose a Non-linear Reconstruction Loss that applies a sigmoid-based squashing function to suppress high-magnitude features, preventing outliers from dominating optimization while preserving sensitivity to normal patterns. In addition, we introduce a statistical calibration scheme that selects the scaling factor $k$ from the confidence interval (CI) of the normal feature distribution, enabling data-driven control of the suppression strength. Our approach achieves competitive or superior anomaly detection performance compared to state-of-the-art methods, reaching 99.0\% Image-AUROC and 97.3\% Pixel-AUROC on MVTec-AD, and 95.3\% Image-AUROC and 99.0\% Pixel-AUROC on VisA. These results indicate that non-linear gradient suppression is an effective mechanism for mitigating outlier leakage and improving anomaly localization in unified industrial inspection settings. The implementation is available at https://github.com/mintii13/Statistical-Non-linear-Reconstruction-Loss.git.

</details>


### [138] [Metric-Guided Synthetic Image Data Rendering for Deep Learning compatible with Agentic AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12874)
*Martina Radoynova,Samuel Pantze,Trina De,Ulrik Günther,Artur Yakimovich*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了GraNatPy，一个用于合成数据生成中渲染场景优化的Python包，通过量化指标提升真实感、多样性和数据集规模，并在病毒噬菌斑检测任务中验证了混合真实与合成数据对提升小目标检测性能的有效性，同时开发了SynthClaw实现渲染参数自动优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度学习在科学视觉应用中依赖大量人工标注的真实数据，成本高且易出错；合成数据生成可缓解该问题，但缺乏量化指标来系统指导渲染场景优化以缩小真实与合成图像之间的域差距。

Method: 1. 开发GraNatPy包，提供量化指标以指导渲染场景改进；2. 验证真实感、多样性和数据集规模与目标检测零样本性能的正相关性；3. 通过病毒噬菌斑照片实验证明梯度相似性对小目标检测的影响，并采用混合真实与合成数据策略提升性能；4. 将程序化渲染转化为智能体技能SynthClaw，自动优化程序化参数。

Result: 量化提升渲染场景的真实感、多样性和数据集规模能改善视觉感知并提升目标检测模型的零样本性能；梯度相似性影响小目标检测性能，混合真实与合成数据可改善该问题；SynthClaw可自动化程序化渲染参数优化。

Conclusion: 合成数据生成中引入量化指标指导渲染优化能有效缩小域差距，混合数据策略和自动化参数优化进一步提升了合成数据在科学视觉应用中的实用性。

Abstract: Deep learning computer vision for scientific applications requires collecting and annotating large datasets in a laborious, expensive and error-prone process. Synthetic data generation through 3D modelling and rendering may simplify this process and increase the accuracy of annotations by generating them programmatically. However, minimising the domain gap between real and synthetic images visually is subjective and lacks systematic quantitative guidance. We present GraNatPy, a Python package with metrics to guide improvement of the rendered scene. We show that quantifiable increase in realism, diversity and size of rendered dataset correlates with improved visual perception of the scene and higher zero-shot performance of an object detection model. Furthermore, we demonstrated using photographs of virological plaque assays that gradient similarity affects performance on small object detection, which can be improved by mixing real and synthetic data. Finally, we turn procedural data rendering into an agentic skill (SynthClaw) to automate the procedural parameter optimisation.

</details>


### [139] [Inhibited Self-Attention: Sharpening Focus in Vision Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12881)
*Peter R. D. van der Wal,Nicola Strisciuglio,George Azzopardi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出抑制性自注意力机制(ISA)，通过保留并利用负注意力分数来抑制背景干扰、增强目标相关特征的选择性，提升ViT的鲁棒性和泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准ViT的自注意力机制由于Softmax归一化仅产生正注意力值，导致注意力分散到背景区域，依赖虚假相关性而非目标相关线索，模型鲁棒性不足。

Method: 受生物视觉系统中的抑制机制启发，提出抑制性自注意力(ISA)，保留并利用负注意力分数来抑制不相关特征并增强对目标物体的聚焦，打破Softmax仅输出正值的限制。

Result: 在ImageNet-1k、COCO及多个鲁棒性基准测试中，ISA提高了目标中心选择性，减少了对捷径的依赖，改善了分布外泛化能力；相关性图显示ViT+ISA的注意力更锐利、更集中于目标区域。

Conclusion: ISA通过引入抑制信号有效提升了ViT的特征选择性和鲁棒性，使模型更可靠地关注目标相关区域而非背景干扰，代码已开源。

Abstract: Vision Transformers (ViTs) have demonstrated remarkable performance in computer vision tasks. However, their self-attention mechanism often diffuses focus across background regions, relying on spurious correlations rather than object-relevant cues. Inspired by inhibitory mechanisms observed in biological vision systems, we propose the Inhibited Self-Attention (ISA), a novel self-attention that integrates inhibitory signals to enhance feature selectivity and suppress spurious responses. In contrast to conventional self-attention, which relies solely on positive attention values due to softmax normalization, our approach retains and utilizes negative attention scores to suppress irrelevant features and sharpen focus on objects of interest. Experiments across multiple datasets, including ImageNet-1k and COCO, and several robustness benchmarks demonstrate that ISA enhances object-centric selectivity, reduces shortcut reliance, and improves out-of-distribution generalization. Our analysis of relevance maps confirms that ViTs with ISA exhibit sharper, more localized focus on object-relevant regions while reducing distractions from non-relevant (background) features, enabling more reliable models.
