We release our code at https://github.com/prdvanderwal/inhibited-self-attention

</details>


### [140] [Hy-Embodied-VLM-1.0: Efficient Physical-World Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12894)
*Ziyi Wang,Xumin Yu,Yongming Rao,Yonggen Ling,Yunheng Li,Oran Wang,Mingqi Gao,Yuchen Zhou,Yves Liang,Zuyan Liu,Yani Zhang,Rui Huang,Xiaoran Xu,Bowen Yuan,Yifu Yuan,Xu Tan,He Zhang,Yufei Huang,Shenghao Zhang,Hongsheng Wu,Han Hu,Zhengyou Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文介绍了Hy-Embodied-VLM-1.0，一个面向具身智能体的高效基础模型。该模型基于动作中心的能力分类法（包含状态理解、动作转换推理、序列与自适应推理三个维度）构建数据流程与混合数据集，采用Hy3-A3B语言主干和Hy-ViT2视觉编码器的高效MoE架构。在38个基准测试中，该模型在19个上达到同类最优，平均性能比上一代提升8.4%，仅激活3B参数即可接近32B参数模型的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 构建具备多模态感知、理解以及动作推理、适应变化和物理世界交互能力的具身智能体，需要从预训练阶段就培养动作中心的能力。

Method: 提出了动作中心的能力分类法（Action-Relevant State Understanding、Action-Transition Reasoning、Sequential and Adaptive Reasoning）；基于此分类法开发系统化数据流程与预训练/后训练数据混合；采用Hy3-A3B语言主干+Hy-ViT2视觉编码器的高效MoE架构。

Result: 在38个基准测试中，19个达到同类模型最优；平均性能比Hy-Embodied-0.5 MoT-2B提升8.4%；仅激活3B参数即可接近32B参数模型的性能；在多轮交互和长程推理的具身智能任务上表现优异。

Conclusion: Hy-Embodied-VLM-1.0通过动作中心的能力分类法指导的数据策略和高效MoE架构，在极小激活参数下实现了与大规模模型可比的具身智能性能，验证了系统性培养动作推理能力的有效性。

Abstract: Building capable embodied agents requires not only multimodal perception and understanding, but also agentic capabilities for reasoning about actions, adapting to evolving situations, and interacting with the physical world. In this report, we introduce Hy-Embodied-VLM-1.0, an efficient and powerful embodied foundation model specifically designed for embodied agents operating in the physical world. To cultivate such capabilities from the pre-training stage onward, we define an action-centric capability taxonomy comprising three progressive dimensions: Action-Relevant State Understanding, Action-Transition Reasoning, and Sequential and Adaptive Reasoning. Guided by this taxonomy, we develop a systematic data pipeline and curate data mixtures spanning both pre-training and post-training. To deliver strong physical-world understanding and interaction capabilities while supporting latency-sensitive deployment, we build our model on the Hy3-A3B language backbone and the Hy-ViT2 vision encoder. Its efficient Mixture-of-Experts architecture combines strong model capacity with high inference efficiency. We evaluate Hy-Embodied-VLM-1.0 on a comprehensive suite of 38 benchmarks covering embodied perception, physical-world understanding, and embodied reasoning. The model achieves the best performance among similarly sized models on 19 of the 38 benchmarks and substantially outperforms strong competitors, including Qwen3.6-A3B and Cosmos 3. Compared with the previous-generation Hy-Embodied-0.5 MoT-2B, Hy-Embodied-VLM-1.0 improves average performance by 8.4%. Despite activating only 3B parameters, it achieves performance close to that of the previous-generation model with 32B activated parameters. Beyond static benchmark evaluation, Hy-Embodied-VLM-1.0 also demonstrates strong performance on embodied agentic tasks requiring multi-turn interaction and long-horizon reasoning.

</details>


### [141] [UniMedSeg: Unified In-Context Learning for Multi-Paradigm 2D/3D Medical Image Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12896)
*Yunzhou Li,Jiesi Hu,Yanwu Yang,Hanyang Peng,Chenfei Ye,Jianfeng Cao,Yixuan Yuan,Ting Ma*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: UniMedSeg提出了一种基于Transformer的统一医学图像分割框架，通过将视觉样例、几何交互、语言指令以及2D/3D图像映射到共享序列空间，利用解耦分裂注意力机制实现线性复杂度，在27个公开数据集上训练，无需任务特定微调即可在视觉上下文学习、交互式分割和语言引导分割上达到SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有医学图像分割基础模型因提示范式（视觉上下文学习、交互式分割、语言引导分割）和空间维度（2D vs 3D）不同而碎片化，导致异构标注和数据无法被单个可扩展模型联合吸收，且限制了跨范式知识迁移。

Method: 提出UniMedSeg框架，核心是将视觉示例、几何交互、语言指令和2D/3D图像统一映射到共享序列空间，通过统一上下文学习接口实现异构医学监督的联合训练；引入解耦分裂注意力（Decoupled Split Attention）机制，将注意力复杂度降至线性，同时保持硬件友好的计算效率和聚焦的上下文-目标交互。

Result: 在由27个公开数据集构建的大规模语料上训练和评估，UniMedSeg在视觉上下文学习、交互式分割和语言引导分割三类任务上均达到SOTA性能，且无需任务特定微调，在多样化的保留任务上展现出强泛化能力。

Conclusion: UniMedSeg通过统一的序列化建模思路有效解决了医学图像分割中提示范式和空间维度的碎片化问题，验证了单一可扩展模型联合学习异构医学监督的可行性，为医学图像分割基础模型提供了一种通用范式。

Abstract: Medical image segmentation foundation models are expected to generalize across diverse clinical scenarios, yet existing universal methods remain fragmented by prompt paradigms and spatial dimensions. Visual in-context learning, interactive segmentation, and language-guided segmentation are typically handled by paradigm-specific models, while 2D and 3D images are also modeled separately. Such isolation prevents heterogeneous annotations and data from being jointly absorbed by a single scalable model and limits cross-paradigm knowledge transfer. To address this bottleneck, we propose UniMedSeg, a Transformer-centric universal segmentation framework that maps visual examples, geometric interactions, language instructions, and 2D/3D images into a shared sequence space, enabling heterogeneous medical supervision to be jointly learned through a unified in-context interface without prompt- or dimension-specific branches. To overcome the long-sequence memory bottleneck caused by visual contexts, we introduce Decoupled Split Attention, which reduces attention complexity to linear while preserving hardware-friendly computation and focused context-target interaction. Extensively trained and evaluated on a large corpus curated from 27 public datasets, UniMedSeg achieves state-of-the-art performance across visual in-context, interactive, and language-guided segmentation without task-specific fine-tuning, demonstrating strong generalization on diverse held-out tasks. The code and model weights are publicly available at https://github.com/Lii1228/UniMedSeg

</details>


### [142] [Rank-1 Identity Consensus Predicts Gallery Enrollment in 1:N Face Matching More Accurately than Score Thresholding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12903)
*Gabriella Pangelinan,Aman Bhatta,Michael C. King,Kevin W. Bowyer*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出并验证了1-consistency方法（基于多个独立训练的人脸识别器返回的top-1身份是否一致来判断样本是否在库中），在36种（图库、探针质量）场景下与两种分数阈值方法对比。结果表明：1-consistency无需预设阈值，在探针质量退化时能达到Oracle阈值方法的精度，且在标记为库内时正确率高达97-100%，远优于阈值方法的66-84%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 1:N人脸识别中，判断探针是否在注册库内至关重要，但误判代价不对称（拒真丢失线索，认假导致错误识别甚至冤案）。现有分数阈值方法受图像质量和图库规模影响大，部署前固定阈值在真实场景下脆弱，亟需一种无需预先知道探测条件且鲁棒的决策方法。

Method: 采用1-consistency方法：使用多个独立训练的人脸识别器，只有当所有识别器返回的rank-1身份一致时，才将探针判定为库内（mate-present）。在36种不同（图库结构、探针质量）场景下进行压力测试，并与Fixed Score-Thresholding（固定阈值）和Oracle Score-Thresholding（每场景重新调优的阈值上限）进行比较。

Result: 1）Fixed Score-Thresholding在质量退化时表现不对称崩塌：MP召回率降至2%以下，MA召回率仍接近100%。2）Oracle Score-Thresholding虽为理论上最优阈值，但1-consistency在退化探针上无需调优即可匹及其精度。3）1-consistency在标记为库内时正确率97-100%，Oracle Score-Thresholding仅为66-84%。

Conclusion: 1-consistency无需预设阈值和预先知道探测条件，在现实复杂场景下能达到Oracle级别的精度，尤其在高代价的认假错误上具有显著优势，是一种实际可用的鲁棒人脸识别决策方法。

Abstract: In operational 1:N face identification, a crucial question arises for each probe: is this person enrolled in the gallery or not? The stakes are high and asymmetric. Rejecting a mate-present (MP) probe loses a valid lead; accepting a mate-absent (MA) probe makes every returned candidate a false identification, at worst a wrongful arrest. Most approaches threshold match scores, but scores shift substantially with image quality and gallery size and composition, making thresholds fixed before deployment brittle under realistic conditions. Our prior work introduced 1-consistency, the only method based on rank consensus across multiple independently trained matchers: a probe is labeled MP if all matchers return the same rank-1 identity. This work stress-tests 1-consistency across 36 (gallery, probe quality) scenarios spanning four quality levels and two structural axes: images per identity and total enrolled identities. We benchmark against two score-thresholding methods that bracket what any deployed threshold could achieve. Fixed Score-Thresholding (FST), calibrated once on baseline conditions, collapses asymmetrically as quality degrades: MP recall falls below 2% while MA recall holds near 100%. Oracle Score-Thresholding (OST), re-tuned per scenario, is the best any threshold could theoretically do, yet for degraded probes 1-consistency matches it with zero tuning. The two differ mainly in error type (OST favors MP recall, 1-consistency favors MA recall), but on one axis 1-consistency does not merely match the oracle: when it labels a probe MP, it returns the correct mate 97-100% of the time versus OST's 66-84% under severe degradation. In short, 1-consistency delivers oracle-level accuracy without the impossible requirement: it sets no threshold, so it needs no advance knowledge of the conditions a probe will arrive in, which is what makes it usable.

</details>


### [143] [Open-KNEAD: Knowledge-grounded Nutrition Estimation via Agentic Decomposition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12911)
*Bruce Coburn,Jingbo Yue,Jinge Ma,Siddeshwar Raghavan,Gautham Vinod,Fengqing Zhu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Open-KNEAD是一个无需训练、可本地部署的知识驱动智能体框架，用于从单张未标注的餐食图像进行营养素估计。它通过将每个分解的食物项基于营养知识检索匹配到FNDDS数据库，在保持可审计记录和隐私保护的同时，显著提升了份量估计的准确性。实验表明，该框架在多个骨干模型和菜系上超越了直接估计和先前检索增强方法，尤其在营养师验证的ACETADA数据集上，本地开源模型比两个前沿闭源模型的直接份量估计提升了约30%和53%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虽然先前研究认为检索增强接地（retrieval-augmented grounding）能提升多模态大模型（MLLM）的营养估计，但现代MLLM的直接估计已能匹配或超越完整的检索流程。这引出一个问题：如果检索不再改善整体估计，它是否仍能为临床提供准确的份量估计和可追溯的逐项记录？本文旨在探索一种既保留检索在可审计性和可解释性上的优势，又能提升份量估计准确性的方法，同时满足临床采纳所需的低用户负担、可解释性和隐私保护。

Method: 提出Open-KNEAD框架：一个无需训练、可本地部署的知识驱动智能体框架。方法包括：(1) 将餐食图像分解为单个食物项；(2) 通过选择性、营养感知的检索将每个食物项匹配到FNDDS数据库代码；(3) 构建可审计的逐项记录；(4) 引入智能体内部的食谱先验步骤（recipe-prior），用于恢复烹饪中添加的非视觉能量（cooking-added energy），以纠偏非美国菜系的估计。整个流程在本地硬件上运行，无需用户提供额外标注。

Result: 在两种开源MLLM家族和三种菜系上，Open-KNEAD在大多数骨干-数据集设置下优于先前的接地方法和直接估计。在营养师验证的ACETADA数据集上，本地开源代理的份量估计比两个前沿闭源模型（直接估计）分别提升了约30%和53%。食谱先验步骤有效恢复了非美国菜系中烹饪添加的隐形能量偏差。

Conclusion: 检索增强接地在现代MLLM中依然有独特价值——它并非为了提升整体估计（因为直接估计已足够好），而是为了提供准确的份量估计和可审计的逐项记录，这正是临床实践所看重的。Open-KNEAD通过知识驱动的智能体框架实现了训练免费、本地部署、隐私保护且可解释的餐食营养评估，为临床采纳提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) are increasingly used for dietary assessment from meal images, where retrieval-augmented grounding was shown to sharpen nutrition estimates. However, we find this premise no longer holds for current MLLMs. A modern MLLM's direct estimate now matches or surpasses the full retrieval pipeline. This raises a question: if retrieval no longer improves the overall estimate, can it still deliver the two things clinicians value, accurate portions and a traceable, item-by-item record? We pursue this while preserving what matters for clinical adoption: minimal user burden (a single, unannotated meal image), explainability (an auditable record), and privacy (locally hosted inference). We introduce Open-KNEAD, a knowledge-grounded agentic framework for meal nutrition estimation that is training-free and locally deployable. Each decomposed food item is grounded to a Food and Nutrient Database for Dietary Studies (FNDDS) code via selective, nutrient-aware retrieval, composing an auditable per-item record. Across two open MLLM families and three cuisines, Open-KNEAD improves portion estimates over both prior grounding methods and direct estimation in most backbone-dataset settings. An agent-internal recipe-prior step further recovers the invisible cooking-added energy that biases estimates on non-US cuisine. The advantage is largest on the dietitian-verified ACETADA dataset, where the local open agent surpasses the direct portion estimates of two frontier closed models by roughly $30\%$ and $53\%$, all while keeping every meal image on local hardware. We release the Open-KNEAD framework and its agent-ready FNDDS knowledge base.

</details>


### [144] [Domain-Incremental Remote Sensing Change Detection via Difference-Guided Adaptation and Frequency-Decoupled Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12934)
*Daifeng Peng,Yaning Li,Haiyan Guan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出DG-FDD框架，通过差异引导动态适配器和频率解耦知识蒸馏策略，解决遥感变化检测模型在域增量学习中的灾难性遗忘问题，在多个数据集上取得了接近单任务独立训练的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有域增量学习方法主要保留图像级表征，忽视了双时相差异线索（bitemporal discrepancy cues），而这些线索对鲁棒的域偏移变化检测至关重要。

Method: 提出DG-FDD框架，包含两个核心组件：1）差异引导动态适配器（DGDA），建模双时相特征差异以促进变化感知的特征适配并减少域特定干扰；2）频率解耦知识蒸馏策略（FDKD-CS），在频域中将结构信息与域风格分离，实现无需历史数据的稳定知识迁移。

Result: 在三个高分辨率RSCD数据集的二域和三域增量协议下，相比独立训练的単任务模型，F1和IoU的平均相对变化在二域序列中仅为-0.23%和-0.45%，在三域序列中为-0.69%和-1.31%。

Conclusion: DG-FDD有效缓解了灾难性遗忘，在历史知识保留与新域适配之间取得了良好的稳定性-可塑性平衡。

Abstract: Remote sensing change detection (RSCD) models are prone to catastrophic forgetting when incrementally adapted to new domains. Existing domain-incremental learning (DIL) methods mainly preserve image-level representations but often overlook bitemporal discrepancy cues, which are critical for robust change detection under domain shifts. To address this limitation, we propose DG-FDD, a domain-incremental change detection framework that integrates Difference-Guided Adaptation and Frequency-Decoupled Distillation. Specifically, the Difference-Guided Dynamic Adapter (DGDA) models bitemporal feature discrepancies to promote change-aware feature adaptation and reduce domain-specific interference. Meanwhile, the Frequency-Decoupled Knowledge Distillation strategy with Cross-domain Synthesis (FDKD-CS) separates structural information from domain style in the frequency domain, enabling stable knowledge transfer without historical data. Extensive experiments on three public high-resolution RSCD datasets under two- and three-domain incremental protocols demonstrate that DG-FDD effectively mitigates catastrophic forgetting. Compared with independently trained single-task models, DG-FDD records mean relative changes in F1 and IoU of only -0.23% and -0.45%, respectively, across six two-domain sequences, and -0.69% and -1.31%, respectively, across the three evaluated three-domain sequences. These results indicate a favorable stability-plasticity balance between historical knowledge retention and new-domain adaptation in continual cross-domain change detection.

</details>


### [145] [Point Tracking in Surgery--The 2025 Surgical Tattoos in Infrared Challenge (STIRC2025)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12939)
*Adam Schmidt,Mert Asim Karaoglu,Zijian Wu,Jiaming Zhang,Yuxin Chen,Tim Salcudean,Ho-Gun Ha,Minkang Jang,Kyungmin Jung,Ihsan Ullah,Hyunki Lee,Suresh Guttikonda,Sarah Latus,Alexander Schlaefer,Xinkai Zhao,Yuichiro Hayashi,Masahiro Oda,Takayuki Kitasaka,Kensaku Mori,Peng Liu,Chenyang Li,Stefanie Speidel,Aoife Gardiner,Agostino Stilli,Danail Stoyanov,Francisco Vasconcelos,Anwesa Choudhuri,Meng Zheng,Zhongpai Gao,Benjamin Planche,Van Nguyen Nguyen,Terrence Chen,Ziyan Wu,Alexander Ladikos,Omid Mohareri*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文介绍了STIR Challenge 2025（STIRC2025），一个针对手术中点跟踪的挑战赛，使用STIR数据集评估算法在准确性和效率方面的表现，并总结了7支参赛队伍的方法与结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 手术中的点跟踪对于下游任务（如分割、3D重建、虚拟组织标记、自主探头扫描和子任务自主性）至关重要，但目前缺乏统一的评估基准。

Method: 组织MICCAI EndoVis 2025框架下的挑战赛，使用STIR数据集，分别从准确性（体内和体外序列）和效率（推理延迟）两个维度评估参赛算法。

Result: 7支队伍参与了挑战，论文总结了各参赛方法的性能表现及排名结果。

Conclusion: STIRC2025为手术点跟踪提供了标准化的评估平台，相关数据集和基准代码已公开，有助于推动该领域的进一步发展。

Abstract: Point tracking in surgery is crucial to enable applications in downstream tasks such as segmentation, 3D reconstruction, virtual tissue landmarking, autonomous probe-based scanning, and subtask autonomy. This paper introduces the 2025 iteration of a point tracking challenge to address this, wherein participants submit their algorithms for quantification. Their algorithms are evaluated using a dataset named surgical tattoos in infrared (STIR), with the challenge named the STIR Challenge 2025 (STIRC2025). The STIR Challenge 2025 comprises two quantitative components: accuracy and efficiency. The accuracy component tests the accuracy of algorithms on in vivo and ex vivo sequences. The efficiency component tests algorithm inference latency. The challenge was conducted as a part of MICCAI EndoVis 2025, and seven teams participated in this challenge. In this paper we summarize the challenge results and participant methods. The challenge dataset is available at: https://zenodo.org/records/20191078, and the code for baseline models and metrics calculation is available here: https://github.com/athaddius/STIRMetrics

</details>


### [146] [ViCo3D: Empowering LiDAR-based Collaborative 3D Object Detection with Vision Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12959)
*Haojie Ren,Songrui Luo,Lingfeng Wang,Yan Xia,Yao Li,Jing Li,Lu Zhang,Jiajun Deng,Yanyong Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出ViCo3D框架，通过将视觉基础模型（VFM）DINOv2适配到LiDAR点云的BEV表示中，增强V2X协同3D检测性能，在DAIR-V2X和V2XSet上达到SOTA，协同增益较先前方法提升达1.8倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于BEV的LiDAR协同感知方法从零训练，得到的BEV表示以几何信息为主，缺乏通用语义先验，限制了特征级协同的效果。而视觉基础模型（VFM）在大规模图像数据上预训练，具备丰富的通用视觉表征能力，但如何将其适配到LiDAR点云领域存在显著的模态差距。

Method: 1) 将点云投影到BEV平面转化为三通道图像，使DINOv2能够从LiDAR输入中提取BEV空间视觉特征；2) 设计多尺度BEV融合模块，将DINOv2特征与LiDAR几何特征在单智能体编码器中有效整合；3) 采用以自我为中心（ego-centric）的跨智能体融合策略聚合多车互补信息。

Result: 在DAIR-V2X和V2XSet数据集上达到SOTA 3D检测性能，在DAIR-V2X上协同增益相较先前方法提升高达1.8倍。

Conclusion: ViCo3D成功将视觉基础模型适配到LiDAR协同感知任务中，有效弥合了图像-点云模态差距，为利用大规模预训练视觉模型提升3D协同检测提供了一种有效方案。

Abstract: LiDAR-based collaborative 3D perception in Vehicle-to-Everything (V2X) systems typically relies on fusing bird's-eye-view (BEV) features across agents. However, current BEV representations, typically extracted by LiDAR backbones trained from scratch, are geometry-dominated and lack general semantic priors, inherently limiting the efficacy of feature-level collaboration. Meanwhile, vision foundation models (VFMs) pretrained on large-scale image data have demonstrated strong capability in learning general-purpose and informative visual representations for 2D tasks, and have the potential to enhance agent-wise LiDAR BEV representations for collaboration. Despite this potential, adapting VFMs to LiDAR-based 3D detection remains challenging due to the substantial image-point cloud modality gap. To bridge this gap, we propose ViCo3D, a collaborative 3D object detection framework powered by VFMs. Specifically, ViCo3D adapts VFMs to LiDAR-based collaborative perception from three aspects: First, ViCo3D projects point clouds onto the BEV plane as three-channel images, enabling DINOv2 to extract BEV-space visual features from LiDAR inputs. Besides, to effectively integrate these DINOv2-derived features with LiDAR geometric features, ViCo3D introduces a multi-scale BEV fusion module within the single-agent encoder. In addition, ViCo3D adopts an ego-centric cross-agent fusion strategy to aggregate complementary information from multiple agents. Experiments on DAIR-V2X and V2XSet demonstrate that ViCo3D achieves state-of-the-art 3D detection performance. Remarkably, it delivers up to 1.8x greater collaborative gains than prior methods on DAIR-V2X. The code will be made public available for future investigation.

</details>


### [147] [Controllable Generation of Diverse Dermatological Imagery for Fair and Efficient Malignancy Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12987)
*Héctor Carrión,Narges Norouzi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出cgDDI混合框架，通过可控生成多样化皮肤病图像（合成健康皮肤、罕见病变迁移、少样本参数化生成），将656张图像扩展400倍以上，在DDI基准上达到90.9%恶性分类准确率和领先公平性指标，跨数据集在F17k上提升13.9%准确率，并开源26.6万+合成图像。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 皮肤病诊断在不同人群间存在公平性差距，而缺乏专家标注的多样化图像（尤其是深肤色和罕见病）阻碍了公平方法的进展。

Method: 提出cgDDI混合框架，包含三个核心组件：(1)合成真实健康皮肤样本而不干扰其他输入属性；(2)将单样本罕见病变非参数化映射到新肤色和新位置；(3)支持仅需10个训练样本的高效参数化生成。支持人力和自动分割掩码，可扩展到无预制作掩码的数据集。

Result: 在DDI基准上，纯合成训练达86.4%恶性分类准确率，真实数据微调后达90.9%的最优性能及领先公平性指标。跨数据集实验在F17k上提升13.9%准确率。

Conclusion: cgDDI通过可控数据生成有效缓解了皮肤病数据多样性和公平性问题，开源资源可进一步支持公平性研究。

Abstract: Accurate dermatological diagnosis naturally necessitates equitable performance across diverse populations, yet a systematic lack of expertly annotated images, especially for underrepresented skin tones and rare diseases, impedes progress toward measurably fair methods. We introduce cgDDI (Controllable Generation of Diverse Dermatological Imagery), a hybrid framework that (1) synthesizes realistic healthy skin samples without disturbing other input properties, (2) maps single-sample rare lesions onto novel skin-tones and locations non-parametrically, and (3) allows for efficient parametric generation with as few as 10 training samples. The framework supports both human and automated segmentation masking, enabling scalability to datasets without pre-made lesion masks. We grow a 656-image dataset by more than 400x and validate across two datasets: biopsy-confirmed Diverse Dermatology Images (DDI) and expert-verified Fitzpatrick17k (F17k). On the DDI benchmark, we achieve malignancy classification accuracy of 86.4% under synthetic-only training and 90.9% state-of-the-art performance with real data fine-tuning, alongside leading fairness metrics. Cross-dataset experiments show +13.9% accuracy improvements on unseen F17k data despite minimal disease overlap. We openly release 266k+ synthetic images, code, and generative models to further support fairness research at https://github.com/hectorcarrion/ControllableGenDDI.

</details>


### [148] [X-Lens: Real-Time Metric Depth Estimation with Heterogeneous Cameras](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12993)
*Heng Zhou,Shuhong Liu,Yonghao He,Bohao Zhang,Fa Fu,Chenhui Hou,Xianbao Hou,Lijun Han,Wei Sui*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: X-lens是一个前馈式紧凑模型，支持从可变数量的鱼眼和针孔校准视图进行度量深度估计。通过可学习校准令牌和雅可比参数化畸变偏置，实现异构相机间的几何一致性，仅用0.04B参数即可达41 FPS，在OmniScene-Full上降低AbsRel 25.4%且参数量减少88.9%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有度量深度估计方法多针对单一相机模型（如针孔或鱼眼），难以高效支持实时下游感知任务中异构相机系统的混合使用，且通常依赖辅助重建目标导致计算复杂。

Method: 提出X-lens模型，包含两个关键组件：(1) 可学习校准令牌，实现鱼眼与针孔投影空间的粗略对齐；(2) 雅可比参数化畸变偏置，注入交叉注意力机制中，建模局部投影变化并促进跨相机一致性。模型直接预测密集深度和全局度量尺度，避免辅助重建目标。

Result: 仅0.04B参数，达41 FPS；在OmniScene-Full上AbsRel降低25.4%，参数量减少88.9%；在鱼眼专用和针孔专用设置上均具竞争力。

Conclusion: X-lens证明了轻量级模型在异构相机系统上进行高精度度量深度估计的可行性，尤其适合资源受限的实时感知场景。

Abstract: We present X-lens, a compact feed-forward model for metric depth estimation from a variable number of calibrated fisheye and pinhole views. To support real-time downstream perception, X-lens is built around a geometry-aware heterogeneous camera formulation with two key components. Learnable calibration tokens provide a coarse alignment between fisheye and pinhole projective spaces, while a Jacobian-parameterized distortion bias injected into cross-attention models local projection changes and promotes cross-camera consistency, enabling robust generalization with only 0.04B parameters and up to 41 FPS. The model predicts dense depth together with a global metric scale, avoiding auxiliary reconstruction targets that increase computation and optimization complexity. To learn such cross-camera generalization at scale and depth, X-lens is trained on multiple public datasets and OmniScene, our newly released large-scale synthetic dataset containing approximately 266K synchronized six-view frames, 1.7M individual images, and 103 indoor and outdoor scenes. Extensive experiments on both real-world and synthetic indoor and outdoor datasets demonstrate superior heterogeneous-camera metric depth accuracy, reducing AbsRel by 25.4\% on OmniScene-Full over the strongest baseline while using 88.9\% fewer parameters, with competitive performance on conventional fisheye-only and pinhole-only settings.

</details>


### [149] [DermDepth: Toward Monocular Metric Scale 3D Reconstruction Models for Dermatology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13010)
*Héctor Carrión,Narges Norouzi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出DermDepth，首个用于皮肤病学领域的单视角度量级3D重建模型，以及合成皮肤镜数据集D-Synth。训练于D-Synth的模型将真实皮肤镜数据的度量尺度误差从16倍以上降至1.1倍以下，并在多个真实世界基准上实现跨尺度、跨肤色的泛化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 皮肤病学实践中，病变大小、形态和纹理的测量与追踪对于伤口或皮肤癌筛查至关重要，但现有研究过度聚焦于2D方法，而3D信息能提供更丰富的诊断依据。缺乏无需额外硬件的单视图度量级3D重建方法。

Method: 提出DermDepth单视图度量级3D模型，并构建D-Synth合成皮肤镜数据集（含像素级完美3D标签）。在合成数据上训练后，通过少量真实临床样本微调实现领域泛化。

Result: D-Synth训练将真实皮肤镜数据的度量尺度误差从16倍以上修正到1.1倍以下，同时保持几何质量并提升纹理丰富度。微调后在三个真实基准上泛化良好，涵盖毫米到厘米级、多种肤色和慢性伤口病例，测量结果与医学文献一致。

Conclusion: 证明了无需额外硬件或多次采集，仅凭单视图即可实现皮肤镜和宏观图像的密集度量级3D重建，为皮肤病学诊断提供了实用且可泛化的3D解决方案。

Abstract: Dermatological practice routinely involves measuring and tracking lesion size, morphology and texture, as critical components of wound or skin cancer screening, monitoring and diagnosis. To accomplish this task, practitioners often image the skin surface with commonly available off-the-shelf camera sensors. This has led to an overwhelming research focus on 2D methods while these objectives naturally benefit from 3D information. In this paper, we demonstrate that dense monocular 3D reconstructions, metric scale measurements and rich surface normal texture estimates are achievable for both dermoscopic and macroscopic cases without the need for additional hardware or multiple captures. We present DermDepth, the first single-view metric scale 3D model for the dermatological domain and D-Synth, the first synthetic dermoscopic dataset with pixel-perfect 3D information. Our experiments show training DermDepth on D-Synth corrects metric scale error from over 16x to under 1.1x for real dermoscopic data, while preserving geometric quality and increasing texture richness. Fine-tuning on a small amount of real clinical samples generalizes our method across three real-world benchmarks spanning the few mm to hundred cm range, diverse skin-tones, chronic wound cases and produces measurements broadly consistent with disease size reported in medical literature. All code, data and models are available at https://github.com/hectorcarrion/dermdepth.

</details>


<div id='cs.CR'></div>

# cs.CR [[Back]](#toc)

### [150] [Cross-Cutting Security Analysis of LLM-Generated Code via Metamorphic Testing and Association Rule Mining](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12089)
*Zedong Peng,Chenggang Wang,Shangyue Zhu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出结合安全导向的蜕变关系（MRs）与关联规则（AR）挖掘的框架，用于检测LLM生成代码中的漏洞、揭示其跨领域违规结构，并追溯至提示级别的风险因素。对5个开源模型生成的3700个代码片段分析显示，68.8%违反至少一个MR；AR挖掘发现强跨领域违规模式（如XSS与弱密码学共同违规以82.5%置信度预测硬编码凭证）；数据库和认证相关提示是跨领域不安全性的强预测因子。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM生成的代码频繁存在安全漏洞，且这些漏洞往往跨多个关注领域（cross-cutting concern），反映了安全的横切性质。现有工作多孤立检测单一漏洞类型，缺乏对漏洞间关联结构及提示层面风险因素的系统理解。

Method: 定义9个安全导向的蜕变关系（覆盖SQL注入、XSS、命令注入、路径遍历、硬编码凭证、弱密码学、内存安全等CWE类别），通过LLM裁判对LLMSecEval基准中5个开源模型的3700个代码片段进行测试；应用关联规则挖掘检测违规间的共现模式；进行提示级风险分析。

Result: 68.8%的片段违反至少一个MR；硬编码凭证（79.1%）和命令注入（74.4%）是最普遍的违规类型；XSS与弱密码学共同违规以82.5%置信度（提升度3.23）预测硬编码凭证；数据库和认证相关提示是跨领域不安全性的强预测因子；65.5%的提示在所有5个模型上产生一致的违规结果。

Conclusion: 不安全的代码生成不是独立缺陷的集合，而是一种结构化的、由提示条件驱动的现象。这启发了聚类感知的验证方法和提示级别的干预策略，以实现更安全的LLM辅助编程。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) frequently generate code with security vulnerabilities, yet these weaknesses are rarely isolated: they often span multiple concern areas simultaneously, reflecting the cross-cutting nature of security in software. We present a framework that combines security-oriented Metamorphic Relations (MRs) with Association Rule (AR) mining to detect vulnerabilities in LLM-generated code, uncover their co-violation structure, and trace that structure back to prompt-level risk factors. We define nine MRs covering major CWE categories, including SQL injection, XSS, command injection, path traversal, hard-coded credentials, weak cryptography, and memory-safety errors, and apply them using an LLM-based judge to 3,700 code snippets generated by five open models from the LLMSecEval benchmark. The results show that 68.8% of snippets violate at least one MR, with hard-coded credentials (79.1%) and command injection (74.4%) among the most prevalent applicable failures. AR mining reveals strong cross-cutting co-violation patterns, notably that XSS and weak cryptography co-violations predict hard-coded credentials with 82.5% confidence (lift = 3.23), along with tightly coupled clusters linking authentication, credential handling, and cryptographic weakness, as well as input-handling and memory-safety failures. We then perform prompt-level risk analysis and find that database- and authentication-related prompts are strong predictors of broad cross-cutting insecurity, while 65.5% of prompts yield consistent violation outcomes across all five models. These findings show that insecure code generation is not merely a collection of independent defects, but a structured and prompt-conditioned phenomenon, motivating cluster-aware verification and prompt-level intervention for safer LLM-assisted programming.

</details>


### [151] [MindReader: Using LLMs to Encourage Memorable and Secure Password Replacement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12148)
*Anna Gerchanovsky,Lujo Bauer,Michael K. Reiter*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出MindReader工具，利用大语言模型帮助用户在更换密码时生成既容易记忆又难以被攻击者猜测的密码变体。用户研究显示，MindReader生成的替换密码比用户自行替换的密码和原始密码更安全，在在线攻击场景下更难被破解，同时记忆性与原始密码相当。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 用户在被要求更换密码时，往往会选择原始密码的可预测变体，这些变体容易被攻击者猜到。现有工具缺乏引导用户生成既安全又易记的替换密码的能力。

Method: MindReader利用LLM推断原始密码各组成部分的语义含义，然后建议语义相关但语法无关的组件来构建新密码。通过用户研究评估密码的安全性和记忆性。

Result: MindReader生成的替换密码比用户自行创建的替换密码和原始密码更安全，即使攻击者知道原始密码和工具实现细节也难以猜测。一周后的登录成功率显示其记忆性与替换密码和原始密码相当。

Conclusion: LLM辅助的密码替换方法可以有效平衡安全性和可记忆性，帮助用户生成抵抗在线攻击的强密码，且不影响用户体验。

Abstract: We report on the design and evaluation of MindReader, a tool that helps a user replace her password when she is required to do so. Left to their own devices, users tend to replace their previous passwords with predictable variations of the original ones. MindReader leverages LLMs to suggest password variations that are chosen to be easy for the user to remember but harder for an attacker to predict. To do this, MindReader infers the meaning behind original password components and then suggests semantically related (yet syntactically unrelated) components for the new password. In a user study, passwords created using MindReader were more secure than both replacement passwords created without using MindReader and original passwords. In particular, MindReader replacement passwords were harder to guess in an online attack than alternative replacement passwords even by an attacker with knowledge of the original password and full knowledge of the tool implementation. Passwords created with MindReader were also comparably memorable to alternative replacement passwords and original passwords, as measured by the ability of users to successfully log in a week after creating their password.

</details>


### [152] [MTD-Playground: An Attacker-Aware Evaluation Framework for Network Moving Target Defense](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12199)
*Mohammad Farhad,Mohoshin Ara Tahera,Padam Jung Thapa,Shuvalaxmi Dass,Bhupendra Acharya*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出MTD-Playground，一个面向攻击者的评估框架，用于对SDN使能的路径随机化（PR-MTD）技术在真实企业级多阶段攻击场景下进行基准测试。采用复合评估方法分析部署有效性、突变间隔权衡及攻防平衡，实验表明激进突变间隔可将攻击成功率降至4-20%，攻击完成时间增至160-311s，同时吞吐量提升30.9%，路径延迟降低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MTD评估因攻击者假设、攻击场景和评估指标不一致而碎片化，缺乏可重复性和面向部署的对比，亟需一个统一、攻击者感知的评估框架来系统衡量SDN路径随机化MTD技术的实际效能。

Method: 提出MTD-Playground框架，引入复合评估方法分析部署有效性、突变间隔权衡和攻防平衡；以周期性路径随机化为代表策略，在企业级多阶段攻击场景下进行实验评估。

Result: 激进突变间隔将攻击成功率降至4-20%，攻击完成时间增至160-311s；PR-MTD提升吞吐量最高30.9%，降低内部路径延迟，且短突变间隔持续获得最高部署有效性和正向防御优势。

Conclusion: 基于SDN的PR-MTD能够在企业环境中实际部署，并有效破坏多阶段攻击进程。

Abstract: Moving Target Defense (MTD) has emerged as a proactive network cyber defense paradigm that increases attacker uncertainty through dynamic network reconfiguration techniques such as Software-Defined Networking (SDN)-enabled path randomization. However, existing evaluations remain fragmented due to inconsistent attacker assumptions, attack scenarios, and evaluation metrics, limiting reproducibility and deployment-oriented comparison. In this paper, we present MTD-Playground, an attacker-aware evaluation framework for benchmarking SDN-enabled path-randomization (PR) MTD techniques under realistic enterprise-style multi-stage attack scenarios. Beyond isolated security and performance metrics, MTD-Playground introduces a composite evaluation methodology for analyzing deployment effectiveness, mutation-interval trade-offs, and defender-attacker operational balance. Using periodic path randomization as a representative PR-MTD strategy, our evaluation shows that aggressive mutation intervals reduce attack success rates to 4-20% while increasing attack completion time to 160-311s across evaluated attack scenarios. At the same time, PR-MTD improves throughput by up to 30.9% and reduces internal-path latency without service interruption. Composite analysis further shows that shorter mutation intervals consistently achieve the highest deployment effectiveness and positive defender advantage. These results demonstrate that SDN-based PR-MTD can substantially disrupt multi-stage attack progression while remaining practically deployable in enterprise environments.

</details>


### [153] [Explaining Intrusion Alert Decisions of Deep Learning-based Network Intrusion Detection Systems for Security Analysts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12203)
*Ayush Kumar,Vrizlynn L. L. Thing*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出EXP-SEC框架，用于解释基于深度学习的网络入侵检测系统（DL-based NIDS）的告警决策。该框架包含取证模块、解释模块和多阶段映射模块，能够生成符合安全分析师领域知识的可解释性结果。实验表明，EXP-SEC在组级和重叠感知的解释效用指标上优于现有最佳方法xNIDS。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的DL-based NIDS虽检测性能优越，但其决策过程缺乏可解释性，难以被安全运营中心（SOC）的分析师理解和信任。现有解释方法（如xNIDS）无法有效处理网络流量中复杂的特征依赖关系（特征可划分为重叠分组，且不同分组重要性不同），也难以生成符合分析师领域知识的解释。

Method: EXP-SEC框架包含三个核心模块：(1) 取证模块——隔离引发告警的可疑数据包/流；(2) 解释模块——处理复杂特征依赖关系，支持重叠特征分组并区分组重要程度；(3) 多阶段映射模块——将基于特征/分组的解释转化为面向安全分析师的领域特定解释。

Result: 在与现有最佳解释框架xNIDS的对比评估中，EXP-SEC在组级和重叠感知的解释效用指标上表现更优，在描述准确性、稀疏性和稳定性等传统特征级指标上性能相当。此外，以最先进的DL-based NIDS为例，展示了EXP-SEC生成的安全分析师友好型解释格式。

Conclusion: EXP-SEC是一种能够更好对齐安全分析师领域知识的NIDS解释框架，特别擅长处理网络流量中复杂的重叠特征分组依赖关系，生成的解释格式更易于SOC分析师理解和使用。

Abstract: In this paper, we present EXP-SEC, a novel framework which can explain the intrusion detection decisions of DL-based NIDS (which lead to security alerts) in a way that is aligned with the domain knowledge of analysts working in Security Operations Center (SOC). We highlight the following features of our framework: (1) a forensic module that isolates the suspect packets/flow which likely caused an alert (2) an explanation module which can handle much more complex feature dependencies in network traffic than existing methods (features can be divided into overlapping groups and some groups are more important than others), and (3) a multi-stage mapping module which translates the feature/group-based explanations generated by explanation module to domain-specific explanations suitable for processing by security analysts. We evaluate EXP-SEC with state-of-the-art DL-based NIDS and our evaluation results show that EXP-SEC outperforms xNIDS (existing best performing explanation framework) in terms of group-level and overlap-aware explanation utility metrics while performing similarly in terms of conventional feature-level metrics such as descriptive accuracy, sparsity and stability. Moreover, taking the case of a state-of-the-art DL-based NIDS, we demonstrate the security analyst-friendly explanation format generated by EXP-SEC.

</details>


### [154] [$\mathrm{P}^{3}$CDA: Privacy-Preserving and Provably Secure Cross Domain Authentication Scheme for Internet of Drones](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12288)
*Chengqi Hou,Beibei Li,Ziqing Zhu,Yang You,Licheng Wang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出P³CDA，一种面向物联网无人机（IoD）环境的隐私保护且可证明安全的跨域认证方案。该方案设计了高效假名管理机制和支持批量假名更新的增强型Merkle哈希树，基于密码累加器实现匿名认证、可追踪性与恶意无人机高效撤销，在CK adversary模型下形式化证明安全性，实验结果表明在计算、通信和存储开销上均优于现有方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着无人机物联网（IoD）的快速扩展和无人机移动性增强，跨域交互不可避免，跨域认证成为基本安全需求。现有认证方案难以同时兼顾强安全性、高效率和身份隐私，无法满足IoD环境高度动态、资源受限的严苛要求。

Method: 1) 设计高效假名管理机制，支持自适应假名生成及批量注册、验证和撤销；2) 提出结构增强的Merkle哈希树（MHT），支持批量假名更新，降低无人机假名存储开销；3) 基于密码累加器构建跨域认证协议，实现匿名认证与可追踪性，支持恶意无人机高效撤销。

Result: 在CK adversary模型下形式化证明了安全性；实验结果表明P³CDA在计算、通信和存储开销方面均低于现有方案。

Conclusion: P³CDA在IoD环境下实现了安全性、效率与隐私保护的平衡，是一种适用于高度动态、资源受限场景的跨域认证方案。

Abstract: With the rapid expansion of the Internet of Drones (IoD) and the increasing mobility of drones, cross-domain interactions among geographically distributed domains have become inevitable. Cross-domain authentication is therefore a fundamental security requirement for IoD. However, existing authentication schemes often struggle to simultaneously achieve strong security, high efficiency, and identity privacy, making them unsuitable for the stringent requirements of highly dynamic and resource-constrained IoD environments. To address this challenge, we propose $\mathrm{P}^{3}$CDA, a privacy-preserving and provably secure cross-domain authentication scheme. First, we design an efficient pseudonym management mechanism that supports adaptive pseudonym generation as well as batch registration, verification, and revocation. Second, we propose a structurally enhanced Merkle Hash Tree (MHT) that supports batch pseudonym updates, thereby reducing the pseudonym storage overhead of drones. Building on these components, we develop a cryptographic accumulator-based cross-domain authentication protocol that enables anonymous authentication with authorized pseudonyms while preserving the traceability and efficient revocation of malicious drones. We rigorously analyze the security of $\mathrm{P}^{3}$CDA and formally prove its security under the Canetti--Krawczyk (CK) adversary model. Extensive experiments demonstrate that $\mathrm{P}^{3}$CDA achieves lower computational, communication, and storage overhead than state-of-the-art schemes.

</details>


### [155] [Antiproof: Synthesizing Vulnerability Detectors and Proofs of Exploitability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12316)
*Alon Shakevsky,Corban Villa,Ion Stoica,Raluca Ada Popa*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: Antiproof是一个端到端的漏洞发现系统，结合神经符号检测器合成（高召回率）与可利用性证明预言机（自动验证），在BountyBench和KEVBench上检测出66个漏洞中的64个（召回率提升超60个百分点），并在50个广泛部署的系统中发现数百个未知漏洞，已获12个CVE分配（含多个LLM系统的远程代码执行漏洞）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有漏洞发现方法难以同时实现高召回率和可靠自动验证，且成本高昂，无法在攻击者利用前有效发现漏洞。

Method: 采用神经符号检测器合成（从漏洞数据集学习并迭代精化静态检测器）与可利用性证明预言机（验证可执行证明是否展示具体攻击者能力）相结合的端到端方法。

Result: 在BountyBench和KEVBench上检测出64/66个漏洞，召回率比基线提升超60个百分点；扫描50个广泛部署系统发现数百个未知漏洞；已获12个CVE分配，包括Ray、SGLang、vLLM、LiteLLM中的远程代码执行漏洞。

Conclusion: Antiproof有效实现了高召回率与可靠自动验证的统一，成功发现多个广泛部署系统中的关键零日漏洞，证明了该方法的实际有效性。

Abstract: Discovering vulnerabilities before attackers exploit them requires high recall and reliable automatic validation, but existing approaches struggle to achieve both without prohibitive cost. We present Antiproof, an end-to-end vulnerability discovery system that combines neuro-symbolic detector synthesis for high-recall discovery with proof-of-exploitability oracles for automatic validation. Antiproof learns and iteratively refines static detectors from vulnerability datasets, then validates candidates by verifying whether executable proofs demonstrate concrete attacker capabilities. Evaluated on BountyBench and our curated KEVBench dataset, Antiproof detects 64 of 66 vulnerabilities, improving recall by more than 60 percentage points over static-analysis and neuro-symbolic baselines. In a scan of 50 widely deployed systems, Antiproof uncovered several hundred previously unknown vulnerabilities. We are responsibly disclosing all confirmed zero-days and have received 12 CVE assignments to date, including remote code execution vulnerabilities in Ray, SGLang, vLLM, and LiteLLM that could allow attackers to take over LLM training and inference systems.

</details>


### [156] [Trust but Verify? Uncovering the Security Debt of Autonomous Coding Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12428)
*A H M Nazmus Sakib,Dipayan Banik,Murtuza Jadliwala*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文通过AIDev数据集对自主编码助手生成的PR进行大规模实证研究，发现38.9%的PR存在安全代码坏味，其中供应链完整性问题占82.3%，硬编码凭证占关键安全坏味的99.6%。人类协作者在智能体辅助工作流中引入了67.6%的真实泄露密钥，而现有自动化与人工审查流程未能检测81.1%的凭证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究主要关注自主编码智能体的功能正确性与生产力，而对其在安全方面的影响缺乏系统性的实证评估。随着编码智能体在高影响文件路径中引入安全风险的速度可能超越传统人工审查能力，亟需大规模研究来量化这些风险。

Method: 使用AIDev数据集，结合经过验证的LLM-as-a-judge框架与人工定性分析，对4,022个PR中的16,112个文件变更进行安全代码坏味的识别与分类。

Result: 38.9%的智能体生成PR包含至少一个安全坏味；供应链完整性问题占所有检测到的安全坏味的82.3%；硬编码凭证占关键严重性安全坏味的99.6%；人类协作者在智能体辅助工作流中引入了67.6%的真实泄露密钥；81.1%的凭证在集成前未被现有审查流程检测到。

Conclusion: 智能体辅助软件开发工作流存在显著安全风险，可能降低开发者的警惕性。亟需在人类-AI协作点直接部署上下文感知的安全护栏。

Abstract: The increasing adoption of autonomous coding agents accelerates software development but also introduces scoped security risks within high-impact file paths that can outpace traditional human review capacity. While prior research has primarily evaluated these systems in terms of functional correctness and productivity, this paper presents a large-scale empirical study using the AIDev dataset to systematically characterize security code smells in agent-generated pull requests (PRs). Through a combination of a validated LLM-as-a-judge framework and manual qualitative analysis, we identify and classify security misconfigurations across 16,112 file changes spanning 4,022 pull requests. Our results reveal that 38.9% of agent-generated PRs contain at least one security smell, with supply chain integrity issues accounting for 82.3% of all detected security smells. Furthermore, hard-coded credentials constitute 99.6% of all critical-severity security smells. Crucially, we find that human collaborators are responsible for introducing 67.6% of genuine leaked secrets within these agent-assisted workflows, while existing automated and human review processes fail to detect 81.1% of these credentials prior to integration. These findings highlight substantial security risks in agent-assisted software development workflows and suggest a potential reduction in developer vigilance. They also underscore the urgent need for context-aware security guardrails implemented directly at the point of human-AI collaboration.

</details>


### [157] [On the Security Implications of PQC in TLS: Handshake Exhaustion and IDS Degradation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12504)
*Lin-Fa Lee,Yi-Yu Chang,Chia-Mu Yu,Kuo-Hui Yeh*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 该研究实证评估了PQC-TLS在握手耗尽攻击下的安全影响，发现PQC放大攻击效果（CPU高负载持续时间延长88倍），并揭示现有深度学习IDS检测性能严重下降（如exosphere召回率仅约50%，HyperVision AU-ROC降至0.49）。研究贡献包括：量化分析检测盲区根因、发布PQC-DDoS混合流量数据集、开源可复现的实验代码与部署脚本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着PQC逐渐集成到TLS 1.3中，其引入的额外计算和通信开销可能在握手阶段放大TLS握手耗尽攻击的影响，导致更严重的DDoS威胁。现有研究缺乏对PQC环境下IDS检测盲区的系统量化分析。

Method: 搭建包含一台PQC-TLS服务器和十个攻击节点的实证测试平台，生成超过16.5GB混合流量数据（含合法浏览行为和高强度握手耗尽攻击），评估PQC对服务器CPU利用率和深度学习IDS检测性能的影响。

Result: PQC-TLS使服务器持续高CPU利用率的持续时间延长88倍；exosphere召回率仅约50%，HyperVision的AU-ROC降至0.49（接近随机水平）。

Conclusion: PQC环境会显著放大TLS握手耗尽攻击效果，并造成现有IDS系统存在严重检测盲区。研究开源了数据集和可复现的实验环境，以支持下一代PQC感知入侵检测系统的研发。

Abstract: Post-Quantum Cryptography (PQC) is increasingly being integrated into TLS 1.3 to enhance resilience against quantum-enabled attacks. However, the additional computational and communication overhead introduced by PQC primitives during the handshake phase may also amplify the impact of TLS handshake exhaustion attacks, leading to more severe Distributed Denial-of-Service (DDoS) threats.
  In this study, we establish an empirical testbed consisting of one PQC-enabled TLS server and ten attacking nodes, generating over 16.5 GB of mixed traffic data that includes both legitimate browsing behavior and high-intensity handshake exhaustion attacks.
  Experimental results show that PQC-TLS can prolong periods of sustained high CPU utilization on the server by up to 88 times, significantly amplifying the effectiveness of such attacks. Furthermore, we evaluate state-of-the-art deep learning-based Intrusion Detection Systems (IDS) and observe a substantial decline in attack detection performance under PQC traffic conditions.
  In particular, exosphere achieves only around 50% recall, while HyperVision's AU-ROC degrades to near-random levels (0.49), revealing critical detection blind spots in existing IDS when operating in PQC environments.
  The main contributions of this work are threefold: (1) we systematically quantify and analyze the root causes of IDS detection blind spots in PQC settings; (2) we publicly release a comprehensive PQC-DDoS hybrid traffic dataset, including precise attack timestamps and server-side resource monitoring data; and (3) we open-source all experimental code and AWS deployment scripts, enabling a fully reproducible cloud-based testing environment.
  These resources aim to support both academia and industry in developing next-generation PQC-aware intrusion detection systems.

</details>


### [158] [When Binaries Talk Back: Representation-Confusion Attacks on LLM-Assisted Reverse Engineering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12507)
*Igor Santos-Grueiro*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出逆向工程中的表示混淆攻击（RARE），即二进制文件可令提取出的数据被误认为指令或独立证据。作者构建RARE-Bench基准测试，并设计RARE-Guard防御机制，通过数据隔离、工具授权、来源溯源等控件防止攻击。实验表明，无防护时模型在35/40对抗样本中提出不安全操作；Provenance Gate可拒绝所有虚假声明（0/40），同时保留所有真实声明（40/40）。多工具融合时，Provenance Gate同样阻止32/32虚假声明。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM辅助逆向工程系统依赖从攻击者控制的二进制文件中提取的字符串、反编译输出和工具报告，这些内容可能被二进制文件伪装成指令或独立证据，导致系统误判。现有系统缺乏对这类混淆攻击的防护机制。

Method: 提出RARE-Bench基准测试（含干净与对抗二进制文件），设计RARE-Guard防御框架，包含Data-Only rendering（数据隔离）、Tool Authorization（工具授权）、Support Gate（支持计数验证）和Provenance Gate（来源溯源分组计数）等控件，并在Ghidra、r2pipe、angr等工具上验证。

Result: 无防护时模型在35/40对抗样本中提出不安全操作；Data-Only rendering下仍有15个不安全提议；Tool Authorization拒绝全部15个并放行40个合法请求。Support Gate验证23/40虚假声明，Provenance Gate验证0/40。多工具融合时Support Gate验证32/32虚假声明，Provenance Gate阻止全部32个。

Conclusion: 二进制派生内容可引导分析但不应对工具拥有权威；多个工具的视图不必然提供独立证据。Provenance Gate通过来源溯源有效防止表示混淆攻击，而确定性渲染器可防止降级声明在最终报告中重现。

Abstract: LLM-assisted reverse-engineering (RE) systems analyze strings, decompiler output, and tool reports derived from ttacker-controlled binaries. A binary can make data look like instructions or records from one origin look like independent evidence. We call such failures Representation-Confusion Attacks in Reverse Engineering (RARE): the pipeline promotes a correctly extracted observation to instruction authority, claim-validating evidence, or trusted analysis state without the authority or support that role requires. RARE-Bench measures these failures with behavior-checked clean and adversarial binaries. After an exploratory 11,520-call study, we test RARE-Guard's authorization and evidence controls on 20 new programs and two models. Without runtime controls, the models propose a planted unsafe action in 35/40 adversarial cases and 0/40 clean cases. When binary-derived content is shown only as data (Data-Only rendering), they still make 15 unsafe proposals. Tool Authorization denies all 15 and authorizes all 40 matched analyst requests. On identical report drafts, Support Gate validates 23/40 false claims by counting records from one origin separately. Provenance Gate groups those records before counting support, validates 0/40 false claims, and retains all 40 supported claims. We then instrument Ghidra, r2pipe, and angr on 16 further programs. In a preselected eight-program subset, no single-tool draft reaches Support Gate's validation threshold for the false claim. In fused drafts across all 16 programs, Support Gate validates 32/32 false claims. Provenance Gate prevents validation of all 32 and retains all 32 supported claims. A deterministic renderer prevents downgraded claims from reappearing in the final report. Binary-derived content may therefore guide analysis without gaining authority over tools, and views from several tools do not necessarily provide independent evidence.

</details>


### [159] [Open-Source Intelligence and Music Information Retrieval for Geographic Attribution of Musical Affect and the Ecological Limits of Population Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12517)
*Mohammadreza Rashidi*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 通过对2393首跨16国民歌的旋律特征分析，发现各国音乐结构差异极其显著（8项特征p<0.001），但音乐情感特征与国民幸福感、个人主义指数等国家指标无显著相关（0/6显著），驳斥了"音乐反映民族性情"的直觉性假设，指出该推论属于生态谬误。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 验证"一个地区的音乐反映了该地区人民的性情"这一普遍直觉的可测量部分，测试音乐情感特征是否与国民幸福感和个人主义等心理社会指标存在关联。

Method: 使用Essen民歌集语料库，从2393首去重民歌中提取8个旋律特征；用调性识别算法取代文件中不可靠的大小调标签；将区域音乐情感指标与World Happiness Report和Hofstede individualism index进行相关性分析。

Result: 各国旋律结构差异高度显著（p<0.001），中国旋律以大跳和高活跃度为特征（唤醒度复合指标+1.24标准差，平均音程间隔2.77半音vs德国2.17）；6项音乐-国家指标相关性均不显著（0/6）。

Conclusion: 音乐情感的地理分布是真实可测量的，但它不能预测人群的幸福或个人主义程度，声称存在这种关系属于生态谬误。

Abstract: A common intuition holds that a region's music mirrors the temperament of its people, so that melancholic melodies mark melancholic populations. We test the measurable half of that intuition and reject the inferential half. Using the Essen Folksong Collection, a corpus of thousands of notated folk melodies, we extract real melodic and affect-related features from 2393 deduplicated melodies spanning 16 countries and 7 geographic regions, with the analysis performed on symbolic scores rather than audio. The mode of each melody is computed with a key-finding algorithm rather than read from the file, because the collection's own documentation warns its major and minor labels are unreliable. Cross-country differences in melodic structure are large and highly significant. All 8 tested features differ across countries at p<0.001, with the leap-related features reaching p<10^-90, and China carries a distinctive wide-leap, high-activity signature (arousal composite +1.24 standard deviations, mean absolute interval 2.77 semitones against Germany's 2.17). We then test the inferential half. We correlate the regional musical-affect measures with two published, validated national indices, the World Happiness Report ladder score and the Hofstede individualism index. None of the 6 correlations is significant (0 of 6). The geography of musical affect is real and measurable, but it does not predict how happy or how individualist a population is, and any claim that it does is an ecological fallacy. We release the full extraction and analysis pipeline, and a fail-closed checker re-derives every number in this paper from the data.

</details>


### [160] [Open-Source Intelligence for Code Provenance and the Security Patterns that Separate Human and Large-Language-Model Implementations of Common Programming Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12524)
*Mohammadreza Rashidi*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文构建了一个可复现的流水线，从9个大语言模型和Stack Overflow收集了31项安全敏感编程任务的528个代码样本，发现：1）可以用93%的准确率区分人类与模型生成的代码；2）模型与人类在安全模式上存在显著差异，且这种差异在Python、JavaScript、Go、Java四种语言中一致存在；3）模型修复漏洞的成功率为77%，但存在"去除不安全模式但未添加正确防御"的部分修复失败模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开发者如今从两个截然不同的来源获取代码：人类在Stack Overflow等平台积累的问答答案，以及大语言模型的输出。这两个来源在代码本身的安全性模式上是否存在差异，以及能否从代码本身追溯其来源，是尚未被系统研究的问题。

Method: 使用完全可复现的流水线，从9个开源权重语言模型和Stack Overflow API收集31项安全敏感编程任务（如OAuth PKCE、JWT验证、密码哈希、SQL访问）的实现，对每个样本使用确定性安全检测器和风格检测器评分。在528个真实样本上训练交叉验证分类器，区分人类与模型来源，以及具体归属到哪个模型。

Result: 人类vs模型来源分类准确率93%（基线78%），7路分类（具体归属哪个模型）准确率48%。安全差异在Python、JavaScript、Go、Java四种语言中一致存在，但来源边界部分依赖于具体语言，不能对称迁移。漏洞修复实验中，77%的修复率但存在部分修复失败模式。

Conclusion: 人类和模型生成的代码存在可检测的差异，模型在安全模式上既有继承人类语料库的部分，也有自身特有的模式。漏洞修复时模型倾向于移除不安全模式但可能遗漏正确防御的添加，提示需要更安全的代码生成策略。

Abstract: Developers now draw code from two very different sources, the accumulated human answers on sites such as Stack Overflow and the output of large language models. We ask two questions about that split. First, can the provenance of a code snippet be recovered from the code itself, and second, do the two sources differ in the security patterns they adopt for the same task. Using only open sources, a public gateway of open-weight language models and the public Stack Overflow API, we build a fully reproducible pipeline that collects real implementations of 31 security-sensitive programming tasks, among them OAuth with PKCE, JWT verification, password hashing, and SQL access, from 9 language models and from human answers, and scores every sample with deterministic security and style detectors. On 528 real samples we train a cross-validated classifier that recovers human versus model provenance with 93 percent accuracy against a 78 percent baseline, and a 7-way classifier that attributes a sample to the specific model that wrote it at 48 percent. We then report where the sources diverge on security, which patterns models adopt more often than the human corpus and which they inherit from it. Running the same tasks in Python, JavaScript, Go, and Java, we find the security divergence holds in every language while the provenance boundary is partly language-specific and does not transfer symmetrically between them. A vulnerability repair case study, in which models are handed insecure code and asked to fix it, finds a 77 percent repair rate across 21 seeds and 12 weakness classes, but a recurring partial-fix failure in which the model removes the insecure pattern without adding the correct defense. The pipeline is data driven, so any new task or language is added as a single specification entry, and a fail-closed checker re-derives every number in this paper from the stored data.

</details>


### [161] [VanillaBench: The Hidden Accuracy Cost of Adversarial Robustness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12545)
*Niklas Bunzel*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出VanillaBench基准，系统量化了对抗训练模型与标准模型之间的准确率差距。对RobustBench中186个鲁棒模型的分析表明，平均干净准确率差距为-7.7到-29.5个百分点，即使最强模型也比同期标准模型低4.0-21.0个百分点，揭示鲁棒性-准确率权衡远比现有文献描述的更显著。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有对抗鲁棒性研究大多孤立地报告鲁棒模型的干净准确率和对抗准确率，很少量化其与对应标准模型之间的准确率差距，导致研究者与实践者无法客观评估鲁棒性带来的准确率代价。

Method: 构建VanillaBench基准，为RobustBench中四个威胁模型下的每个对抗训练模型，计算其与Papers with Code中多个标准参考模型的准确率差异，包括所有条目、无额外数据条目、模型发表年份的最佳标准模型以及架构匹配的基线模型。

Result: 186个鲁棒模型相对于最佳标准模型的平均准确率差距为-7.7到-29.5个百分点；每个威胁模型中最鲁棒的模型也落后同期标准模型4.0-21.0个百分点；架构匹配对比（消除架构差异）的平均差距为-3.5到-17.5个百分点；当使用完全相同的架构时，差距缩小至-4.0到-14.0个百分点。

Conclusion: 鲁棒性-准确率权衡远大于以往论文所呈现的程度，当前出版物缺乏评估该代价所需的标准基线。未来鲁棒性评估应将标准模型作为参考基线，报告准确率差距作为标准组件。

Abstract: Adversarial robustness research has produced hundreds of defended models over the past decade, yet the literature almost universally reports robustness results in isolation: standard (clean) accuracy and adversarial accuracy of the robust model are shown, but the gap to the corresponding vanilla model is rarely quantified. We introduce VanillaBench, a systematic benchmark that makes this gap explicit. For every adversarially-trained model catalogued by RobustBench across four threat models, we compute the accuracy difference against multiple vanilla references from Papers with Code, computed over both all entries and no-extra-data entries, the best vanilla model as of the robust model's publication year, and an architecture-matched baseline. Across all 186 robust models, the mean delta clean relative to the best vanilla model ranges from -7.7 to -29.5 percentage points, and even the single most robust model per track still trails its temporal vanilla counterpart by 4.0-21.0 points. The architecture-matched comparison, which isolates the effect of adversarial training from architectural differences, reveals a mean gap of -3.5 to -17.5 points. Restricting this architecture-matched comparison to models whose vanilla accuracy is known for the exact same architecture, rather than approximated from a related one, narrows the gap to -4.0 to -14.0 points. These results demonstrate that the robustness-accuracy trade-off is substantially larger than what is typically conveyed by individual papers. This information is critical for practitioners and decision-makers. When deploying models in real-world settings, the accuracy cost of robustness directly affects business outcomes, yet current publications do not provide the vanilla baseline needed to assess it. We argue that future robustness evaluations should report vanilla-referenced accuracy gaps as a standard component.

</details>


### [162] [How Agentic Is Agentic Commerce? A Population-Scale Measurement of x402 Adoption and Authenticity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12575)
*Shengchen Ling,Yajin Zhou,Lei Wu,Cong Wang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文对x402协议（号称最广泛部署的AI代理经济支付协议）进行了首次大规模测量，发现其在Base链上的1.367亿笔结算中，21.20%为虚假交易，63.78%为集群内部结算，真正的独立支付仅占很小比例。结算数量并非采用率的指标，而是可制造性的指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 揭露AI代理经济声称的"数亿笔结算"这一指标被严重夸大：由于facilitator赞助Gas费用且链上无法追溯实际付款人，结算数量可以被利益相关方几乎零成本地制造，从而误导对AI代理经济实际采用率的判断。

Method: 对Base链上的x402协议进行全量测量（覆盖280天窗口），通过元交易层解析实际付款人，构建支付图对每一笔结算进行溯源分类，同时辅以Solana链的粗粒度数据作为补充。

Result: Base链上136,708,672笔结算，总额$44,121,383.81；所有维度高度集中（付款方、收款方、金额的Gini系数均>0.98）；21.20%为虚假交易；63.78%为集群内部结算；真正独立支付介于$187,861.35（可证明到达命名服务方）与$20,258,746.09（无法证明被制造）之间。

Conclusion: x402的结算数量衡量的是可制造性（manufacturability），而非真实采用率（adoption）。AI代理经济尚未真正到来，当前呈现的是一个运营商驱动的、星形拓扑、机器定时、Gas补贴的集中化经济结构。

Abstract: AI agents are said to be forming an economy in which they pay, on their own, for the data, APIs, and compute they consume. x402, which settles a stablecoin payment on-chain for each purchase, is the most widely deployed protocol for this, and its hundreds of millions of settlements are read as proof that the economy has arrived. We show the count cannot be read as adoption: it is the one metric an interested party can manufacture almost for free, since the facilitator sponsors the gas and nothing on-chain marks who controls a payment. We give the first population-scale measurement of x402 on Base, supplemented with a coarser Solana census. Identifying settlements from their on-chain event and resolving the true payer through the meta-transaction layer, we sort each by what its trace can prove via a payment graph. Over a 280-day window Base carries 136{,}708{,}672 settlements worth \$44{,}121{,}383.81, concentrated on every axis we measure (payer, recipient, and value Gini all above 0.98), yet 21.20\% are fictitious and 63.78\% internal settlement within a linked cluster. What is genuinely independent is bounded: it lies between the \$187{,}861.35 that demonstrably reaches a nameable service and the \$20{,}258{,}746.09 (45.92\% of value) not provably manufactured. Finally, we resolve the count's manufacturable component, a coherent operator-driven economy, star-shaped, machine-timed, and gas-subsidized. Settlement count measures manufacturability, not adoption.

</details>


### [163] [PVDetector: Detecting Prompt Injection Attacks on Purpose-Specific LLM Agents through Policy-Violation Concept Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12624)
*Junhui Wang,Hangtao Zhang,Zhirun Zheng,Li Zeng,Jiejun Xiao,Xi Luo,Lihua Yin,Saiqin Long*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出PVDetector，一种基于隐藏层激活空间分析的无训练提示注入检测框架。该方法利用LLM在遇到违反策略请求时内在保留的潜在违反策略（PV）概念，通过测量隐藏状态与PV概念的对齐程度来检测提示注入攻击，在多个LLM和数据集上实现了<1%的假阴性率，优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有提示注入检测方法主要依赖输入-输出模式分析，效果有限。作者从隐藏激活空间入手，发现LLM内在具备识别策略违规的能力，并希望利用这一特性构建更有效的检测方法。

Method: PVDetector是一种无训练框架，从策略违反/合规提示的对比对中离线推导出PV概念（潜在违反策略概念），在推理时通过测量隐藏状态与PV概念的语义对齐程度来检测提示注入攻击。

Result: 在多个LLM和数据集上，PVDetector实现了<1%的假阴性率，且辅助开销极小，一致优于现有最先进方法。

Conclusion: 通过分析隐藏激活空间而非输入-输出模式，能更有效地检测针对特定用途LLM的提示注入攻击，验证了LLM内在的策略违规感知能力可用于防御。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly deployed as purpose-specific agents to handle domain-specific tasks such as customer service and code generation. These agents are expected to comply with not only generic safety guardrails but also purpose-specific restrictions tailored to their designated roles. Such additional restrictions enlarge the attack surface, particularly to prompt injection (PI) attacks. To defend against such attacks, existing detection methods primarily rely on analyzing input-output patterns, yet yield limited effectiveness. To address this limitation, we turn to analyzing the hidden activation space and discover that LLMs inherently retain latent policy-violation (PV) concepts when prompted with requests beyond their designated purpose. Particularly, PV concepts capture the semantics of conflicts between user queries and predefined restrictions, implicitly reflecting LLMs' intrinsic awareness of recognizing policy violations. Building on this insight, we propose PVDetector, a training-free framework that detects PI attacks during LLM inference by measuring hidden-state alignment with PV concepts, which are derived offline from the contrastive pairs of policy-violating and policy-compliant prompts. Experiments across multiple LLMs and datasets show that PVDetector achieves <1\% false negative rate with minimal auxiliary overhead, consistently outperforming state-of-the-art methods. Our code is available at https://github.com/Claresigle/PVDetector .

</details>


### [164] [Auditable and Transparent Fully Authenticated Disk Encryption via USB Storage Interposition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12716)
*Enrique Soriano-Salvador,Gorka Guardiola Múzquiz*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出并实现了一个名为CC（Cryptographic Companion）的中间USB设备方案，使用通用单板计算机（如树莓派）在主机和外部硬盘之间提供全认证磁盘加密（FADE），主机无需感知加密过程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统全磁盘加密由操作系统驱动实现，存在对主机系统信任依赖度高、易受恶意磁盘固件攻击等问题。探索一种独立于主机的中间设备方案，提供灵活性、低成本、透明性和更高的安全性。

Method: 采用支持USB On-The-Go的通用单板计算机（如树莓派）运行Linux，作为主机与外部USB硬盘之间的中间设备。该设备对外呈现标准USB大容量存储接口，对内部执行加密/解密和认证操作，将加密后的数据块存储到外部硬盘。

Result: 实现了CC研究原型，实验评估表明该方案对大多数常见使用场景具有足够的效率。

Conclusion: CC方案提供了一种灵活、低成本、透明、使用通用硬件和开源软件的替代FDE方案，可适应新兴密码方案并缓解恶意磁盘固件威胁，实验证明其性能可满足日常使用需求。

Abstract: Full Disk Encryption (FDE) has become increasingly important in the last decades due to the evident confidentiality concerns. In most systems, encryption is provided by an operating system driver, through which the user can transparently access the encrypted disk after supplying the required keys (or the credentials from which those keys are derived). In this work, we explore an alternative approach: the use of an intermediate USB device placed between the host system and the external hard disk, where the encrypted data are stored. Although there are existing devices that follow this inline approach (such as RAID controllers and USB enclosures with built-in encryption), we explore the use of a general-purpose single-board computer running Linux with USB On-The-Go support (e.g. a Raspberry Pi), to provide FADE (Fully Authenticated Disk Encryption). Our system, named CC (Cryptographic Companion), performs the cryptographic operations (encryption/decryption and authentication), providing a standard USB mass-storage interface to the host (which is entirely unaware of the presence of encryption) while relying on the external USB hard disk to store the corresponding encrypted blocks. Our design provides several key advantages: flexibility, low cost, transparency, the use of generic hardware and free and open-source software, adaptability to emerging cryptographic schemes, and mitigations against malicious disk firmware. This paper presents the design and implementation of the CC and an experimental evaluation of our current research prototype, which indicates that it is sufficiently efficient for most common use cases.

</details>


### [165] [Bulkhead: Automated Semantic Detection and Remediation of Container Escape Vulnerabilities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12723)
*Qiyuan Fan,Zhi Li,Junjie Li,XiaoFeng Wang,Bin Yuan,Deqing Zou*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: Bulkhead是一个结合大语言模型与形式化方法的自动化框架，用于检测和修复容器生态系统中因跨边界路径错误解析导致的路径遍历（PaTra）漏洞。它通过多智能体系统识别入口点、恢复调用链、分析漏洞并生成PoC验证，最后通过断言驱动验证确保补丁正确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有容器文件系统隔离因跨边界路径误解析导致PaTra漏洞频发，尤其在AI工作负载共享GPU等资源时更严重。内核级防御侵入性强且未被主线接受，静态规则检测误报率高，人工审计成本高且难规模化。

Method: 提出Bulkhead框架：1) 多智能体系统利用多维度知识模式（高风险功能模式、调用链模式）定位跨边界交互入口并恢复执行路径；2) 检测模块分析调用链识别缺失安全检查、TOCTOU等漏洞并生成PoC；3) 补丁模块基于模型检测模板进行断言驱动验证保证修复正确性。

Result: 自动发现并验证PaTra漏洞，生成PoC exploits，并提供经过形式化验证的补丁修复方案。

Conclusion: Bulkhead通过LLM与形式化方法结合，实现了语义级漏洞发现与自动化修复，克服了传统方法在准确性、可扩展性和安全性方面的局限。

Abstract: Filesystem isolation in container ecosystems is often weakened by cross-boundary path misresolution, causing path traversal (PaTra) vulnerabilities. These vulnerabilities stem from insecure host-container interactions and have become increasingly pervasive as cloud systems mount shared resources, such as GPUs and agent workspaces, into containers to support AI workloads. Existing defenses remain inadequate. Kernel-level protections are intrusive, can destabilize system calls, and have therefore not been accepted into the Linux mainline. Detection methods rely on static rule matching or manual code auditing. Static rules can flag path-related functions but fail to capture the semantics needed to determine whether a host-container interaction exists, causing many false positives. Manual review requires domain expertise, making it costly, inefficient, and difficult to scale.
  To address this threat, we present Bulkhead, an automated framework that integrates large language models (LLMs) with formal methods for semantic vulnerability discovery and remediation. Bulkhead uses a multi-agent system to identify and repair PaTra vulnerabilities through multi-dimensional knowledge patterns generalized from known cases. It first applies high-risk functional patterns to locate entry points for cross-boundary interactions in containerized code, then uses call-chain patterns to recover the corresponding execution paths at suitable depth. The Detection pipeline analyzes these call chains against the application scenarios and threat model, identifying vulnerabilities such as missing security checks and TOCTOU flaws in cross-boundary interactions, and generating proof-of-concept (PoC) exploits for validation. These PoCs then guide patch generation. To ensure remediation correctness, the Patch pipeline performs assertion-driven verification using predefined model-checking templates.

</details>


### [166] [Stability Buys Time: A Re-Keying Game for Encrypted Multi-Agent Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12742)
*Sai Sandeep Damera,John S. Baras*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文研究了在近似同态加密（CKKS）控制回路中，面对高级持续威胁（APT）的安全博弈模型。提出了一个两阶段攻防博弈：被动侦察（利用CKKS噪声泄露）和主动操纵（被残差检测器捕获）。防御者的最优策略是重密钥（re-keying）而非自举（bootstrapping），Stackelberg均衡下重密钥频率由图的拓扑脆弱性决定。系统存在一个安全可行窗口，由FHE精度、控制精度和重密钥节奏三者权衡决定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有近似FHE的安全性分析是静态的，加密控制假设云是诚实但好奇的，持久威胁博弈从未进入密码系统内部。然而在实际控制回路中，CKKS的解密噪声会泄露密钥信息，且循环必须反复解密以执行控制动作，这种泄露不可避免。因此需要建模闭环系统在APT下的真实安全动态。

Method: 将控制回路的安全问题建模为一个两阶段博弈：第一阶段是被动侦察（对应已知的洪泛攻击权衡），第二阶段是主动操纵。防御者的主动防御手段是重密钥（重置累积泄露），而非自举（不重置泄露）。主动阶段是一个检测规避的时序博弈，攻击者在隐蔽性和操纵效果间权衡。通过Stackelberg均衡分析，找出防御者能阻止攻击者的最慢重密钥节奏，该节奏由控制系统的图拓扑脆弱性决定。

Result: 边缘稳定图需要比重连通图更频繁地重密钥。系统存在一个"安全可行窗口"：下限是"可安全化地板"（security floor），上限是"静态足够天花板"（static-suffices ceiling），有效安全点位于该窗口内，此时重密钥是精度效率的代价。

Conclusion: 近似密码系统在反馈回路中的安全性是一个动态博弈，防御者的移动是方案自身的刷新。这一结论不仅适用于加密控制，也适用于任何必须反复解密以执行动作的系统。

Abstract: Encrypted control lets a cloud coordinate a fleet of agents on fully homomorphically encrypted state, keeping their positions and commands private. The approximate scheme for real-valued control, CKKS, returns decryptions that carry the encryption noise, a key-recovery leak; the loop must decrypt to actuate, so the leak is unavoidable. Yet the security of approximate FHE is studied statically, encrypted control assumes an honest-but-curious cloud, and persistent-threat games never reach inside the cryptosystem. We model the loop's security under an advanced persistent threat as a two-phase game, passive reconnaissance then active manipulation, separated by a measured residual detector that sees only the manipulation. The passive phase reduces to the known flooding tradeoff; the active defense is re-keying, not bootstrapping, since only re-keying resets accumulated leakage. The active phase is a detection-evasion timing game: overt manipulation is caught, so the rational adversary stays stealthy, and at its Stackelberg equilibrium the defender re-keys on the laziest cadence that denies it, set by the control-theoretic fragility of the graph topology. The marginally-stable graph must re-key far more often than the well-connected one. A three-way tension among FHE precision, control accuracy, and re-key cadence sets where this game lives, between a securability floor and a static-suffices ceiling. The efficient secure point is that window, where re-keying is the price of precision efficiency. More broadly, security for an approximate cryptosystem in a feedback loop is a dynamic game whose defender's move is the scheme's own refresh, applying beyond control to any system that must repeatedly decrypt to act.

</details>


### [167] [A Scalable Cloud-Orchestrated and Service-Oriented Multi-Domain QKD Network with PQC Integration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12765)
*Konstantinos Krilakis,Antonia Tsili,Aikaterini Mandilara,Dimitris Syvridis*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种融合多厂商QKD、SDN编排和零信任网络访问的跨域量子安全网络架构，通过实验验证了其在异构基础设施中的互操作性和低开销表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有QKD网络因厂商接口专有、可信节点限制和互操作性不足而难以跨异构基础设施和管理域扩展。

Method: 提出一种灵活的多域多站量子安全网络架构，集成厂商无关QKD、SDN编排和云管理信任服务；基于零信任网络访问协议，采用后量子密码（PQC）签名和密钥封装算法的多级认证机制。

Result: 在真实测试床上部署包含3个厂商QKD节点的域和无QKD基础设施的域，实验表明PQC和SDN开销在受限设备上较低，主要瓶颈是QKD密钥检索和厂商特定密钥流限制。

Conclusion: 该框架在保持灵活性和互操作性的同时，将量子安全密钥传输扩展到原生QKD边界之外，兼容现有基础设施。

Abstract: Quantum key distribution (QKD) offers unconditional security but existing QKD networks remain difficult to scale across heterogeneous infrastructures and administrative domains due to vendor-specific interfaces, trusted-node constraints, and limited interoperability. This work presents a flexible multi-domain and multi-site quantum-secure network architecture integrating vendor-agnostic QKD, SDN orchestration, and cloud-managed trust services. Communication is based on Zero Trust Network Access protocols featuring multi-level authentication mechanisms building upon post-quantum cryptography (PQC) signature and key encapsulation algorithms. The system is deployed on a real-world testbed with domains incorporating QKD nodes from 3 vendors, as well as domains without QKD infrastructure elements. Experimental results show that PQC and SDN overhead remain relatively low even on constrained devices, with the main bottleneck being QKD key retrieval and vendor-specific key streaming limitations. The proposed framework extends quantum-safe key transport beyond native QKD boundaries while preserving flexibility, interoperability, and compatibility with existing infrastructures.

</details>


### [168] [Silent Alarm: A J-Space Protocol for Comparing Danger Recognition Across Models and Quantization Levels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12792)
*Roman Prosvirnin,Victor Minchenkov,Alexey Soldatov,Vladimir Bashun*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出JADR协议，通过分析模型内部Jacobian空间（J-space）在生成首个响应token前的表征来评估越狱鲁棒性，无需外部评判模型，并在六个模型及三种量化精度下验证了SafetyAUC指标的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有越狱鲁棒性评估依赖LLM-as-judge的外部评判，对评分流程敏感且仅观测给定攻击集上的表面行为，无法揭示安全机制内部隐藏的脆弱性。

Method: 1) 在模型生成首个响应token前，记录每层J-space的top-k tokens；2) 将tokens映射至六类行为情景轴；3) 基于StrongREJECT（危险样本）与XSTest/OKTest（安全对照）对比计算SafetyAUC；4) 全程基于模型自身激活，无需外部评判模型。

Result: SafetyAUC指标能显著区分强/弱内部安全机制的模型，且能捕捉不同量化精度（BF16/INT8/INT4）下安全效果的实质性差异。

Conclusion: JADR提供了一种无需外部评判、可解释的模型内部安全机制评估方法，支持跨模型及模型变体（量化、微调）的公平比较。

Abstract: Jailbreak-robustness research typically evaluates safety through generated responses using an LLM-as-judge approach. Such evaluations, however, are sensitive to the benchmark's grading procedure and capture only observed behavior on a given set of attacks, without directly revealing the hidden fragility of the underlying safety mechanisms. This work proposes JADR (Jacobian Assessment of Danger Recognition), a protocol that measures a model's internal representation through Jacobian space (J-space, a recently proposed workspace of verbalizable concepts) before the first response token is generated. For every prompt and layer we record the top-k J-space tokens; these are grouped into six behavioral scenario axes and compared between a danger sample based on StrongREJECT and a safe control drawn from XSTest and OKTest. The method does not call on an external judge model: the computation runs entirely locally, on the activations of the model under evaluation, which lets us compare both different models against each other and modifications of a single model -- quantization and fine-tuning in particular -- on the same terms. The final comparison rests on the proposed SafetyAUC metric, complemented with bootstrap confidence intervals. The protocol is applied to six models (Qwen3-1.7B, Qwen3-4B, Qwen3-8B, Qwen3-Uncensored-4B, Qwen3-SafeRL-4B, Gemma 2 9B) across three weight-representation regimes -- BF16, INT8, and INT4 -- and checked against an independent behavioral evaluation with the StrongREJECT grader. The metric separates models with a strong versus a weak internal safety mechanism with statistical significance and captures substantively different effects across quantization regimes.

</details>


### [169] [Watermark Forensics for Generative Models: An Information-Theoretic Perspective](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13003)
*Xiaoyu Li,Zheng Gao,Xiaoyan Feng,Jiaojiao Jiang,Yulei Sui,Jiankun Hu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文研究生成模型水印的"法医学阶梯"——检测、归因、定位、提取——并证明每个阶梯在样本长度n上的理论代价。主要定理给出了多用户归因和载荷提取的精确熵率定律：归因于N个用户之一需要Θ(log N/h)个token，提取ℓ-bit载荷需要Θ(ℓ/h)个token，且这些界是紧的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有水印研究大多仅关注检测（判断文本是否为机器生成），但水印可以承担更丰富的法医学功能：归因到具体用户、提取隐藏载荷、定位经过编辑后仍存留的部分。作者希望系统性地理解这些不同功能在样本长度上的代价，建立统一的理论框架。

Method: 引入信息轮廓ν(t)=I(S;X_t|X_<t)作为核心分析工具，刻画每个token泄漏关于秘密S的信息量。利用精确对齐技术，推导出归因和提取的熵率定律。提出基于解码器对每个候选者使用其自身实现惊异度进行阈值化的方法，替代自然碰撞计数分析以消除无界过收费。

Result: 1) 统计无失真方案下，归因于N个用户之一需要Θ(log N/h)个token，这是首个针对多用户归因的紧熵率定律；2) 提取ℓ-bit载荷需要Θ(ℓ/h)个token；3) 存在Θ(log N)个token的窗口，文本可证明是机器生成但无法归因；4) 存在足迹-分辨率不确定原理；5) 在GPT-2、Pythia-410M、Qwen2.5上的实验恢复了预测常数。

Conclusion: 水印法医学阶梯的不同层级有本质不同的代价结构：检测仅需"存在性"（有标记与无标记分布的距离），而归因和提取需要"信息量"（信息轮廓的总质量），定位则依赖于信息质量的分布方式。两种质量模型（每token弱标记 vs 少数token强标记）是信息轮廓约束的两种不可比方案。两个间隙（可检测但不可归因窗口、足迹-分辨率不确定原理）是真实的而非建模假象。

Abstract: A watermark in a generative model's output is usually asked only whether a text is machine-made. The same mark can do more: attribute it to the user who produced it, extract a hidden payload, or localize the part that survives editing. These form a forensic ladder, and we ask what each rung costs in the sample length $n$.
  One object organizes the answers. Let $S$ be the secret the mark carries (a user's identity or payload), and let the information profile $ν(t)=I(S;X_t\mid X_{<t})$ record how much the $t$-th token reveals about $S$ given the earlier ones. Its total mass pays for attribution and extraction; how that mass is spread pays for localization; and detection alone is paid for not by information but by presence, the distance from the marked to the unmarked distribution. The literature's two quality models, a mark subtle on every token and one that stamps a few tokens loudly, are two incomparable ways of capping this profile.
  Our main theorem settles the ladder's entropy column. For statistically distortion-free schemes, attributing a text to one of $N$ users costs $Θ(\log N/h)$ tokens over every stationary-ergodic source of entropy rate $h$, sharp to a $(1+o(1))$ factor: to our knowledge the first tight entropy-rate law for multi-user attribution (via exact alignment). The natural collision-counting analysis overcharges without bound; only a decoder thresholding each candidate by its own realized surprisal attains the rate while almost never implicating an innocent user. A matching converse makes the law two-sided, and extraction of an $\ell$-bit payload costs $Θ(\ell/h)$. Two gaps are real, not modeling artifacts: a $Θ(\log N)$-token window in which a text is provably machine-made yet unattributable, and a footprint-resolution uncertainty principle. Experiments on GPT-2, Pythia-410M, and Qwen2.5 recover the predicted constants.

</details>


<div id='stat.ML'></div>

# stat.ML [[Back]](#toc)

### [170] [Did We Actually Fix It? An Independent Adversarial Stress-Test of Post-Point-Adjustment Evaluation Metrics for Time-Series Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11969)
*Zongye Lyu*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文对时间序列异常检测中12种评估指标进行了对抗性压力测试，发现修复是局部的：affiliation-F1和所有基于ROC的指标可被无技能检测器轻易攻破（游戏化率62-99%），而基于PR的指标和PA%K具有抵抗力（14-18%）。VUS-ROC和VUS-PR之间存在单向显著差异，且该模式在多个基准上复现。建议优先使用PR类指标或PA%K，将affiliation-F1和ROC-AUC变体视为可攻破的，并需按基准逐一验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Kim等人(2022)已证明Point-adjustment协议存在严重缺陷，社区迁移到替代指标（PA%K、range-based precision/recall、affiliation precision/recall、VUS ROC/PR）。然而，这些"修复"指标是否真正解决了问题？现有的稳健性检查要么由提出者自行进行，要么只是理论性的，缺乏独立、对抗性、基于SOTA的实际基准测试。

Method: 在UCR Anomaly Archive（主基准，250个序列）及其他五个基准（SMD、SMAP、MSL、NAB、PSM）上，对12种指标进行压力测试。使用平凡的和对抗性的无技能评分生成器，并与最佳真实检测器的得分进行比较。采用预注册的SOTA相对可游戏性标准：如果无技能生成器达到最佳真实检测器得分的>=90%，则认为该指标在该序列上被攻破。

Result: affiliation-F1在99%的序列上被攻破，所有基于ROC的指标（约62-64%）也被无技能检测器攻破——并且是"真正地"攻破（真实检测器在它们攻破的序列上得分为0.97-1.00）。基于PR的指标和PA%K具有抵抗力（14-18%）。发现一个决定性的单向McNemar分裂：VUS-ROC在119个序列上被攻破而VUS-PR未被攻破，反向情况为零。ROC-over-PR方向在所有五个额外基准上复制且从未反向，但绝对可游戏化率依赖于基准，没有任何一个指标在所有六个基准上都保持安全。

Conclusion: "修复"只是局部的。建议优先使用基于PR的指标或PA%K，将affiliation-F1和ROC-AUC变体视为可攻破的，并按基准逐一验证而非假设鲁棒性。发布了可pip安装的压力测试工具，包含所有指标实现的冻结提交版本，以及指标选择决策协议。

Abstract: Point-adjustment (PA), long the default scoring protocol in time-series anomaly detection (TSAD), was shown by Kim et al. (2022) to award near-perfect F1 to random scores. The field migrated to replacement metrics: PA%K, range-based precision/recall, affiliation precision/recall, and Volume-Under-the-Surface (VUS) ROC/PR. But did the fix work? Every robustness check to date is proposer-run or theoretical; no independent, adversarial, SOTA-relative audit on real benchmarks exists. We provide one. We stress-test 12 adopted metrics against trivial and adversarial no-skill score generators on the 250-series UCR Anomaly Archive (primary) plus five further benchmarks (SMD, SMAP, MSL, NAB, PSM), under a pre-registered SOTA-relative gameability criterion (a metric is gamed on a series if a no-skill generator reaches >=90% of the best real detector's score). The fix is only partial: affiliation-F1 (gamed on 99% of series) and every ROC-based metric tested (all roughly 62-64%) are gamed by no-skill detectors, genuinely so (real detectors score 0.97-1.00 on the very series they game), while the PR-based metrics and PA%K resist (14-18%). A decisive, one-directional McNemar split: VUS-ROC is gamed on 119 series where its sibling VUS-PR is not, and the reverse on none. The ROC-over-PR direction replicates across all five further benchmarks and never reverses, but the absolute gameability rate is benchmark-dependent and no single metric stays safe on all six (even VUS-PR is gamed on NAB). We release a pip-installable stress-test harness with all metric implementations vendored at frozen commits, plus a metric-selection decision protocol: prefer a PR-based metric or PA%K, treat affiliation-F1 and the ROC-AUC variants as gameable, and verify per benchmark rather than assuming robustness.

</details>


### [171] [Learning the Graphical Nature of Symmetries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12026)
*Rashid Barket,Enrico Grimaldi,Yacoub Hendi,Edward Hirst,Adam Onus,Harmeet Singh*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文构建了包含131,406个Cayley图的数据集，覆盖阶数不超过767（除512外）的所有有限群，记录了代数标签与图结构统计量，用于研究有限群性质在Cayley图可观测量中的反映，并贡献了新的OEIS数列。网络分析发现了若干经验规律并提出了可检验猜想，最后比较了经典模型、MLP与GNN在预测群性质上的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 有限群是刚性代数对象，其Cayley图展现了丰富的网络几何结构，可用于度量、比较和学习群论性质。构建大规模Cayley图数据集旨在提供新的基准，用于研究有限群性质如何通过Cayley图可观测量反映出来。

Method: 构建了包含131,406个Cayley图的数据集，覆盖阶数≤767（除512外）的所有有限群；记录代数标签与群性质，收集图、环、距离和谱统计量；进行网络分析识别经验规律并提出猜想；比较经典模型、MLP与GNN（GIN、GCN等）在预测代数群性质上的表现。

Result: 恢复了已知OEIS序列并贡献了关于单群及由至多三、四、五个元素生成的群的新序列；发现了平方聚类、Cayley图直径、平均图无序度和幂零群谱间隙等经验关系；GNN（尤其是GIN和特定阶数下的GCN）能直接从图结构中恢复显著的结构信号，图感知架构在这些群论图表示上表现出阶段性最优性。

Conclusion: 工程化的图统计量对预测群性质高度有效，而GNN能直接从图结构中捕获结构信号；图感知架构在群论图表示上具有阶段性最优表现，为利用图学习方法研究有限群性质提供了新途径。

Abstract: Finite groups are rigid algebraic objects, whose Cayley graphs expose a rich network geometry through which group-theoretic structure can be measured, compared, and learned. In this paper, a dataset of $131{,}406$ Cayley graphs is constructed, covering all groups of order at most $767$ except order $512$, recording exact algebraic labels for group properties together with a broad collection of graph, cycle, distance, and spectral statistics.
