This census aims to provide novel benchmarks for studying how finite-group properties are reflected in Cayley graph observables. It also yields new enumerative contributions: alongside recovering known OEIS sequences for standard group classes, new sequences for monolithic groups and for groups generated by at most three, four, and five elements are contributed to the OEIS. The accompanying network analysis identifies several empirical regularities and formulates testable conjectures, including relationships involving square clustering, Cayley graph diameter, average graph disorder, and spectral eigengaps of nilpotent groups. Finally, a comparison between classical models, an MLP, and graph neural network architectures is performed for predicting algebraic group properties directly from Cayley graph data. The results show that engineered graph statistics are highly informative, while GNNs, especially GIN and in some fixed-order settings GCN, can recover substantial structural signal directly from the graph. Such that graph-aware architectures show phases of optimality on these group-theoretic graph representations.

</details>


### [172] [Dynamic Online Processor-Native Inference for State Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12095)
*Orestis Kaparounakis*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 提出了一种利用处理器原生不确定性追踪的贝叶斯滤波技术，通过确定性分层重要性重构实现任意程序模型的近似滤波，在匹配结果质量下比直接蒙特卡洛方法平均加速805倍，并在精度-延迟权衡上帕累托主导粒子滤波。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传感器丰富的数据驱动应用中，贝叶斯方法用于从噪声测量和物理模型推断动态系统潜在状态，但似然计算是准确后验和高效推理的关键瓶颈。

Method: 采用处理器原生不确定性追踪进行不确定性传播和推理，通过原生操作实现确定性分层重要性重构，为任意程序代码模型提供确定性延迟和有界内存使用。

Result: 在三个非线性状态空间系统上，该方法在模型评估中匹配结果质量时比直接蒙特卡洛平均加速805倍，在后验推理中实现帕累托主导的精度-延迟权衡，且RMSE与基线粒子滤波保持竞争力。

Conclusion: 该方法实现了确定性近似滤波，具有显著的速度优势和良好的精度-效率平衡。

Abstract: Sensor-rich data-driven applications increasingly use Bayesian approaches to infer latent states of dynamic systems from noisy sensor measurements and physical models. Yet the computation of the likelihood remains an essential bottleneck for accurate posteriors and performant inference. This paper presents a Bayesian filtering technique that uses processor-native uncertainty tracking for both uncertainty propagation and inference. The technique implements deterministic hierarchical importance restructuring through a native operation, giving deterministic latency and bounded memory use for arbitrary models written as program code. Benchmarks across three nonlinear state-space systems compare the approach against particle filters and Monte-Carlo-based likelihood estimators. The technique enables deterministic approximate filtering with as high as 805$\times$ average speedup against direct Monte Carlo work at matched result quality for model evaluation, and Pareto-dominant accuracy-latency trade-offs for posterior inference while remaining competitive in RMSE with baseline particle filters.

</details>


### [173] [Falsifying Causal Graphs With Outlier Events](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12145)
*William Roy Orchard,Philipp M. Faller,Dominik Janzing*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 提出通过异常事件传播来证伪候选因果图的新方法，利用"弱异常 rarely 导致强异常"原则构建统计检验，可在单异常样本下操作并保证假阳性控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实因果关系 rarely 已知，从数据推断因果图困难，需要一种在缺乏 ground truth 时评估候选因果图质量的方法。

Method: 利用"弱异常 rarely 导致强异常"原则，反其道而行之，提出首个统计检验来验证候选图是否为真实因果图，检验其隐含的异常传播是否与数据一致。

Result: 所提统计检验具有假阳性控制能力，对错误因果图有功效保证，且可在单异常样本下运行。

Conclusion: 通过异常传播一致性来证伪候选因果图是一种有效的因果图评估方法，解决了缺乏 ground truth 下的验证难题。

Abstract: True causal relationships are rarely known, and inferring causal graphs from data is hard. A fundamental challenge is how to assess whether a given causal graph is good in the absence of a ground truth. We propose falsifying candidate causal graphs based on whether they can explain the propagation of an outlier event. Our approach leverages a key principle: weak outliers rarely cause strong ones. While this principle has previously been used in root cause analysis to identify root causes without prior knowledge of the graph, we turn it on its head and use it to falsify candidate causal graphs whose implied outlier propagation is inconsistent with the data. To this end, we present the first statistical tests for the hypothesis that a candidate graph is the true causal graph, and show they have false positive control, power guarantees against incorrect causal graphs, and can operate with a single outlier sample.

</details>


### [174] [Thompson Sampling Is 2-Competitive for Mistakes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12389)
*Mark Sellke,Gregory Valiant*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文证明Thompson sampling在贝叶斯Bandit模型中，其期望错误次数（选择次优臂的次数）不超过任何其他策略的2倍，证实了Guha和Munagala在2014年的猜想，且因子2是最优的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量化Thompson sampling在贝叶斯Bandit模型中的最优性保证，证实Guha和Munagala在2014年提出的关于遗憾因子的猜想。

Method: 论文假设隐式臂过程独立且每个臂仅在播放时演化，考虑基于均值奖励定义最优臂的随机Bandit场景，通过理论分析证明Thompson sampling在任意非递增轮次权重序列（包括固定horizon和几何折扣）下，其期望错误次数不超过任何其他策略的2倍。

Result: Thompson sampling的期望错误次数（选择次优臂）不超过任何其他策略的2倍，且该因子2是紧的（best possible）。

Conclusion: 在独立臂过程且仅播放时演化的条件下，Thompson sampling具有2倍最优性保证，确认了此前猜想的最优常数因子。

Abstract: We consider Bayesian bandit models and prove that Thompson sampling makes at most twice the expected number of mistakes (selections of a suboptimal arm) as any other policy. Our analysis applies as long as the latent arm processes are independent and each arm evolves only when played. For stochastic bandits with best arm defined via mean reward, this confirms a conjecture of Guha and Munagala from 2014, where the factor $2$ is already best possible. The result holds under any nonincreasing sequence of round weights, including fixed horizon and geometric discounting.

</details>


### [175] [Contrast-Free ICA and Causal Inference via Wasserstein Distances to the Gaussian](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12832)
*Félix Laplante,Christophe Ambroise,Pierre Humbert*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出基于平方2-Wasserstein距离到标准高斯分布的非高斯性度量，用于线性ICA和LiNGAM因果推断，证明了独立源线性组合的Wasserstein非高斯性严格小于源的加权和，从而精确识别ICA解混矩阵和因果顺序，并提供了三种实用求解器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有ICA和LiNGAM方法通常依赖高阶统计量（如四阶累积量）或互信息，而Wasserstein距离作为基于最优传输的度量，能提供更稳健的非高斯性度量。作者希望建立一种基于Wasserstein距离的统一框架，同时解决ICA和因果结构学习问题。

Method: 理论层面：证明当至多一个源为高斯时，涉及至少两个源的任何单位范数线性组合的平方2-Wasserstein距离严格小于相应源距离的加权和，从而在总体层面精确识别ICA解混矩阵（至多符号置换）；通过最小二乘残差类似地刻画因果顺序。估计层面：定义经验插件估计量，在有限矩假设下给出无分布一致收敛界。算法层面：提出三种求解器——基于Picard迭代的正交优化器（ICA）、穷举动态规划（因果顺序搜索）和贪心变体。

Result: 理论结果：ICA解混矩阵可精确识别（至多符号置换）；因果顺序可通过Wasserstein距离最小化刻画。经验结果：在ICA和因果推断两项任务上展示了竞争性性能。

Conclusion: Wasserstein距离提供了一种有效的非高斯性度量，在ICA和LiNGAM因果推断中表现良好，理论保证充分，且提供了开源实现。

Abstract: We study the squared $2$-Wasserstein distance to the standard Gaussian as a non-Gaussianity criterion and use it for linear Independent Component Analysis (ICA) and causal inference in Linear Non-Gaussian Acyclic Models (LiNGAM). The analysis relies on a strict inequality between the Wasserstein non-Gaussianity of independent standardized sources and that of their linear combinations. When at most one source is Gaussian, any unit-norm linear combination involving at least two sources has strictly smaller squared Wasserstein distance than the corresponding weighted sum of source distances. At the population level, this yields exact identification of the ICA unmixing matrix, up to signed permutation, and gives an analogous characterization of causal orders through least-squares residuals. We then define empirical plug-in estimators and prove distribution-free uniform convergence bounds under finite-moment assumptions, before detailing three practical solvers: a Picard-style orthogonal optimizer for ICA, an exhaustive dynamic program for causal-order search, and a greedy order-search variant. Empirically, we demonstrate competitive performance for both tasks and provide open-source implementations for source separation and causal inference.

</details>


### [176] [ANGLE: Angular Neural Generative Learning via Engression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12833)
*Rajdeep Pathak,Archi Roy,Tanujit Chakraborty*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出ANGLE，一种轻量级深度生成框架，用于圆形数据的非参数分布回归。通过广义圆形能量分数(GCES)损失优化生成映射，学习角度响应的完整条件分布。该框架具有严格适当的损失函数和旋转等变性，支持预加性和后加性噪声模型，可处理外推、充分降维和条件分布相等性检验等挑战。在目标姿态估计和风向预测任务中展示了优越性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统回归方法对圆形数据（角度/方向）的条件均值估计在多模态、偏斜或非对称数据下具有几何误导性，无法捕捉完整条件分布信息。

Method: 提出ANGLE框架，采用轻量级深度生成模型，通过广义圆形能量分数(GCES)损失优化生成映射，学习圆形响应的完整条件分布。建立了损失函数的严格适当地性和估计量的旋转等变性理论性质。

Result: 在目标姿态估计（监视、自动驾驶）和风向预测（能源系统）等实际任务中，展示了优于传统方法的预测性能和鲁棒的不确定性量化能力。

Conclusion: ANGLE为圆形数据的分布回归提供了统一的非参数框架，有效解决了外推、充分降维和条件分布相等性检验等此前未被充分探索的挑战。

Abstract: Circular data, representing angles or directions, are frequently encountered in computer vision, biology, geology, and meteorology. Traditional regression targets the conditional mean, which is often geometrically misleading for circular responses under multimodal, skewed, or asymmetric data structures. To address these limitations, a lightweight deep generative framework, namely ANGLE, is introduced for non-parametric distributional regression on the circle. The full conditional distribution of an angular response, given Euclidean and circular covariates, is learned through a generative map optimized via a generalized circular energy score (GCES) loss. Desirable theoretical properties, including the strict propriety of the loss and the rotational equivariance of the estimators, are established. Furthermore, both pre- and post-additive noise models are accommodated. A unified toolbox is provided for advancing previously underexplored challenges in circular statistics: extrapolation, sufficient dimension reduction, and conditional distribution equality testing. The framework's efficacy is demonstrated through extensive simulations and real-world applications. Specifically, the proposal is utilized for object pose estimation from imagery and wind direction prediction, which are integral to surveillance, autonomous vehicles, and energy systems, respectively. Superior predictive performance and robust uncertainty quantification of the proposed method in these tasks are revealed.

</details>


### [177] [Accelerated Mixing Time of Randomized Hamiltonian Monte Carlo](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12902)
*Siddharth Mitra,Vishwak Srinivasan,Xiuyuan Wang,Andre Wibisono*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文证明了随机哈密顿蒙特卡洛（RHMC）算法在从对数凹概率分布采样时具有加速混合时间保证。当目标分布满足α-Talagrand不等式（如α-强对数凹）时，使用均值为Θ(α^{-1/2})的三角或指数分布随机积分时间，RHMC在KL散度上指数收敛，总积分时间为O(α^{-1/2} log(ε^{-1}))。对于一般对数凹分布，使用均值指数增长的三角分布随机积分时间序列，总积分时间为O(ε^{-1/2})。分析基于哈密顿动力学沿平均KL散度的界，受加速优化方法的启发。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为RHMC算法提供严格的加速混合时间理论保证，填补其在一般对数凹分布上收敛速度的理论空白，并建立与加速优化方法之间的理论联系。

Method: 利用哈密顿动力学沿平均KL散度的界进行分析，采用三角分布或指数分布的随机积分时间，并针对不同条件的对数凹分布设计不同的时间分布策略（固定均值与指数增长均值）。

Result: 对于满足α-Talagrand不等式的分布，RHMC达到ε误差的总积分时间为O(α^{-1/2} log(ε^{-1}))；对于一般对数凹分布，总积分时间为O(ε^{-1/2})。

Conclusion: RHMC在对数凹分布采样中具有加速混合时间保证，其收敛速率与分布条件相适应，且分析方法为理解基于哈密顿动力学的采样算法提供了新视角。

Abstract: We show the Randomized Hamiltonian Monte Carlo (RHMC) algorithm has accelerated mixing time guarantees for sampling from log-concave probability distributions. RHMC proceeds by repeatedly simulating the continuous-time Hamiltonian dynamics for some random integration times, and resetting the velocity to be an independent Gaussian random variable between each simulation. We show that when the target distribution is log-concave and satisfies an $α$-Talagrand inequality (for example, if the target distribution is $α$-strongly log-concave), if we use a random integration time from either the triangular or the exponential distribution with mean $Θ(α^{-1/2})$, then RHMC converges exponentially fast in KL divergence, and the total integration time to reach error $\varepsilon$ in KL divergence scales as $O(α^{-1/2} \log(\varepsilon^{-1}))$. We also show that when the target distribution is log-concave, if we use a sequence of random integration times from the triangular distribution with exponentially increasing means, then the total integration time to reach error $\varepsilon$ in KL divergence scales as $O(\varepsilon^{-1/2})$. Our analysis relies on a bound on the average KL divergence along Hamiltonian dynamics, which is inspired by an analogous result on accelerated optimization methods based on Hamiltonian dynamics.

</details>


### [178] [LatentFlow: A General Framework for Conditioning Stochastic Processes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12922)
*Louis Sharrock,Lachlan Astfalck,Henry Moss*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: LatentFlow 提出了一种无需训练、无神经网络近似的通用框架，通过将随机过程表示为潜在创新的确定性映射，将过程级条件推断转化为潜空间推断，仅通过求解一个反向时间SDE即可实现高效条件采样，在多种模型类上均适用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随机过程模型通常易于模拟但难以进行条件推断，非线性观测、非高斯似然、黑箱信息和全局约束等因素导致条件分布难以处理，需要针对特定模型定制化构造，缺乏通用方法。

Method: 将随机过程写作 tractable 潜在创新 ξ₀ 的确定性映射 f₀ = T_θ(ξ₀)，将过程级条件推断转化为潜空间推断：通过逆映射将似然拉回潜空间，用可导的概率流采样潜空间条件分布，再将样本前向映射回过程空间。近似仅源于有限终端噪声、蒙特卡洛引导和时间离散化。

Result: 该方法在目标分布层面是精确的，条件采样仅需求解一个反向时间SDE，可在单CPU上数秒内完成，覆盖了经典空间先验、非线性随机动力学、物理与生命科学中的机制模型、随机PDE、重尾与极值、点过程与离散状态过程、神经网络或模拟器定义的过程等多种模型类。

Conclusion: LatentFlow 为条件随机过程推断提供了首个统一的、无需训练的、可系统减少近似误差的通用框架，显著扩展了条件采样的适用范围。

Abstract: Stochastic-process models are, as a rule, far easier to simulate than to condition. Non-linear observations, non-Gaussian likelihoods, black-box information, and global constraints all induce intractable conditional laws, requiring bespoke, model-specific constructions. We introduce LatentFlow, a single framework for conditioning stochastic processes, with no learned neural approximations and no training. Our starting point is to write the stochastic process as the deterministic image of a tractable latent innovation, $f_0 = T_{\vartheta}(ξ_0)$, with $ξ_0$ sampled from a simple reference distribution. This reduces process-level conditioning to latent-space inference: pull the likelihood back through $T_{\vartheta}$, sample the resulting latent law with a tractable guided probability flow, and push the samples forward. This construction is provably exact at the level of the target law; in practice, approximation enters only through finite terminal noising, Monte Carlo guidance, and time discretisation of the continuous-time dynamics, each of which is explicit and systematically reducible. As LatentFlow is training-free, conditioning reduces to solving a single reverse-time SDE. This enables conditional sampling in seconds on a single desktop CPU across model classes that have never shared a scalable method: classical spatial priors, nonlinear stochastic dynamics, mechanistic models from the physical and life sciences, stochastic PDEs, heavy-tails and extremes, point and discrete-state processes, and neural or simulator-defined processes.

</details>


### [179] [Ensemble Controlled-Flow Filtering for Implicit Data Assimilation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12975)
*Zhuoyuan Li,Yue Zhao,Ming Li*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出隐式数据同化框架及集成受控流滤波器(EnCF)，通过能量倾斜定义分析律，利用随机受控流和伴随匹配实现观测依赖控制，适用于非高斯、多对一、多模态及隐式观测模型，并证明了理想精确性、局部误差分解及滤波稳定性下的误差不累积特性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有集成滤波器（如卡尔曼类滤波）要求观测机制提供残差结构或似然梯度，但许多实际观测机制（多对一、隐式、非光滑、仅可通过模拟访问）无法满足这些条件，因此需要一种不依赖似然梯度指导的通用数据同化方法。

Method: 提出隐式数据同化：将分析律定义为预测分布的能量倾斜；提出EnCF，通过随机受控流实现该更新，利用伴随匹配从终端能量梯度学习观测依赖控制；对于模拟器定义观测，EnCF-LF从样本学习代理条件能量并应用相同受控流求解器。

Result: 证明了理想精确性，推导了一步误差分解，并建立了滤波稳定性下局部误差不累积的理论保证。数值结果表明：卡尔曼类滤波在光滑加性高斯观测下仍更优，而所提方法在非高斯、多对一、多模态和隐式观测模型上表现更好。

Conclusion: 隐式数据同化框架及EnCF/EnCF-LF有效扩展了数据同化方法的应用范围，能够处理传统方法不适用的观测机制，为复杂观测场景下的状态估计提供了新途径。

Abstract: Data assimilation estimates the state of a dynamical system from model forecasts and incoming observations. Many observation mechanisms, however, are many-to-one, implicit, non-smooth, or accessible only through simulation, and need not provide the residual structures or likelihood guidance required by existing ensemble filters. We introduce implicit data assimilation, in which the analysis law is defined as an energy tilt of the forecast distribution. We then propose the Ensemble Controlled-flow Filter (EnCF), which realizes this update through a stochastic controlled flow and learns the observation-dependent control by adjoint matching from terminal energy gradients. For simulator-defined observations, EnCF-LF learns a surrogate conditional energy from samples and applies the same controlled-flow solver. We prove ideal exactness, derive a one-step error decomposition, and establish non-accumulation of local errors under filter stability. Numerical results show that Kalman-type filters remain preferable for smooth additive-Gaussian observations, while the proposed methods are better suited to non-Gaussian, many-to-one, multimodal, and implicit observation models.

</details>


### [180] [Survival of the fittest Cox model: Pivotal variable selection for time-to-event data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.19374)
*Maxime van Cutsem,Sylvain Sardy*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文通过偏似然的平方根变换，提出了一种新的变量选择方法，该方法结合了BIC等信息准则和lasso等惩罚回归方法的优点，在生存分析的变量选择上优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 改进Cox比例风险模型中的变量选择，解决现有方法中正则化参数选择受未知基线风险函数和删失机制影响的问题。

Method: 对Cox模型的偏似然函数进行平方根变换，使得正则化参数的选择不受基线风险函数和删失机制的干扰；该方法融合了信息准则（如BIC）和惩罚回归（如lasso）的优势。

Result: 在模拟数据和真实数据上，该方法在支持恢复（support recovery）方面显著优于当前主流方法。

Conclusion: 提出的平方根偏似然变换方法能够有效且稳健地进行变量选择，在生存分析中具有实用优势。

Abstract: We revisit Cox's proportional hazards model to improve variable selection in survival analysis. A square-root transformation of the partial likelihood renders the selection of the regularization parameter pivotal, free of the unknown baseline hazard and censoring mechanism. The resulting criterion borrows from information criteria such as BIC and from penalized regression methods such as the lasso, taking the best of both. On simulated and real data, our method substantially improves upon state-of-the-art approaches used daily in support recovery.

</details>


<div id='math.ST'></div>

# math.ST [[Back]](#toc)

### [181] [Causal Graphs, Markov Properties and Do-calculus for Stochastic Differential Equations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12140)
*Philip Boeken,Joris M. Mooij*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 本文建立了随机微分方程(SDE)系统的因果图模型理论，提出了因果SDE系统的可解性条件，证明了σ-分离马尔可夫性和do-演算等核心结果，并引入时间分割系统用于分析子采样时间序列、连续时间Granger因果性和局部独立性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SDE被广泛用于建模连续时间动力系统，但其图形因果模型尚未得到充分理解，缺乏严格的因果语义框架。

Method: 提出因果SDE系统的可解性条件，建立Lipschitz半鞅SDE的一般类；证明σ-分离马尔可夫性和do-演算；对加性噪声SDE证明更强的d-分离性质；引入时间分割系统分析不同时间区间上的因果关。

Result: 建立了因果SDE系统的σ-分离马尔可夫性质和do-演算；对加性噪声SDE证明了d-分离马尔可夫性质（即使系统存在循环）；证明了有向路径缺失意味着因果效应缺失；时间分割系统可推理子采样时间序列、连续时间Granger非因果性和局部独立性。

Conclusion: 该框架使得基于约束的因果发现算法（PC、FCI、CCD、CCI）可直接应用于SDE系统，前提是样本路径间的条件独立性可被一致检验。

Abstract: Stochastic differential equations (SDEs) are widely used to model continuous-time dynamical systems, but graphical causal models for them are not yet well-understood. We consider systems of causal SDEs that are equipped with an explicit causal semantics. We pose solvability conditions for systems of causal SDEs such that they have well-defined observational and interventional distributions - even after marginalisation - and provide a general class of Lipschitz semimartingale SDEs that satisfies these conditions. As core results we establish the $σ$-separation Markov property and the do-calculus in terms of the system's causal graph for probabilistic independence and interventions on the level of sample paths. For a class of additive-noise SDEs we prove a stronger $d$-separation Markov property, even if the system is cyclic. As a corollary of the do-calculus, we obtain an explicit causal interpretation of the graph: that the absence of a directed path implies the absence of a causal effect. We further introduce time-split systems, which consider the causal relations between the processes when evaluated on disjoint intervals or time-points, and use them to reason about subsampled time-series, continuous-time Granger non-causality and local independence. Finally, we discuss how constraint-based causal discovery algorithms (PC, FCI, CCD, CCI) apply directly to SDEs within our framework when conditional independence between sample paths can be consistently tested.

</details>


### [182] [MixCIT: A Kernel Based Local-Polynomial Debiased Test for Conditional Independence on Mixed-Type Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12830)
*Mengxiao Gao,Kyra Gan,Promit Ghosal*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新的图基条件独立性检验方法，通过混合匹配策略（离散变量精确匹配+连续变量k近邻匹配）构建核相似性统计量，针对连续变量引入局部多项式去偏修正，严格推导了渐近零分布，证明了维度无关的n^{-1/4}检测阈值，并开发了接近二次复杂度的高效算法，避免了全局核方法的三次瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 条件独立性检验是因果发现和变量选择的核心工具，但现有非参数方法在处理异质性数据（混合离散/连续变量）时无法同时满足统一性、高效性和统计保证。

Method: 提出图基检验统计量：对离散分量使用精确匹配、连续分量使用k近邻匹配构建复合邻域，比较核相似性；当存在连续变量时，采用局部多项式去偏修正以消除局部平滑偏差；基于解析图校准实现近二次复杂度算法。

Result: 严格建立了跨所有数据类型组合的渐近零分布；证明了维度无关的n^{-1/4}检测阈值，消除了高维几何估计中的相变现象；算法复杂度接近二次，优于全局核方法的三次复杂度。

Conclusion: 该方法为异质性数据下的条件独立性检验提供了首个统一、高效且具有严格统计保证的非参数解决方案，在检测性能和计算效率上均优于现有方法。

Abstract: Conditional independence testing (CIT) is fundamental to modern statistical inference in areas related to causal discovery and variable selection. While marginal independence is relatively well-understood, despite multiple advances, no existing non-parametric CIT provides a unified, efficient, and statistically guaranteed solution across heterogeneous data. We introduce a graph-based test statistic comparing kernel similarities of the response within composite neighborhoods that use exact matching on discrete components and $k_n$-nearest-neighbor matching on continuous ones. The raw statistic, related to prior constructions, suffices under fully discrete conditioning. However, when at least one conditioning variable is continuous, we instead use a local-polynomial debiased variant that cancels the local smoothing bias. We rigorously establish its asymptotic null distribution across all data-type combinations. We further prove a dimension-free $n^{-1/4}$ detection threshold under local alternatives, eliminating the phase transition that affects geometric estimators in high dimensions. Finally, we develop efficient algorithms with near-quadratic complexity and analytic graph-based calibration, bypassing the cubic bottlenecks of global kernel methods.

</details>


### [183] [The Benjamini--Hochberg Procedure Can Fail to Control the FDR for Correlated Two-Sided Gaussian Tests](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12208)
*Edgar Dobriban*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 本文证明Benjamini-Hochberg程序在相关双侧高斯p值下无法在标称水平上控制错误发现率，通过因子模型和区间算术严格证明FDR>0.0104（标称水平α=0.01），推翻了流行二十年的猜想。该证明由GPT-5.6 Pro完成并经作者仔细验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 质疑并推翻一个被广泛相信二十年之久的猜想，即Benjamini-Hochberg程序在相关高斯p值下能有效控制FDR，通过构造反例证明其失败。

Method: 构造因子模型产生相关双侧重尾p值，采用严格的区间算术证书进行形式化验证，并辅以蒙特卡洛实验作为数值支持。

Result: 在标称水平α=0.01下，对于足够大的假设数量，严格证明FDR超过0.0104，超出标称水平。

Conclusion: BH程序在相关高斯p值下无法保证FDR控制，推翻了长期存在的猜想；证明了使用大语言模型辅助严格数学证明的可行性。

Abstract: We show that the Benjamini--Hochberg procedure can fail to control the false discovery rate (FDR) at its nominal level for correlated two-sided Gaussian $p$-values. We construct a factor model for which, at level $α=0.01$, a rigorous interval-arithmetic certificate proves $FDR>0.0104$ for all sufficiently large numbers of hypotheses. This disproves a conjecture widely believed to be true for twenty years. Monte Carlo experiments are consistent with the theoretical result. The proof was obtained by GPT-5.6 Pro and carefully checked by the author.

</details>


### [184] [The exact region determined by Kendall's tau, Spearman's footrule and Blomqvist's beta](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12841)
*Jacob Israel Orenday Lares,Marcus Rockel*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 本文精确刻画了二元copula类上Kendall's tau、Spearman's footrule和Blomqvist's beta三个秩相关系数的联合可行区域，发现该区域完全由已知的(φ,β)和(τ,φ)区域决定，即给定footrule后beta对tau无额外约束，并证明了区域凸性、纤维对称性及体积为31/40。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 确定三个经典秩相关系数（Kendall's tau、Spearman's footrule、Blomqvist's beta）在二元copula类上的精确联合取值区域，填补相关系数联合可行域刻画的理论空白。

Method: 构造性证明：使用M的shuffles的两个单参数族实现(φ,β)区域下边界上tau的极值，通过序数求和将这些族扩展到整个区域，并利用concordance函数的双仿射性质填充垂直纤维。

Result: 联合区域Ω_{τ,φ,β}由约束条件精确刻画：-1≤b≤1, 3/16(1+b)²-1/2≤p≤1-3/8(1-b)², 4p/3-1/3≤t≤2p/3+1/3；该区域凸性成立，固定footrule的截面为矩形，存在关于τ=φ的仿射对称性，体积为31/40。

Conclusion: 三个系数的联合区域完全由已知的两两区域决定，Blomqvist's beta在给定footrule后对Kendall's tau的可行范围不产生额外的尖锐约束，揭示了这三个秩相关系数之间内在的依赖结构。

Abstract: We determine the exact region $Ω_{τ,φ,β}:=\{(τ(C),φ(C),β(C)):C\in\mathcal{C}\}$ of possible joint values of Kendall's tau, Spearman's footrule and Blomqvist's beta over the class $\mathcal{C}$ of all bivariate copulas. The region consists precisely of all triples $(t,p,b)$ satisfying $-1\le b\le 1$, $\frac{3}{16}(1+b)^2-\frac12\le p\le 1-\frac38(1-b)^2$ and $\frac43 p-\frac13\le t\le \frac23 p+\frac13$. In other words, the known exact $(φ,β)$- and $(τ,φ)$-regions already characterize the joint region, so that, once the value of Spearman's footrule is fixed, Blomqvist's beta imposes no additional sharp restriction on the possible values of Kendall's tau. The proof is constructive: two one-parameter families of shuffles of $M$ realize the extreme values of Kendall's tau along the lower boundary of the $(φ,β)$-region, ordinal sums spread these families through the whole region, and the vertical fibres are filled using the biaffinity of the concordance function. We further show that $Ω_{τ,φ,β}$ is convex with rectangular fixed-footrule sections, identify an affine symmetry of its fibres about $τ=φ$, and compute its volume, which equals $\frac{31}{40}$.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [185] [Are we Merging the Right Models? Impact of Expert Training Duration on Model Merging for LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11997)
*Nikita Kozodoi,Zainab Afolabi,Jack Butler*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了多任务模型合并中专家模型训练时长对合并质量的影响，发现简单平均法在过拟合时性能急剧下降，而基于稀疏化的方法在远超验证最优的训练步数后达到最佳性能，并通过偏差-方差分解分析解释了这一现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 挑战多任务模型合并中默认使用最优验证损失检查点的惯例，系统研究专家模型训练时长对合并质量的影响。

Method: 在五个领域（数学、代码、指令跟随、多语言、安全）上微调专家模型，跨三个模型规模（Qwen 3.5 0.8B、2B、4B），保存从最优训练步数的25%到500%的检查点，在每种训练时长下评估五种合并方法。

Result: 简单平均法在过拟合时性能急剧下降，而基于稀疏化的方法在远超验证最优步数后达到最佳性能；存在方法依赖的模式。

Conclusion: 训练时长和合并方法应联合选择而非独立选择；通过偏差-方差分解分析，类比随机森林中平均受益于高方差个体学习器的现象来解释该结果。

Abstract: Multi-task model merging combines separately trained expert models into a single model that handles all tasks without co-training. Standard practice merges experts at their optimal validation loss. We challenge this convention by systematically studying how training duration of domain experts affects the quality of the merged model. We fine-tune experts on five domains (Math, Code, Instruction Following, Multilingual, and Safety) across three model sizes (Qwen 3.5 0.8B, 2B, and 4B), saving checkpoints from 25\% to 500\% of the optimal training steps and evaluating five merging methods at each duration. Our findings reveal a striking method-dependent pattern: simple averaging degrades sharply with overfitting, while sparsification-based methods achieve their best performance well past the validation optimum. We formalize this through bias-variance decomposition analysis, drawing a parallel to random forests where averaging benefits from high-variance individual learners. These results suggest that training duration and merging method should be chosen jointly rather than independently.

</details>


### [186] [Cluster-Weighted EDMD](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12243)
*Lorenzo Tomaz,Judd Rosenblatt,Flavio Kicis,Thomas B. Jones,Diogo Schwerz de Lucena*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Cluster-Weighted EDMD (CW-EDMD)方法，通过联合学习软相空间划分和每个簇的EDMD算子，利用期望最大化(EM)目标基于几何邻近性和预测残差分配转移，使簇专业化于局部Koopman模型准确的区域。在Lorenz、阻尼摆和Duffing系统上，36种配置和10个种子实验中，CW-EDMD相比于同阶EDMD在一步预测和5秒展开预测中表现更优：288组配对比较中有258个显著误差降低、4个增加、26个无差异，中位一步误差降低分别达57倍、2.7倍和12倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准EDMD使用单一全局算子拟合整个动力系统，但当不同状态空间区域呈现不同局部动力学时，全局算子的表达能力不足，无法捕捉系统的局部非线性行为。

Method: 提出CW-EDMD方法，联合学习软相空间划分和每个簇的局部EDMD算子。使用EM目标函数，基于几何邻近性和预测残差共同分配每个转移，使得簇在局部Koopman模型准确的区域形成专业化，而非仅仅在数据密集区域。

Result: 在Lorenz、阻尼摆和Duffing系统上，36种配置和10个随机种子实验中，CW-EDMD明显优于同阶EDMD。288组配对比较中，258个有显著误差降低、4个增加、26个无差异。中位一步误差降低：阻尼摆57倍、Duffing 2.7倍、Lorenz 12倍。

Conclusion: CW-EDMD通过局部化Koopman算子有效解决了全局EDMD在处理多区域不同局部动力学时的低效问题，显著提升了对非线性动力系统的预测精度。

Abstract: Extended Dynamic Mode Decomposition (EDMD) approximates Koopman operators from data, but a single global operator is inefficient when different state-space regions exhibit distinct local dynamics. We introduce Cluster-Weighted EDMD (CW-EDMD), which jointly learns a soft phase-space partition and a per-cluster EDMD operator. Its Expectation-Maximization (EM) objective assigns each transition based on both geometric proximity and prediction residuals, so clusters specialize where local Koopman models are accurate rather than where the data are dense. On Lorenz, damped pendulum, and Duffing systems, across 36 configurations and 10 seeds, CW-EDMD improves matched-degree EDMD in one-step and 5s-rollout prediction. Across 288 paired comparisons, there are significant error reductions in 258 cases, increases in 4, and no differences in 26. Median one-step error reductions are 57x, 2.7x, and 12x on pendulum, Duffing, and Lorenz, respectively.

</details>


### [187] [PolarBM: Complex-valued Boltzmann Machine for Modeling Audio Signals in Polar and Log-polar Coordinates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12417)
*Toru Nakashika,Kohei Yatabe*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出PolarBM和LogPolarBM，一种能在极坐标下自然处理复数变量的玻尔兹曼机，通过显式建模幅度与相位之间的依赖关系，实现了对复数信号（如音频）更精确的建模。实验表明，其受限变体（PolarRBM/LogPolarRBM）在建模精度上优于传统模型和深度神经网络。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统机器学习方法处理复数数据时，通常采用实值框架简化问题，这虽然计算高效，但丢弃了幅度与相位之间内在关系的结构信息。为保留复数信号中幅度-相位耦合的物理重要关系，亟需一种能自然处理复数变量、且能显式建模相位对幅度依赖性的概率模型。

Method: 提出PolarBM，在极坐标系中为复数变量定义概率密度函数，使相位显式依赖于幅度。进一步提出LogPolarBM，对幅度进行对数尺度建模以符合人类听觉感知，推导出灵活的功率加权非中心复高斯（PW-NCCG）条件概率密度函数，其边缘幅度分布包含Rice、Nakagami和非中心卡方分布作为特例。同时引入对应的受限玻尔兹曼机变体PolarRBM和LogPolarRBM以提高实用性。

Result: 实验表明，通过显式建模幅度与相位的依赖关系，所提出的RBM在建模精度上优于包括深度神经网络在内的传统模型。实验聚焦音频信号，但方法具有通用性。

Conclusion: PolarBM系列模型通过极坐标下的幅度-相位显式依赖建模，有效保留了复数数据的结构信息，在音频、无线通信、量子力学等涉及复数数据的科学与工程领域具有广泛的应用潜力。

Abstract: Although vast amounts of data, such as audio signal spectra, are naturally represented using complex numbers, conventional machine learning methods often simplify complex-domain problems by employing frameworks designed for real-valued variables. While this simplification offers computational benefits, it discards structural information regarding the inherent relationship between amplitude and phase. In this paper, we propose a novel Boltzmann machine (BM), named PolarBM, capable of naturally handling complex-valued variables in the polar coordinate (i.e., an amplitude-phase representation). PolarBM defines a probability density function for complex variables in which the phase explicitly depends on the amplitude, thereby capturing the physically important relationships of complex-valued signals. Furthermore, to process audio signals in accordance with human auditory perception, we propose LogPolarBM, which models amplitude on a logarithmic scale. This extension yields a flexible conditional probability density function, a power-weighted noncentral complex Gaussian (PW-NCCG) distribution, whose marginal amplitude distribution encompasses the Rice, Nakagami, and noncentral chi distributions as special cases. For practical applications, we also introduce the restricted variants of these proposed models: PolarRBM and LogPolarRBM. Experimental results demonstrate that by explicitly modeling the dependency between amplitude and phase, the proposed RBMs achieve superior modeling accuracy compared to conventional models, including deep neural networks. Although our experiments focus on audio signals, the utility of the proposed BMs is not limited to audio applications; their potential extends widely across various fields of science and engineering that involve complex-valued data, such as wireless communications and quantum mechanics.

</details>


### [188] [Fisher Rank Inflation: A Spectral Signature of Memorization under Label Noise](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12438)
*Satwik Bathula,Anand A. Joshi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现了一个称为Fisher Rank Inflation的谱特征：在标签噪声训练中，每个样本最后一层梯度的中心化散度的有效秩会在记忆腐蚀标签时短暂膨胀，随后收缩。该特征可用于识别腐蚀样本、量化腐蚀程度，并在多个数据集和模型上得到验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度网络在标签噪声下训练时，通常会先学习干净结构再记忆腐蚀标签。作者希望探究这一转变过程中是否留下可检测的谱特征，从而理解记忆化与结构学习之间的过渡机制。

Method: 计算每个样本在最后一层梯度的中心化散度矩阵，分析其有效秩随训练动态的变化。推导了一阶留一法归因公式，识别腐蚀样本贡献强于干净样本的条件，并通过CIFAR-10/100/10N、SmallCNN/ResNet18/ViT等设置进行实验验证。

Result: 有效秩呈现一致的膨胀-崩塌轨迹：峰值时腐蚀样本在最高贡献样本中占比达69.2%-96.2%（CIFAR-10N上94.4%）；有效秩随腐蚀程度单调增加（从28.88到97.09）；秩膨胀的起始点往往早于测试性能下降。

Conclusion: Fisher Rank Inflation是连接腐蚀样本富集、腐蚀程度以及从结构学习到记忆化转变的可靠谱特征，可用于早期检测腐蚀样本和监测训练动态。

Abstract: Deep networks trained with label noise often learn clean structure before memorizing corrupted labels. We show that this transition leaves a spectral signature in the centered scatter of per-example last-layer gradients. Its effective rank transiently expands during memorization and contracts after corrupted labels are fit. We call this phenomenon Fisher Rank Inflation. Corrupted labels increase effective rank by injecting spectral mass into low-energy or previously unused eigendirections, increasing the entropy of the gradient spectrum. We derive a first-order leave-one-out attribution formula, identify conditions under which corrupted examples contribute more strongly than clean examples, and explain why attribution signals weaken once the normalized Fisher-gradient spectrum stabilizes. We test these predictions on CIFAR-10, CIFAR-100, and CIFAR-10N using SmallCNN, ResNet18, and Vision Transformers. Across settings, Fisher effective rank exhibits a consistent inflation--collapse trajectory aligned with memorization. At peak-rank checkpoints, corrupted examples are enriched among the highest rank-contributing samples, with top-100 noisy fractions from \(69.2\%\) to \(96.2\%\) across five-seed synthetic-corruption experiments and \(94.4\%\pm1.9\%\) on CIFAR-10N. First-order spectral attribution closely matches exact leave-one-out contributions in convolutional models and remains enriched in the Vision Transformer. Peak effective rank increases monotonically with corruption severity, from \(28.88\pm1.95\) under clean training to \(97.09\pm1.78\) at \(60\%\) corruption. In several settings, the retrospectively identified onset of rank inflation precedes observable test degradation. These results establish Fisher Rank Inflation as a spectral signature connecting corrupted-example enrichment, corruption severity, and the transition from structure learning to memorization.

</details>


### [189] [What Does Goodness Measure? A Likelihood-Ratio Account of Forward-Forward Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12501)
*Paolo Giannitrapani*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文从显式生成模型角度重新解释了Forward-Forward算法的核心启发式设计：平方 goodness 是尺度不同的两个零均值总体间似然比检验的充分统计量，FF阈值是该检验的决策边界。推广到各向异性总体得到马氏距离 goodness，重尾总体得到饱和统计量（其斜率为后验精度，即除性归一化）。层间归一化必须去除长度而保留每坐标能量，否则会导致深度坍缩；成对目标存在尺度膨胀捷径，白化 goodness 可消除。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Forward-Forward算法中平方 goodness、层间归一化等设计通常被视为启发式方法，缺乏理论依据。本文旨在从生成模型视角为这些设计提供理论解释。

Method: 将FF算法置于显式生成模型框架下，推导平方 goodness 与似然比检验统计量的等价性；分析各向同性和各向异性总体下的推广形式；研究重尾分布下的饱和统计量及其与除性归一化的联系；分析层间归一化的必要条件和深度坍缩现象。

Result: 平方 goodness 是零均值不同尺度总体似然比检验的充分统计量；各向异性总体推广为马氏距离 goodness；重尾总体产生饱和统计量，其斜率为后验精度（除性归一化）；层间归一化需去除长度保留每坐标能量，否则深度坍缩；白化 goodness 消除尺度膨胀捷径。

Conclusion: FF算法的核心设计并非简单启发式，而是有显式生成模型的理论基础，这为理解和改进FF算法提供了新视角。

Abstract: The Forward-Forward (FF) algorithm trains each layer locally, so that a scalar goodness - the sum of squared activations - is high on real inputs and low on contrastive ones, with activations normalized between layers. Both choices are usually treated as heuristics. Under an explicit generative model they are not: the squared goodness is the sufficient statistic of a likelihood-ratio test between two zero-mean populations differing in scale, and the FF threshold is its boundary. It generalizes: anisotropic populations yield a Mahalanobis goodness, the plain square being its isotropic case; heavy-tailed populations yield a saturating statistic whose slope is a posterior precision - divisive normalization - with bounded evidence and an advantage only under aggregation. The same lens characterizes the inter-layer normalization: it must remove the length while preserving per-coordinate energy, explaining a depth collapse we observe under unit-norm normalization; and the pairwise objective admits a scale-inflation shortcut that a whitened goodness removes.

</details>


### [190] [OmniPMNet: Bridging discrete and gridded PM10 forecasts via omni-query neural processes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11896)
*Shuangshuang He,Shuo Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: OmniPM-Net是一种基于ConvCNP的融合模型，通过地形感知的高斯集合卷积和多尺度空间源注意力机制，将图神经网络站点预测与CAMS网格预报融合到统一空间表示中，在保持站点级精度的同时输出网格化PM10预报，经2024年中国1618个站点验证，MAE为21.14 μg/m³，相比CAMS降低30%，在高浓度尾部和沙尘事件中表现尤为突出。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有PM10预报中，化学传输模型（CTM）能提供网格化预报但存在局部偏差，图神经网络（GNN）在站点预测上精度高但无法输出网格化结果，两种方法各有局限，缺乏既能保持站点精度又能生成连续空间场的统一预报框架。

Method: 采用ConvCNP作为基础架构，设计地形感知高斯集合卷积将不规则GNN站点预测提升到规则网格；提出多尺度空间源注意力（SSA）模块将GNN站点预报与CAMS网格预报融合；使用共享全查询读取（omni-query readout）从统一空间表示中解码出一致的站点或网格PM10预测，预报时长108小时。

Result: 基于2024年中国1618个空气质量监测站全年数据，OmniPM-Net站点级MAE为21.14 μg/m³，优于GNN基线的22.00 μg/m³；将CAMS的MAE降低30%；在高浓度尾部（90分位数MAE）相比GNN降低9%、相比CAMS降低25%；在沙尘事件中分类检测能力提升且能追踪空间演变轨迹。

Conclusion: OmniPM-Net成功弥合了站点级精确预报与网格化空间场预报之间的鸿沟，在保持甚至超越GNN站点精度的同时，提供了CTM级别的网格化输出，尤其在高浓度和沙尘极端场景下优势显著，为多源预报融合提供了有效范式。

Abstract: Forecasting particulate matter (PM10) requires both station-scale accuracy and continuous spatial fields, especially during severe dust storms. Chemical transport models (CTMs) provide gridded forecasts but retain local biases, whereas graph neural networks (GNNs) track monitoring sites well at short lead times but do not produce gridded outputs. Here we present OmniPM-Net, a Convolutional Conditional Neural Process (ConvCNP)-based fusion model that reconciles these two forecast types within a shared spatial representation. A terrain-aware Gaussian set convolution lifts irregular GNN station forecasts onto a regular grid, where a multi-scale Spatial Source Attention (SSA) module blends them with Copernicus Atmosphere Monitoring Service (CAMS) forecasts; a shared omni-query readout then decodes this representation into consistent PM10 predictions at either stations or grid cells over a 108 h horizon. Evaluated across 1,618 air-quality monitoring stations throughout China over the full year of 2024, OmniPM-Net matches the station-level accuracy of the stronger GNN baseline (mean absolute error 21.14 versus 22.00 ug/m3) and reduces the CAMS mean absolute error by 30%, while simultaneously delivering the gridded fields that the discrete GNN cannot. Its clearest gains are in the high-concentration tail, where the 90th-percentile MAE falls by 9% relative to the GNN and 25% relative to CAMS, and during dust episodes, where it improves categorical detection skill while tracking the evolving spatial trajectory.

</details>


### [191] [Semidirect Fourier Delta Attention: Phase-Controlled Delta Memory with Constructive Chunk-WY Kernels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11897)
*Tiantian Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Semidirect Fourier Delta Attention (SFDA)，通过将Kimi Delta Attention中的实对角衰减替换为块旋转傅里叶控制（相位参数），实现了对循环记忆状态的精确调控。核心贡献是构造了分块WY分解，证明了仿射块转移的精确性、形式化稳定性与复杂度边界，并在玩具级状态追踪实验中验证了相位机制对循环记忆学习的必要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 线性注意力用固定循环状态替代softmax注意力不断增长的KV缓存，但这种压缩限制了精确状态追踪和长上下文记忆能力。现有方法（如Kimi Delta Attention）使用实对角衰减，缺乏对周期性/循环记忆模式的显式建模能力。

Method: 提出SFDA，将衰减矩阵Λ_t从实对角推广为复相位对角块（α_t⊙e^{iθ_t}），实现块旋转傅里叶控制。核心理论贡献是构造了分块WY分解，将累积矩阵乘积A_t...A_1分解为Γ_t - Y_t M_t W_t^*的形式，证明秩增长被限制在固定分块内，从而实现精确的仿射块转移。

Result: 数值验证了代数分解的正确性。在玩具级状态追踪实验中，SFDA学会了循环记忆模式（如周期性状态跟踪），而相位被禁用的KDA基线表现接近随机猜测，证明了相位机制的关键作用。

Conclusion: SFDA通过傅里叶相位控制实现了对循环记忆的有效建模，为线性注意力提供了更丰富的状态表示能力。但融合核实现和大规模语言模型对比实验留待未来工作。

Abstract: Linear attention replaces softmax attention's growing KV cache with a fixed recurrent state, but this compression limits exact state tracking and long-context memory. We introduce \emph{Semidirect Fourier Delta Attention} (SFDA), a phase-controlled generalization of Kimi Delta Attention that replaces real diagonal decay with block-rotational Fourier control: \[ S_t=(I-β_t k_tk_t^*)Λ_tS_{t-1}+β_tk_tv_t^*, \qquad Λ_t=\diag(α_t\odot e^{iθ_t}). \] Our main result is a constructive chunk-WY factorization for products \(A_t=Λ_t-u_tr_t^*\), giving \[ A_t\cdots A_1=Γ_t-Y_tM_tW_t^* \] with rank growth bounded inside fixed chunks. This yields an exact affine chunk transfer, formal stability and complexity bounds, and a compact characterization of phase-plus-low-rank memory. We verify the algebra numerically and show in toy state-tracking experiments that SFDA learns cyclic memory where the phase-disabled KDA baseline remains near chance. Fused kernels and large-scale language-model comparisons are left to future work.

</details>


### [192] [Repairing Shape-Prior Shortcuts in Long-Range Single-Shot Fringe Projection Profilometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11928)
*Adam Haroon,Cody Fleming,Beiwen Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对单帧条纹投影轮廓术（FPP）中深度回归网络利用形状先验捷径（从物体边界而非条纹相位恢复深度）导致性能瓶颈的问题，本文提出PhiCalNet，通过输出包裹相位表示并利用固定可微标定层映射到深度，从架构上消除形状先验解。实验表明，PhiCalNet将物体平均绝对误差从14.54 mm降至4.46 mm（提升3.3倍），并首次在FPP中实现逐像素共形不确定性量化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有单帧FPP深度回归网络存在形状先验捷径问题：网络依赖物体边界而非条纹相位信息恢复深度，导致性能瓶颈。增加数据量或模型容量无法解决，因为两者均不改变优化器搜索的假设空间。

Method: 提出PhiCalNet，输出包裹相位表示(sinφ, cosφ)，通过固定可微标定层映射到深度，从架构上消除形状先验解；将条纹阶次作为辅助输入解决单帧映射的非单射性；引入逐像素共形不确定性量化。

Result: 在15600张条纹图像、50个物体的光真实合成基准上，PhiCalNet将物体MAE从14.54 mm降至4.46 mm（3.3倍提升），误差仅集中在±π包裹不连续处（占像素0.103%）；三帧扩展达到1.16 mm；拒绝顶部5%不确定性像素可使RMSE降低64%（基线仅3.5%）。

Conclusion: 通过架构设计而非损失惩罚消除形状先验捷径是更有效的策略；包裹相位表示结合可微标定层可显著提升单帧FPP深度估计精度；共形不确定性量化可准确定位误差源。

Abstract: Single-shot fringe projection profilometry (FPP) networks that regress depth directly can exploit a shape-prior shortcut, recovering depth from object boundaries rather than from fringe phase. On a photorealistic synthetic benchmark (15,600 fringe images, 50 objects at 1.5-2.1 m standoff), the best such UNet baseline plateaus at 14.54 mm object mean absolute error (MAE), and neither more data nor more capacity removes the shortcut, because neither changes the hypothesis space the optimizer searches. We introduce PhiCalNet, which outputs a wrapped-phase representation $(\sinφ, \cosφ)$ and maps it to depth through a fixed differentiable calibration layer, removing the shape-prior solution architecturally rather than by a loss penalty. Because the single-shot mapping is non-injective without fringe order, PhiCalNet takes the fringe order as auxiliary input, an assumption a sensitivity analysis shows tolerates realistic decoding error; a physics-informed (PINN) baseline with the same physics as a soft penalty yields no gain, isolating the architectural choice as the operative factor. PhiCalNet reduces object MAE 3.3x to 4.46 mm, its residual confined to 0.103% of pixels at the $\pmπ$ wrap discontinuity, and a three-frame extension reaches 1.16 mm. Two checks agree: interpretability makes phase the most decodable internal feature, and pixel-wise conformal uncertainty quantification, to our knowledge the first for FPP, localizes error at the same discontinuity, where rejecting the top 5% of pixels by snapshot disagreement cuts root-mean-square error by 64% versus 3.5% for the baseline.

</details>


### [193] [Qubit-Efficient Quantum Search for Hyperdimensional Decomposition via Logarithmic Encoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11936)
*Sanggeon Yun,Hyunwoo Oh,Ryozo Masukawa,Raheeb Hassan,Mohsen Imani*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种量子高效的超维计算分解框架，使用对数复杂度（O(log D)）的量子比特表示替代传统的线性复杂度（O(D)），在保持二次搜索优势的同时大幅降低量子比特需求，实验证明最多可减少2000倍量子比特用量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统HDC分解需要在N^F个候选元组中搜索，计算代价过高；现有量子方法虽提供二次加速，但依赖O(D)量子比特的超向量表示，量子比特效率低，限制了可扩展性。

Method: (1) 提出对数超向量编码和对数绑定编码，将表示成本从O(D)降至O(log D)；(2) 设计可逆超向量查找算子，支持电路级稠密超向量操作；(3) 结合改进的Dürr-Høyer量子搜索算法，保持O(√(N^F))搜索复杂度。

Result: 实验验证了正确的相似度计算和在可执行范围内的准确分解能力，与基于显式D量子比特的基线方法相比，量子比特缩放显著改善，最多减少2000倍量子比特用量。

Conclusion: 该量子高效框架有效解决了HDC分解中量子比特效率低下的瓶颈，在保持量子搜索加速优势的同时实现了显著更优的量子资源效率，为大规模HDC分解的量子实现提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Hyperdimensional Computing (HDC) represents symbols using high-dimensional hypervectors of dimension $D$. In hypervector decomposition, the objective is to recover $F$ constituent hypervectors, each drawn from a codebook of size $N$, from a bound target hypervector. This requires searching over $N^F$ candidate tuples, making the task computationally prohibitive at scale. Recent quantum approach provides a quadratic search advantage, but typically rely on qubit-inefficient $O(D)$-qubit hypervector representations. We propose a qubit-efficient quantum framework for HDC decomposition that reduces the representation cost to $O(\log D)$. The framework introduces logarithmic hypervector and binding encodings, together with a reversible hypervector lookup operator for circuit-level manipulation of dense hypervectors. Combined with a modified Dürr-Høyer search procedure, the method preserves $O(\sqrt{N^F})$ search complexity while substantially reducing qubit usage. Experimental results validate correct similarity computation, accurate decomposition in executable regimes, and significantly improved qubit scaling over baselines based on explicit $D$-qubit hypervector encodings, achieving up to $2{,}000\times$ fewer qubits.

</details>


### [194] [Mirror Horizon: Viable Path Entropy as a Measure of Bounded Reflection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11937)
*Tiantian Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出**可行路径熵（VPE）**，一种在有限预算下衡量智能系统经反复反思后能维持的连贯延续能力指标。在GSM8K推理实验中，VPE揭示模型能力不取决于参数量，而取决于在有限反思协议下可达的已验证延续容量。其中Qwen2.5-1.5B在160 token预算下展现出比Qwen2.5-3B更强的镜像视界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有智能评估偏重单次准确率或pass@k，无法捕捉系统在反复自我反思下的持续连贯能力。作者旨在建立一种能衡量"系统在有限预算内通过反复反思能维持多少条合理延续路径"的理论框架。

Method: 提出VPE指标，包含两部分：到达可行延续的概率 + 成功rollout中验证延续模式的多样性。理论层面建立了直觉（局部欠定约束）、品味（不变性选择压力）、反思（品味引导的欠定消解）、几何（使未来反思稳定的学习结构）四个概念的形式化框架。实验在GSM8K上使用Qwen2.5-Instruct系列模型进行32次rollout采样，比较96和160两种token预算下的表现。

Result: 提高token预算（96→160）显著扩展了验证可达性、降低了零可达率、增加了验证模式熵并提升了平滑VPE。Qwen2.5-1.5B在160 token时获得了最强镜像视界，超越参数更多的Qwen2.5-3B。

Conclusion: 能力不是参数量，而是在有限反思协议下可达的已验证延续容量结构。镜像理论作为度量层面的解释框架成立，VPE能揭示one-shot准确率或pass@k无法反映的深层能力差异。

Abstract: Mirror Theory proposes that an intelligent system should be studied not only by what it represents, but by what coherent continuations it can sustain under repeated reflection. We make this claim operational through \emph{viable path entropy} (VPE), a finite-budget measure of verified continuation capacity. Given a mirror state, a rollout protocol, a verifier, and a mode map, VPE decomposes bounded capability into two parts: the probability of reaching a viable continuation and the diversity of verified continuation modes reached among successful rollouts. This paper restores the full theoretical scaffold behind the measure: intuition as local underdetermining constraint, taste as invariant-selecting pressure, reflection as taste-guided resolution of underdetermination, and geometry as the learned structure that makes future reflection stable. We then instantiate the theory in language-model reasoning experiments on GSM8K. Across Qwen2.5-Instruct models, 32 sampled rollouts per problem, and two reflection horizons, increasing the token budget from 96 to 160 substantially expands verified reachability, reduces zero-reachability, increases verified-mode entropy, and improves smoothed VPE. At 160 tokens, Qwen2.5-1.5B realizes the strongest mirror horizon among the tested models, even though Qwen2.5-3B has more parameters. This shows that mirror horizon is not parameter count, but accessible verified continuation capacity under a bounded reflection protocol. The result supports Mirror Theory as a measure-level account: capability is the structure of viable continuations made reachable, not merely one-shot accuracy or pass@k.

</details>


### [195] [Mathematics of Data Science](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11938)
*Afonso S. Bandeira,Amit Singer,Thomas Strohmer*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本书系统介绍了数据科学的数学基础，涵盖高维数据分析、矩阵分解、回归与正则化、图与聚类、降维、优化、分类、深度学习、压缩感知等核心主题，为数据科学提供了严格的数学框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数据科学作为交叉学科缺乏统一的数学基础教材，现有资料或偏重应用而忽视理论严谨性，或过于抽象难以与实际算法联系。本书旨在填补这一空白，为数据科学从业者和研究者提供坚实的数学理论支撑。

Method: 采用章节式教材结构，从高维空间的几何直觉出发，依次引入奇异值分解（SVD）、主成分分析（PCA）、线性回归、正则化、图论与聚类、扩散映射、随机投影、优化理论、分类方法、深度学习数学原理、图拉普拉斯的大样本极限、测度集中、高斯分析、矩阵集中不等式、压缩感知以及低秩矩阵恢复等主题。

Result: 形成了一套从基础线性代数到前沿低秩恢复的完整数学体系，涵盖了数据科学中核心算法的理论保障与数学推导。

Conclusion: 该书可作为数据科学方向的研究生教材或参考书，强调了数学证明与算法设计之间的联系，有助于读者深入理解数据科学算法的内在机理与适用边界。

Abstract: This book is about the mathematical foundations of data science.
  1. Introduction
  2. Curses, Blessings, and Surprises in High Dimensions
  3. Singular Value Decomposition and Principal Component Analysis
  4. Linear Regression and Regularization
  5. Graphs, Networks, and Clustering
  6. Nonlinear Dimension Reduction and Diffusion Maps
  7. Linear Dimension Reduction via Random Projections
  8. Optimization for Data Science
  9. Classification
  10. A Mathematical Introduction  to Deep Learning
  11. Large Sample Limit of Graph Laplacians
  12. Community
  13. Concentration of Measure and Gaussian Analysis
  14. Matrix Concentration Inequalities
  15. Compressive Sensing and Sparsity
  16. Low-Rank Matrix Recovery

</details>


### [196] [CARE-LoRA: Compressed Activation REconstruction for Memory-Efficient LoRA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11940)
*Gengyu Zhang,Haiyin Ran,Zhengbao He,Yuhang Liu,Hanling Tian,Zhehao Huang,Xiaolin Huang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CARE-LoRA提出一种数据感知的压缩激活重建框架，通过利用LoRA固有的投影结构，在前向传播中使用低秩压缩激活替代完整输入激活，并计算轻量级重建矩阵用于反向传播梯度重建，从而在显著降低内存占用的同时保持LoRA矩阵完全可训练，性能与标准LoRA持平或更优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模预训练模型在有限内存下微调困难，LoRA虽大幅减少可训练参数，但反向传播所需保留的激活值仍构成主要内存瓶颈。

Method: 利用LoRA分支自然产生的低秩压缩激活替代完整输入激活，在前向传播中计算轻量级重建矩阵，反向传播时用该矩阵重建梯度信号，使LoRA矩阵保持完全可训练状态。

Result: 在多种模型和下游任务上，CARE-LoRA在显著降低整体内存占用的情况下，取得了与标准LoRA及代表性LoRA变体竞争甚至更优的性能。

Conclusion: CARE-LoRA通过激活压缩与重建机制有效缓解了LoRA微调中激活值内存瓶颈问题，是一种内存高效且性能不损的PEFT方法。

Abstract: As the scale of large pre-trained models continues to grow, fine-tuning them under limited memory budgets has become increasingly challenging. Low-Rank Adaptation (LoRA), currently one of the most widely adopted parameter-efficient fine-tuning (PEFT) methods, mitigates this challenge by optimizing only low-rank adaptation matrices, thereby greatly reducing the number of trainable parameters. With the parameter overhead substantially reduced, the activations retained for backpropagation have emerged as the primary remaining memory bottleneck during LoRA fine-tuning. To address this, we propose CARE-LoRA, a data-aware Compressed Activation REconstruction framework. By exploiting the inherent projection structure of LoRA, CARE-LoRA replaces the full input activation with the low-rank compressed activation naturally produced by the LoRA branch. It further computes a lightweight reconstruction matrix during the forward pass with negligible additional computation cost, which is used during backpropagation to reconstruct the gradient signal, thereby keeping LoRA matrices fully trainable. Extensive experiments across diverse models and downstream tasks demonstrate that, while substantially reducing the overall memory footprint, CARE-LoRA achieves competitive or even superior performance compared with standard LoRA and representative LoRA variants. Our code is publicly available at https://github.com/fishandyu/CARE-LoRA .

</details>


### [197] [How Query Visibility Changes KV-Cache Compression Rankings: A Matched-Budget Audit](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11942)
*Daming Luo,Christy Liang,Junyu Xuan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: KV-cache压缩方法在查询感知和查询无关两种协议下表现显著不同。在更现实的查询无关场景中，多数方法（如SnapKV）不如简单基线（保留开头+最近窗口），只有KeyDiff始终优于基线。方法间的性能差异与其评分信号对查询的可见性高度相关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前KV-cache压缩方法的评估几乎都采用查询感知协议（即压缩前问题已附加在上下文中），但实际部署中更经济的场景是文档一次性压缩后回答多个未来问题（查询无关协议）。现有评估范式与真实用例不匹配，因此需要重新审计。

Method: 在固定模型（7-9B三个开源模型）、压缩比、实例和解码条件下，对六种已发表的压缩方法与三种简单基线进行匹配预算的审计。使用查询感知和查询无关两种协议，在RULER-8192（144,300对评估）和LongBench（40,800对）上测试，并通过50,000次重采样配对bootstrap进行统计检验。

Result: (1) 查询可见性改变方法排名：查询无关协议下，在共享注意力的五种方法中，仅KeyDiff始终优于最佳-of-3基线（36个单元中31个胜出）；SnapKV平均比"保留开头+最近窗口"基线低0.066。(2) 两种协议间的性能下降与评分信号中查询的可见性一致：SnapKV下降最大（Delta=+0.198，其64令牌观察窗口包含问题），KeyDiff下降最小（Delta=+0.011，其评分不含查询项）。

Conclusion: KV-cache压缩的评估需要转向查询无关协议以匹配实际部署。现有方法的排名在两种协议下会翻转，且方法的设计（查询是否参与评分）直接影响其鲁棒性。这提示未来方法设计应减少对查询信号的依赖。

Abstract: KV-cache compression methods are predominantly evaluated with the query appended to the context before compression -- a query-aware protocol. Yet the economic case for a compressed KV cache is reuse: compress a document once, answer many future questions against it. In that deployment, compression must happen query-agnostic -- before any question is seen. We present a matched-budget audit of six published compression methods against three trivial baselines on three open 7-9B models (144,300 paired evaluations on RULER-8192; 40,800 on LongBench; 50,000-resample paired bootstrap throughout). Everything is held fixed -- model, compression ratio, instances, decoding -- except the scoring rule. Three findings. (1) Query visibility changes the rankings: under the agnostic protocol, of the five audited methods that share a common attention backend, only KeyDiff beats a best-of-3 trivial baseline consistently (31 of 36 cells), and the most widely deployed method, SnapKV, loses to "keep the start and the recent window" on average (-0.066). (2) The per-method drop between the two protocols is ordered consistently with how visible the question is to each method's scoring signal, legible in its source code: from Delta=+0.198 for SnapKV (the question sits inside its 64-token observation window) down to Delta=+0.011 for KeyDiff (its score contains no query term at all).

</details>


### [198] [BattVAE-GP: Generative Modeling of Long-Horizon Battery Degradation with Uncertainty Quantification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11943)
*Raghvender Raghvender,Mahdi Abid,Ferran Brosa Planella,Charles Delacourt,Arnaud Demortière*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种混合物理-概率学习框架BattVAE-GP，利用VAE将电池退化轨迹编码到二维潜空间，再通过多任务高斯过程进行连续插值和不确定性量化，实现对未见充电倍率下锂离子电池退化轨迹的高效代理建模。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长时域物理仿真计算成本高昂，难以在宽泛工况下密集探索电池退化行为，亟需一种计算高效且能够提供不确定性估计的代理模型。

Method: 1) 用PyBaMM的DFN/P2D电化学模型生成循环分辨退化数据；2) 提取容量对齐的电压及导数特征，用VAE编码到二维潜空间；3) 在潜空间训练稀疏多任务高斯过程，以循环数和C率为输入进行连续插值；4) 解码GP预测的潜状态得到电压-容量演化；5) 通过蒙特卡洛传播潜后验至SOH预测器实现不确定性感知的健康状态估计。

Result: 协议级留出评估下，潜空间GP准确恢复未见C率轨迹，不确定性行为与训练数据支撑一致；未见内插C率下生成的潜轨迹在相邻模拟协议间保持连贯。

Conclusion: BattVAE-GP为长时域退化建模提供了计算高效且不确定性感知的代理框架，为向更丰富工况扩展及仿真-实验融合奠定了基础。

Abstract: Long-horizon physics-based simulations of battery degradation provide mechanistic insight but remain computationally expensive, limiting their use for dense exploration of operating conditions over extended cycle life. Here, we propose a hybrid physics-probabilistic learning framework for surrogate modeling of lithium-ion battery degradation trajectories at unseen charging rates. Cycle-resolved degradation data generated with a DFN/P2D electrochemical model in PyBaMM are first transformed into capacity-aligned voltage and derivative features and encoded using a Variational Autoencoder (VAE). The resulting two-dimensional latent space organizes degradation trajectories according to both cycle progression and charging protocol. A sparse multitask Gaussian process (GP) is then trained in this latent space using cycle number and C-rate as input variables, providing continuous interpolation of latent degradation dynamics together with posterior uncertainty estimates. Under protocol-level holdout evaluation, the latent-space GP accurately recovers unseen C-rate trajectories and exhibits uncertainty behavior consistent with the support of the training data. When queried at unseen interior C-rates, the model generates latent trajectories that remain coherently positioned between neighboring simulated protocols. Decoding the GP-predicted latent states through the frozen VAE decoder yields smooth voltage-capacity evolution, while Monte Carlo propagation of the GP latent posterior through an auxiliary latent to State of Health (SOH) predictor provides uncertainty-aware SOH estimates. The proposed BattVAE-GP framework therefore offers a computationally efficient and uncertainty-aware surrogate for long-horizon degradation modeling, providing a structured basis for extending battery health prediction toward richer operating conditions and future simulation-experiment fusion.

</details>


### [199] [Generalized Distribution-Free Semi-Supervised Learning with Risk Rewrite](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11947)
*Yushi Hirose,Hiroo Irobe,Takafumi Kanamori*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种广义半监督学习框架，通过线性组合成分风险构建无偏风险估计器，将PNU学习推广到多分类，并实现了方差最小化与理论保证，在基准测试中表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有半监督学习方法依赖分布假设，违反时性能下降；PNU学习虽无分布假设但限于二分类且方差最优性不明确。

Method: 提出广义框架，通过线性组合成分风险构建无偏风险估计器；推导最小可达方差；建立方差缩减与泛化性能之间的理论联系。

Result: 提出的估计器在非对称损失场景下方差低于PNU；两种实用SSL方法在二分类和多分类基准上匹配或超越现有方法。

Conclusion: 该广义框架统一并扩展了PNU学习至多分类，方差最优性理论上优于PNU，实用性得到实证验证。

Abstract: Typical semi-supervised learning (SSL) methods rely on distributional assumptions, and their performance degrades when these are violated. While PNU learning, a risk rewriting method, offers a distribution-free alternative, it is restricted to binary classification and its variance optimality remains unclear. In this paper, we propose a generalized framework that constructs unbiased risk estimators using linear combinations of component risks, subsuming PNU learning and extending to multiclass classification. We derive the minimum achievable variance, demonstrating our estimator can attain lower variance than PNU in asymmetric loss scenarios. Furthermore, we establish a generalization bound directly linking this variance reduction to improved learning performance. Based on these theoretical insights, we introduce two practical SSL methods that empirically match or outperform existing approaches on binary and multiclass benchmarks.

</details>


### [200] [Scale-Aware Attention for Scarce Neural Data: An RG-Flow Transformer on Sleep-EDF EEG](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11950)
*Dibakar Sigdel*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文在真实稀缺的EEG数据上，对比了具有重整化群（RG）归纳偏置的RG-Flow Transformer与参数匹配的普通Transformer在睡眠分期任务中的表现。结果表明两者在分类准确率上无显著差异（77.3% vs 77.0%），普通Transformer在数据稀缺时甚至略优；但RG-Flow能够从学习到的异常维度γ恢复出连续的谱指数β（R²=0.416），提供了普通模型无法具备的可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 脑场电位功率谱遵循1/f^β规律，其非周期指数β反映皮层状态和睡眠深度。作者希望探究是否可以通过引入重整化群（RG）归纳偏置的Transformer架构，在真实、稀缺的EEG数据上获得性能优势或更好的可解释性。

Method: 提出RG-Flow Transformer，在标准自注意力之外增加尺度感知流，包含可学习的异常维度γ、块自旋粗粒化和熵门控同步桥。使用PhysioNet Sleep-EDF数据集，在严格无泄露的留一受试者交叉验证下，与参数匹配的普通Transformer及仅层次结构的消融模型进行对比，评估5类AASM睡眠分期准确率，并扫描数据预算以检验归纳偏置交叉点。

Result: 在5个受试者和5个随机种子下，RG-Flow与普通Transformer在5类睡眠分期准确率上无统计显著差异（77.3% vs 77.0%，配对p=0.294）。数据稀缺时未出现预期中的归纳偏置优势，普通Transformer在所有数据预算下甚至略优。但RG-Flow能从学习的γ恢复出样本外的连续谱指数β，R²达到0.416。

Conclusion: RG归纳偏置在稀缺EEG数据上并未带来分类性能提升，但赋予模型重要的可解释性——能够恢复与皮层状态相关的谱指数β，这是普通Transformer无法提供的。

Abstract: Brain field potentials are scale-free: their power spectra follow a $1/f^β$ law whose aperiodic exponent $β$ tracks cortical state, and sleep depth in particular is a shift in $β$. We ask whether a transformer endowed with an explicit renormalization-group (RG) inductive bias -- the RG-Flow Transformer, which couples ordinary self-attention to a scale-aware stream with a learnable anomalous dimension $γ$, block-spin coarse-graining, and an entropy-gated synchronization bridge -- has an advantage over a parameter-matched vanilla transformer on \emph{real, scarce} EEG. Using the PhysioNet Sleep-EDF corpus with a strict leakage-free by-subject hold-out, we (i) benchmark RG-Flow against a param-matched vanilla transformer and a hierarchy-only ablation on 5-class AASM sleep staging, (ii) sweep the per-subject data budget to look for the inductive-bias crossover predicted when data are scarce, and (iii) test whether RG-Flow's learned $γ$ tracks the measured spectral exponent $β$ out-of-sample -- a quantity the vanilla model does not possess. Across $5$ subjects and $5$ seeds under leave-one-subject-out cross-validation, RG-Flow and the vanilla transformer are statistically indistinguishable on 5-class staging (77.3\% vs 77.0\% accuracy; paired $p=0.294$), and the predicted scarce-data crossover does not appear: vanilla is numerically ahead at every data-limited budget. What does separate the models is interpretability -- RG-Flow recovers the continuous spectral exponent out-of-sample ($β$-recovery $R^2 = 0.416$), a capability the vanilla architecture has no analogue for.

</details>


### [201] [Scalable Optimal Transport Algorithm for Network Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11952)
*Elaheh Hassani,Durga Mandarapu,Qi Yu,Hanghang Tong,Ariful Azad*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: FastAlign是一个基于最优传输的、支持稀疏性的网络对齐框架，通过稀疏感知图计算和领域特定内核融合（包括自定义SpMM内核），在不牺牲对齐质量的前提下，在CPU上实现3.89-9.45倍、GPU上实现2.24-32.54倍的端到端加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于最优传输的网络对齐方法虽然准确率高，但依赖稠密矩阵的反复构建与更新，可扩展性差，难以适应大规模网络。

Method: FastAlign保留原始OT公式，将其计算重新解释为一组重复的混合稀疏-稠密操作；结合稀疏感知图计算和领域特定内核融合，并设计了自定义SpMM内核以提高计算效率。

Result: 在保持与现有OT方法相当的对齐质量前提下，端到端运行时间在CPU上减少3.89-9.45倍，在GPU上减少2.24-32.54倍。

Conclusion: FastAlign成功解决了OT网络对齐方法中准确率与可扩展性之间的权衡，为大规模网络对齐提供了高效的稀疏化解决方案。

Abstract: Network alignment identifies node correspondences across different networks and is a fundamental primitive in many data science applications, including social network analysis, fraud detection, and knowledge graph integration. However, state-of-the-art network alignment methods often achieve high accuracy by repeatedly constructing and updating dense matrices, sacrificing scalability in the process. To address this scalability limitation without compromising alignment accuracy, we present FastAlign, a scalable, sparsity-aware framework for optimal transport-based network alignment. Rather than introducing a new alignment model, FastAlign preserves the original OT formulation and reinterprets its computation as a set of recurring mixed sparse-dense operations. FastAlign combines sparsity-aware graph computation with domain-specific kernel fusion, including a custom SpMM kernel. Our results show that FastAlign achieves alignment quality comparable to state-of-the-art OT-based methods while substantially reducing end-to-end runtime up to 3.89x-9.45x on CPU and 2.24x-32.54x on GPU.

</details>


### [202] [When Does Reward Teach State? A Hidden-Automaton Instrument and the Group-Language Boundary](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11953)
*Jim Allchin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种白盒方法，通过将任务建模为隐藏确定性有限自动机（DFA），分离评估强化学习代理的奖励获取能力与潜在状态学习能力，并揭示了高奖励并不等同于任务理解，且潜在状态是否被恢复可提前预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强化学习代理获取高奖励时，是否真正学到了任务的潜在状态，还是仅仅利用了与奖励相关的捷径？由于“真实状态”通常无法定义，这一问题难以回答。本文旨在通过白盒工具使该问题变得可精确回答。

Method: 将任务表达为隐藏确定性有限自动机（DFA），代理观察符号流并在部分控制下间歇选择下一个符号，最终获得稀疏终端奖励。利用已知的自动机结构，获得最优回报（使奖励成为可解释的归一化分数）和每一步的精确潜在状态（无需展示给代理即可探测其表示）。通过三个可控轴（优化器强度、任务结构、观测信息量）分析奖励成功与潜在状态学习之间的耦合关系。

Result: 1. 弱策略RL下代理虽能获得奖励但状态探测为随机水平，但PPO+GAE可部分恢复状态（高方差）。2. 置换（群语言）结构可作为预警信号，在153个容量控制的自动机上以0.86的精度（89/103）单向标记感知差距。3. 无标签辅助任务在观测无状态信息时无效，恢复程度与观测揭示状态信息的比例成正比。区分了感知差距（状态不可线性恢复但可表示）与规划差距（状态可恢复但未使用）。

Conclusion: 高奖励并非任务理解的证据；代理是否恢复潜在状态是可以在训练前预测的。奖励唯一评估无法区分的感知差距与规划差距可通过本文方法明确区分。

Abstract: Does a reinforcement-learning agent that earns high reward represent its task's latent state, or only a reward-correlated shortcut? The question is usually unanswerable: the "true state" is undefined. We make it exactly answerable with a white-box instrument: express the task as a hidden deterministic finite automaton (DFA), let the agent observe a symbol stream and intermittently choose the next symbol under partial control, and grant one sparse terminal reward for acceptance. Knowing the automaton gives two things for free: the optimal return (so reward becomes an interpretable normalized score) and the exact latent state at every step (so we can probe the agent's representation without ever showing it). Reward success and latent-state learning thus become separately measured quantities whose coupling is governed by three controllable axes. Optimizer strength: under weak on-policy RL the agent earns reward with the state probe at chance for every architecture, tempting the conclusion that sparse RL cannot install latent state; a pre-registered control overturns it -- PPO+GAE recovers the state, but only partially and with high seed variance. Task structure: permutation (group-language) structure is a warning sign computable from the transition function before any training, and held out on 153 capacity-controlled fresh automata it flags perception gaps at precision 0.86 (89 of 103), in one direction only. Observation informativeness: a label-free auxiliary is vacuous when observations carry no state and recovers it in proportion to how much they reveal. The payoff is a distinction reward-only evaluation cannot make: a perception gap (latent state not linearly recoverable, though representable) versus a planning gap (state recoverable but unused). High reward is thus not evidence of task understanding; whether an agent recovers latent state is predictable in advance.

</details>


### [203] [Graph-Constrained Policy Learning for Extreme Clinical Code Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11954)
*Amritpal Singh,Sebastian Torres,Khawar Shakeel,Syed Ahmad Chan Bukhari*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种图约束遍历策略，将ICD代码预测建模为有限时域决策过程，通过语言模型逐层遍历剪枝后的代码层次结构，将极端多标签分类转化为稀疏、层次感知的子集决策。在MIMIC-IV上，监督策略SFT-1+在完整15,761代码空间上达到0.527 micro-F1，优于CAML、LAAT、PLM-ICD等平坦基线，且单个策略可匹配三级联专家系统的性能，同时避免上下文窗口溢出问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有ICD代码预测系统将任务视为平坦多标签分类，独立评分每个代码，对罕见标签的训练信号有限，未能利用代码层次结构中的约束关系。

Method: 提出图约束遍历策略：将ICD预测建模为有限时域决策过程，语言模型在剪枝后的代码层次图中逐层选择有效子节点，直到到达可计费叶节点，将极端多标签预测转化为稀疏、层次感知的子集决策，保证输出结构有效。

Result: SFT-1+在50代码子集上micro-F1为0.709，在完整15,761代码空间上micro-F1为0.527；全设置下相比最佳平坦基线提升0.044 micro-F1和0.157 macro-F1；单个策略匹配三级联专家系统，避免了28-32%测试笔记上的上下文溢出。

Conclusion: 简单的图约束策略学习可超越更复杂的平坦、级联和强化学习替代方案，用于极端临床代码预测；增加监督轨迹数据是唯一持续提升性能的干预措施，GRPO强化学习相比监督续训无额外收益。

Abstract: Clinical code prediction maps unstructured discharge summaries to ICD-10-CM leaf codes in a large, sparse, and deeply hierarchical label space. Most systems treat the task as flat multi-label classification, scoring codes independently and providing limited training signal for rare labels. We propose a graph-constrained traversal policy that formulates ICD prediction as a finite-horizon decision process over a pruned code hierarchy. A single language model descends the graph level by level, selecting valid child nodes until billable leaf codes are reached. This converts extreme multi-label prediction into sparse, hierarchy-aware subset decisions while guaranteeing structurally valid outputs.
  On MIMIC-IV discharge summaries, our best supervised policy, SFT-1+, achieves 0.709 micro-F1 on a curated 50-code subset and 0.527 micro-F1 on the full 15,761-code space, outperforming flat baselines including CAML, LAAT, and PLM-ICD. In the full setting, SFT-1+ improves over the strongest flat baseline by 0.044 micro-F1 and 0.157 macro-F1, suggesting that graph-constrained decomposition mitigates the rare-code bottleneck. A controlled factorial study evaluates architecture, training algorithm, and data budget. Across both scales, one shared policy matches a three-specialist cascade while avoiding its context-window overflow on 28-32% of full-space test notes. Increasing supervised trajectory data is the only intervention that consistently improves performance, while GRPO reinforcement learning provides no benefit over supervised continuation with matched data. These results show that simple graph-constrained policy learning can outperform more complex flat, cascaded, and reinforcement-learning alternatives for extreme clinical code prediction.

</details>


### [204] [Exact and Certified Data Shapley for Weighted k-Nearest-Neighbor Regression and Soft-Label Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11956)
*Zongye Lyu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了加权KNN回归Data Shapley的首个伪多项式时间精确算法（基于联合整数状态计数动态规划），以及连续权重与目标的认证FPTAS，解决了该问题长期存在的计算难题。实验验证了算法零偏差，并揭示了精确值在确定性、认证误差界及审计估计器方面的价值，而蒙特卡洛方法即使在大预算下也无法重现精确的top-10%排序。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 加权KNN回归与软标签预测的Data Shapley精确计算一直存在空白：唯一精确方法是O(N^K)暴力枚举，指数于邻域大小K。根本障碍在于加权回归预测是两个依赖于联盟的比值，其归一化分母破坏了先前多项式算法依赖的可加性、阈值和重复结构。本文旨在填补这一理论空白。

Method: (i) 提出首个伪多项式时间精确算法：针对离散权重与目标，设计基于联合整数状态(sum of w, sum of w*y)的计数动态规划，复杂度在固定格点精度下关于N和K为多项式；(ii) 针对连续权重与目标，提出认证FPTAS，提供可机器检验的逐值误差证书；(iii) 证明了无条件Omega(D_w)输出规模下界及访问模型硬度结果；(iv) 扩展至加权软标签多类场景。

Result: 精确算法在12,716个对抗性实例上与穷举枚举零偏差验证；FPTAS在86,400次检验中从未违反误差证书。在下游误标签检测任务中，精确值与蒙特卡洛Data Shapley在数据集层面统计等价（TOST, n=8, p<10^-4），但蒙特卡洛在任何预算（最高3,000次排列，约1.28e6次效用评估）下均无法复现精确的top-10%排序。

Conclusion: 精确值的核心价值不在于统计上优于蒙特卡洛（二者数据集层面等价），而在于确定性、可认证的误差界，以及作为审计估计器的精确参照标准。本文填补了加权KNN回归Data Shapley精确计算的理论空白，并提供了首个开源实现与基准真值。

Abstract: Data Shapley is the standard principled answer to which training points are worth what, and its k-nearest-neighbor (KNN) specialization is the version deployed in practice: the exact estimator shipped by toolkits such as pyDVL and OpenDataVal. Exact algorithms are known for unweighted KNN and for weighted KNN classification, but weighted KNN regression and soft-label prediction have resisted: the only exact method is an O(N^K) brute force, exponential in neighborhood size K. The obstruction: the weighted regression prediction is a ratio of two coalition-dependent sums, whose normalization denominator breaks the additive, threshold, and duplication structures the prior polynomial algorithms rely on. We close this gap. We give (i) the first pseudo-polynomial-time exact algorithm (polynomial in N and K at fixed lattice precision) for weighted KNN-regression Data Shapley, a counting dynamic program over the joint integer state (sum of w, sum of w*y), verified against exhaustive enumeration with zero mismatch on 12,716 adversarial instances; (ii) a certified FPTAS for continuous weights and targets, with a machine-checkable per-value error certificate never violated across 86,400 checks; (iii) a complexity landscape, including an unconditional Omega(D_w) output-size lower bound and access-model hardness results; and (iv) a weighted soft-label multi-class extension. We release an open-source, CPU-only library and the first exact weighted-regression Data Shapley ground truth. On downstream mislabel detection our exact values are statistically equivalent to Monte-Carlo Data Shapley (dataset-level TOST, n=8, p<10^-4), the pre-registered outcome; the value of exactness is instead determinism, a certified error bound, and an exact reference for auditing estimators: Monte-Carlo did not reproduce the exact top-10% ranking at any budget tested, up to 3,000 permutations (~1.28e6 utility evaluations).

</details>


### [205] [Constructed Reality, Contested Priors: Decoupling and the Architecture of Cognitive Relapse Under the Free Energy Principle](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11958)
*MD Ibrahim Hossain Ridoy*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过卷积变分自编码器与循环潜变量预测器构成的代理系统，研究了"本体论反转"现象：预测系统能否将合成环境永久性地采纳为默认假设。实验发现，系统的表征能力与默认行为出现显著解耦——无论保留率如何，表征精度始终接近上限，而默认行为则完全取决于保留率，并在中间阶段出现"认知退行"现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在自由能原理下，预测系统并不直接观察现实，而是维护一个生成世界模型并将其最优假设作为经验。作者提出"本体论反转"概念，探究能否构建足够一致的合成环境，使预测系统的推理机制将其采纳为默认假设，永久取代最初塑造它的环境。由于在神经系统中诱导和监测这种转变在伦理和技术上均不可行，因此通过计算代理来研究底层问题。

Method: 采用卷积变分自编码器（VAE）结合循环潜变量预测器作为代理系统，其证据下界（ELBO）目标在符号上与变分自由能数学等价。网络先在基线视觉域上训练，然后进入混合流阶段，通过扫掠的排练率r控制基线内容在向目标域过渡期间的保留程度。分别追踪表征能力（潜空间能区分的范围）和默认行为（系统不受约束时生成的内容）。

Result: 在90次运行的完整扫掠中，表征能力与默认行为出现显著分歧：表征精度始终保持在0.97至0.998的高水平，不受排练率r影响；而默认行为几乎完全由r决定，覆盖了系统的整个输出范围。在中间r值时，系统的默认输出先向目标域上升，随后在训练保持不变的条件下部分回退至基线域，这一结构性失败被定义为"认知退行"。

Conclusion: 对现实采纳的抵抗并不等同于学习速度问题，而是一种具有自身独特失败模式的结构性属性。本研究仅作为计算存在性证明，不进行进一步推论。

Abstract: Under the free energy principle, a predictive system does not observe reality directly; it maintains a generative model of the world and experiences that model's best current hypothesis. Can a synthetic environment be made consistent enough that a predictive system's own inference machinery adopts it as this default hypothesis, permanently displacing the environment that first shaped it? We call this state ontological inversion. Because inducing and monitoring such a transition in a nervous system is neither ethical nor technically feasible, we study the underlying computational problem through a controlled proxy: a convolutional variational autoencoder paired with a recurrent latent predictor, whose evidence lower bound objective is mathematically identical, up to sign, to variational free energy itself. The network is trained first on a baseline visual domain, then on a mixed stream in which a swept rehearsal ratio r controls how much baseline content persists during transition to a target domain. Representational capacity, what the latent space can discriminate, is tracked separately from default behavior, what the system generates when left unconstrained. Across a full sweep of 90 runs, the two diverge sharply: representational accuracy stays near ceiling, 0.97 to 0.998, regardless of r, while default behavior spans nearly the system's entire range depending on r alone, a decoupling of learning from acceptance. More strikingly, at intermediate r the system's default output rises toward the target domain, then partially reverts toward the baseline while training continues unchanged, a structural failure we term cognitive relapse. Resistance to reality-adoption is not reducible to learning speed; it is a structural property with its own distinct failure modes, established here as a computational existence proof and nothing further.

</details>


### [206] [Evaluating Reliability in Machine Learning Models for Early Chronic Kidney Disease Prediction: A Systematic Review of Data Leakage and Predictor Stability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11963)
*Mashrul Hossain,Nafesa Kibria,Fahim Shahriar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统综述了机器学习在慢性肾病早期检测中的研究，发现大多数报告的性能提升源于数据泄露等方法论问题，而非真正的预测能力。通过引入信息泄露分类体系和量化评分框架，揭示了高泄露研究报告的平均准确率（95.48%）显著高于无泄露研究（80.2%），且超过80%的预测指标缺乏可重复性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管机器学习用于慢性肾病早期检测的研究激增，但现有研究存在方法学缺陷（如数据泄露、缺乏时序患者记录、临床指标不一致），导致结果不一致且可能具有误导性，亟需系统评估方法学可靠性。

Method: 通过系统性文献检索筛选出19篇相关研究，引入结构化信息泄露分类体系和量化泄漏评分框架，进行跨研究特征稳定性分析。

Result: 高泄漏研究报告平均准确率95.48%，而无泄漏研究为80.2%，性能虚高约15.28%；超过80%的预测指标缺乏可靠性。

Conclusion: 许多报道的性能提升源于方法学局限而非真实预测能力，强调需要严格的数据管理实践和标准化报告规范。

Abstract: The early detection of Chronic Kidney Disease using machine learning has attracted significant interest in healthcare-related computer science. Despite rapid advancements in this field, many reported studies remain inconsistent and potentially misleading. A significant drawback is the lack of organized evaluation regarding methodological concerns. Key issues include data leakage, limited access to temporal patient records and inconsistency in reported clinical indicators. This research offers a systematic literature review of existing CKD prediction studies using interpretable machine learning techniques, where nineteen relevant studies were selected via systematic searches across major academic databases. To assess methodological reliability, this study introduces a structured taxonomy of information leakage and a quantitative leakage scoring framework to systematically evaluate reliability across CKD prediction studies. The analysis reveals a strong relationship between leakage and inflated performance. Here, High leakage-studies report an average accuracy of 95.48%, compared to 80.2% for leakage-free studies, reflecting an increase of approximately 15.28%. Furthermore, a cross-study feature stability analysis shows that only a small subset of predictors is consistently reproducible, with over 80% lacking reliability. Overall, the findings suggest that many reported performance improvements stem from methodological limitations rather than true predictive capability.

</details>


### [207] [LIDAR-AD: A Decoder-Free Latent-Interaction Dreamer with Action-Residual Chains for Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11964)
*Yongzhi Liu,Yang Xiao,Zhong Cao,Zeng Kang,Sunan Zhang,Zhaozhi Dong,Guojun Yu,Weichao Zhuang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: LIDAR-AD提出了一种无解码器的潜在交互梦境模型（Latent-Interaction Dreamer），通过冗余约简的潜在对齐替代观测重建，并采用残差动作链建模连续控制，在自动驾驶长时域闭环决策中显著优于现有世界模型方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多源观测包含与控制无关的冗余信息，而可靠驾驶决策依赖于风险相关关系、未来动力学和连续动作调整。观测重建和绝对动作建模在学习决策相关的潜在动力学方面是次优的。

Method: 1) 无解码器架构，用冗余约简的潜在对齐替代观测重建；2) 将车辆控制建模为残差动作更新链；3) 残差动作序列对比学习，对齐多步残差驱动的展开与未来潜在状态；4) 潜在-tanh残差参数化保持内部动作可达性并实现平滑的长期控制。

Result: 在多种模拟驾驶场景中持续优于世界模型基线，获得最高奖励和最佳成功率；在nuPlan导出的日志重构建场景中验证了实际交通布局下的可迁移性。

Conclusion: LIDAR-AD通过潜在对齐、残差动作链和对比学习的协同设计，有效提升了风险感知状态抽象、连续控制建模和长时域动力学预测能力。

Abstract: Autonomous driving requires long-horizon closedloop decision making in dynamic traffic environments. Latent world models offer an effective framework for this problem by enabling imagination-based decision making in compact latent spaces. However, multi-source observations contain controlirrelevant redundancy, whereas reliable driving decisions rely on risk-relevant relations, future dynamics, and continuous action adjustments. This mismatch makes observation reconstruction and absolute action modeling suboptimal for learning decisionrelevant latent dynamics. We propose LIDAR-AD, a decoderfree Latent-Interaction Dreamer with Action-Residual Chains for autonomous driving. LIDAR-AD replaces observation reconstruction with redundancy-reduced latent alignment, encouraging compact representations of risk-relevant relations in multi-source driving inputs. It further models vehicle control as residual action updates and uses residual-action sequence contrastive learning to align multi-step residual-driven rollouts with future latent states. A deterministic analysis shows that the latent-tanh residual parameterization preserves interior action reachability while representing smooth long-horizon control as compact local updates. Together, these designs improve risk-aware state abstraction, continuous-control modeling, and long-horizon dynamics prediction. Extensive experiments across diverse simulated driving scenarios demonstrate that LIDAR-AD consistently outperforms world-model baselines, achieving the highest reward and the best success rate among learning-based methods. Evaluations on nuPlan-derived log-reconstructed scenarios further demonstrate the transferability of LIDAR-AD under real-world traffic layouts.

</details>


### [208] [Beyond Coordinate Gauge: An Audited Protocol for Detecting Donor-Specific Functional Fingerprints after Neural Collapse](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11967)
*Truong Xuan Khanh,Phan Thanh Duc*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了神经坍缩（Neural Collapse）条件下，独立训练的网络在仿射对齐后是否仍能检测到功能差异。通过对五组独立训练的MNIST网络进行对齐，发现供体网络的功能指纹在受体网络中仍可区分（精确排列检验p=0.0083），但该结论仅限于可检测性，不涉及可移植性或因果持久性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经坍缩使得独立训练的网络趋近于共享的低维几何结构，这引发了一个核心问题：在收敛到相似几何后，网络间是否存在可检测的功能差异？本研究旨在严格区分并检验这一问题的不同维度（可检测性、可移植性、因果持久性）。

Method: 选用五组独立训练且达到神经坍缩状态的MNIST网络，采用经验证的仿射校正对齐方法将供体网络头部映射到受体网络坐标空间。在受体基线校正后，通过排列检验（exact permutation p=0.0083）评估供体功能指纹的可区分性，并进行了泄漏审计以验证结果的稳健性。

Result: 在20个有序供体-受体配对中，全部被正确识别（精确排列检验p=0.0083），且结果通过了泄漏审计的稳健性检验。这表明在神经坍缩条件下，独立训练网络间的功能差异在仿射对齐后仍然可检测。

Conclusion: 本研究证实了在受控条件下，独立训练的神经网络在神经坍缩后仍保留可检测的功能指纹，但这一结论仅限于可检测性层面，不推广至可移植性或因果持久性。该工作建立了结合对齐、歧义诊断和泄漏控制的分析框架，但结论的泛化性尚待验证。

Abstract: Independently trained neural networks have no shared neuron-index reference frame, so comparing them requires accounting for coordinate freedom.
